new file mode 100644

@@ -0,0 +1,18 @@
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019: Production <see cref="IIntegrationLaneFilter"/>. Selects the Category=Integration cases of one
+/// host by excluding the other host's Platform trait, so untagged integration cases run on both hosts.
+/// </summary>
+sealed class IntegrationLaneFilter : IIntegrationLaneFilter
+{
+    /// <inheritdoc />
+    public string Compose(IntegrationLaneHost host) => host switch
+    {
+        IntegrationLaneHost.Windows => "Category=Integration&Platform!=Linux",
+        IntegrationLaneHost.Linux => "Category=Integration&Platform!=Windows",
+        _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(host), host, "Unknown integration lane host."),
+    };
+
+    /// <summary>TR-MCP-NUKE-019: The host the running build is on.</summary>
+    public static IntegrationLaneHost CurrentHost()
+        => OperatingSystem.IsWindows() ? IntegrationLaneHost.Windows : IntegrationLaneHost.Linux;
+}

+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019: Host operating system whose Category=Integration cases a MigrationIntegrationTests run selects.
+/// </summary>
+enum IntegrationLaneHost
+{
+    /// <summary>A Windows host (PAYTON-LEGION2).</summary>
+    Windows,
+
+    /// <summary>A Linux host (LAB-OMARCHY).</summary>
+    Linux,
+}
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019: Composes the dotnet test filter of the host-scoped integration lane. Cases tagged
+/// Platform=Linux run only on Linux hosts, cases tagged Platform=Windows only on Windows hosts, and untagged
+/// integration cases on both.
+/// </summary>
+interface IIntegrationLaneFilter
+{
+    /// <summary>Returns the test filter that selects the Category=Integration cases of <paramref name="host"/>.</summary>
+    /// <param name="host">Host the lane runs on.</param>
+    string Compose(IntegrationLaneHost host);
+}
diff --git a/build/IntegrationLanePreconditionContracts.cs b/build/IntegrationLanePreconditionContracts.cs

@@ -0,0 +1,19 @@
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019: whether MigrationIntegrationTests may start its test processes, and why not.
+/// </summary>
+/// <param name="CanRun">True when the lane may run its tests.</param>
+/// <param name="FailureMessage">When <paramref name="CanRun"/> is false, the message that names the missing session; otherwise null.</param>
+sealed record IntegrationLaneReadiness(bool CanRun, string? FailureMessage);
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019: decides, before any test runs, whether the host session can run the integration lane.
+/// On Windows the lane runs in an interactive desktop session of the logged-on user; elsewhere no session
+/// requirement applies.
+/// </summary>
+interface IIntegrationLanePrecondition
+{
+    /// <summary>Evaluates the precondition for one host and one session.</summary>
+    /// <param name="host">Host the lane runs on.</param>
+    /// <param name="userInteractive">Whether the running process is in an interactive desktop session.</param>
+    IntegrationLaneReadiness Evaluate(IntegrationLaneHost host, bool userInteractive);
+}
diff --git a/build/IntegrationLaneProjects.cs b/build/IntegrationLaneProjects.cs

@@ -0,0 +1,29 @@
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019 AC-2: Production <see cref="IIntegrationLaneProjects"/>. The six lane projects in their run order;
+/// no other project joins the lane (QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.IntegrationTests, QBrainAi.Acid.IntegrationTests and
+/// QBrainAi.TransactionSecurity.IntegrationTests stay out until PLAN-SESSIONTRIAGEFIX-001 merges, TODO
+/// IMPL-TXNINTEGRATION-001); a solution that lacks a lane project fails before any test runs.
+/// </summary>
+sealed class IntegrationLaneProjects : IIntegrationLaneProjects
+{
+    static readonly string[] LaneProjects =
+    [
+        "QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests",
+        "QBrainAi.McpAgent.Tests",
+        "QBrainAi.Repl.Core.Tests",
+        "QBrainAi.QBAgent.Tests",
+        "Build.Tests",
+        "QBrainAi.Repl.IntegrationTests",
+    ];
+
+    /// <inheritdoc />
+    public IReadOnlyList<string> Select(IReadOnlyList<string> solutionProjectNames)
+    {
+        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(solutionProjectNames);
+        var missing = LaneProjects.Where(project => !solutionProjectNames.Contains(project, StringComparer.Ordinal)).ToArray();
+        if (missing.Length > 0)
+            throw new InvalidOperationException("MigrationIntegrationTests needs lane projects the solution lacks: " + string.Join(", ", missing));
+
+        return [.. LaneProjects];
+    }
+}

+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019 AC-2: Selects the projects MigrationIntegrationTests runs from the solution's project names:
+/// QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests, QBrainAi.McpAgent.Tests, QBrainAi.Repl.Core.Tests, QBrainAi.QBAgent.Tests, Build.Tests
+/// and QBrainAi.Repl.IntegrationTests, in that order. QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.IntegrationTests,
+/// QBrainAi.Acid.IntegrationTests and QBrainAi.TransactionSecurity.IntegrationTests are not lane projects until
+/// PLAN-SESSIONTRIAGEFIX-001 merges (TODO IMPL-TXNINTEGRATION-001); the .Validation projects, the AI review projects and
+/// QBrainAi.PluginIntegration.Tests, which has its own target, are not lane projects.
+/// </summary>
+interface IIntegrationLaneProjects
+{
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Returns the lane's projects, in run order, from <paramref name="solutionProjectNames"/>; throws
+    /// <see cref="InvalidOperationException"/> naming any lane project the solution lacks.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="solutionProjectNames">The names of the solution's projects, in any order.</param>
+    IReadOnlyList<string> Select(IReadOnlyList<string> solutionProjectNames);
+}
diff --git a/build/InteractiveSessionPrecondition.cs b/build/InteractiveSessionPrecondition.cs
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..583e2ec4
--- /dev/null
+++ b/build/InteractiveSessionPrecondition.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,17 @@
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019: production <see cref="IIntegrationLanePrecondition"/>. On Windows the integration lane runs
+/// only from an interactive desktop session of the logged-on user; a WSMan, SSH or service session is refused
+/// before any test runs, with a message that names the missing session. No session requirement applies elsewhere.
+/// </summary>
+sealed class InteractiveSessionPrecondition : IIntegrationLanePrecondition
+{
+    /// <inheritdoc />
+    public IntegrationLaneReadiness Evaluate(IntegrationLaneHost host, bool userInteractive) =>
+        host == IntegrationLaneHost.Windows && !userInteractive
+            ? new IntegrationLaneReadiness(
+                false,
+                "MigrationIntegrationTests needs an interactive desktop session on Windows (TR-MCP-NUKE-019): this process "
+                + "is not interactive (for example a WSMan, SSH or service session). Run the target from a console in the "
+                + "logged-on user's interactive desktop session.")
+            : new IntegrationLaneReadiness(true, null);
+}
diff --git a/build/LinuxProviderTestProvisioner.cs b/build/LinuxProviderTestProvisioner.cs
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..2649ae8c
--- /dev/null
+++ b/build/LinuxProviderTestProvisioner.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,320 @@
+using System.Data.Common;
+using System.Diagnostics;
+using System.Globalization;
+using System.Net.Sockets;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-018: Production Linux provisioning of the provider test endpoints (operator decision 2026-10-11,
+/// BUG-BASELINE-005). Each variable already set is adopted; otherwise it is loaded from the test-database env file and
+/// its dedicated qbrain-test container is started when stopped. Every endpoint must accept TCP before tests run.
+/// Actions and errors name variables, the file, containers and host:port only, never connection-string values.
+/// </summary>
+sealed class LinuxProviderTestProvisioner : IProviderTestProvisioner
+{
+    private static readonly TimeSpan ProbeTimeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(2);
+    private const int ProbeAttempts = 30;
+
+    private static readonly (string Variable, string Container, int DefaultPort, bool IsSqlServer)[] Endpoints =
+    [
+        (ProviderTestNames.PostgresVariable, ProviderTestNames.PostgresContainer, 5432, false),
+        (ProviderTestNames.SqlServerVariable, ProviderTestNames.SqlServerContainer, 1433, true),
+    ];
+
+    /// <inheritdoc />
+    public ProviderTestProvisioningResult Provision(string envFilePath, IProviderTestHost host)
+    {
+        ArgumentException.ThrowIfNullOrWhiteSpace(envFilePath);
+        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(host);
+        var actions = new List<string>();
+        var errors = new List<string>();
+        Dictionary<string, string>? fileValues = null;
+        var fileMissing = false;
+
+        foreach (var (variable, container, defaultPort, isSqlServer) in Endpoints)
+        {
+            var value = host.GetVariable(variable);
+            var loadedFromFile = false;
+            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
+            {
+                actions.Add("adopt " + variable + " (already set)");
+            }
+            else
+            {
+                if (fileValues is null && !fileMissing)
+                {
+                    var text = host.ReadFile(envFilePath);
+                    if (text is null)
+                    {
+                        fileMissing = true;
+                        errors.Add("test-database env file not found: " + envFilePath);
+                    }
+                    else
+                    {
+                        fileValues = ParseEnvFile(text);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                if (fileMissing)
+                {
+                    continue;
+                }
+
+                if (!fileValues!.TryGetValue(variable, out value) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
+                {
+                    errors.Add(variable + " is not defined in " + envFilePath);
+                    continue;
+                }
+
+                host.SetVariable(variable, value);
+                actions.Add("load " + variable + " from " + envFilePath);
+                loadedFromFile = true;
+            }
+
+            var startedNow = false;
+            if (loadedFromFile)
+            {
+                var state = host.GetContainerState(container);
+                if (state == ProviderTestContainerState.Missing)
+                {
+                    errors.Add("container " + container + " not found");
+                    continue;
+                }
+
+                if (state == ProviderTestContainerState.Stopped)
+                {
+                    if (!host.StartContainer(container))
+                    {
+                        errors.Add("container " + container + " could not be started");
+                        continue;
+                    }
+
+                    actions.Add("start " + container);
+                    startedNow = true;
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (!TryReadEndpoint(value, isSqlServer, defaultPort, out var endpointHost, out var port, out var problem))
+            {
+                errors.Add(variable + " " + problem);
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            var endpoint = endpointHost + ":" + port.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
+            var attempts = startedNow ? ProbeAttempts : 1;
+            var reachable = false;
+            for (var attempt = 0; attempt < attempts && !reachable; attempt++)
+            {
+                reachable = host.CanConnect(endpointHost, port, ProbeTimeout);
+                if (!reachable && attempt + 1 < attempts)
+                {
+                    Thread.Sleep(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1));
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (reachable)
+            {
+                actions.Add("endpoint " + endpoint + " reachable");
+            }
+            else
+            {
+                errors.Add("endpoint " + endpoint + " for " + variable + " is not accepting connections");
+            }
+        }
+
+        return new ProviderTestProvisioningResult(actions, errors);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Parses KEY=VALUE lines; skips blanks and comments; strips an export prefix and matching quotes.</summary>
+    internal static Dictionary<string, string> ParseEnvFile(string text)
+    {
+        var values = new Dictionary<string, string>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
+        foreach (var raw in text.Split('\n'))
+        {
+            var line = raw.Trim();
+            if (line.Length == 0 || line.StartsWith('#'))
+            {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            if (line.StartsWith("export ", StringComparison.Ordinal))
+            {
+                line = line["export ".Length..].TrimStart();
+            }
+
+            var equals = line.IndexOf('=', StringComparison.Ordinal);
+            if (equals <= 0)
+            {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            var key = line[..equals].Trim();
+            var value = line[(equals + 1)..].Trim();
+            if (value.Length >= 2 && (value[0] == '"' || value[0] == '\'') && value[^1] == value[0])
+            {
+                value = value[1..^1];
+            }
+
+            values[key] = value;
+        }
+
+        return values;
+    }
+
+    private static bool TryReadEndpoint(string connectionString, bool isSqlServer, int defaultPort, out string host, out int port, out string problem)
+    {
+        host = string.Empty;
+        port = defaultPort;
+        problem = string.Empty;
+        var builder = new DbConnectionStringBuilder();
+        try
+        {
+            builder.ConnectionString = connectionString;
+        }
+        catch (ArgumentException)
+        {
+            problem = "is not a valid connection string";
+            return false;
+        }
+
+        if (isSqlServer)
+        {
+            var server = FirstValue(builder, "Server", "Data Source", "Address", "Addr", "Network Address");
+            if (server is null)
+            {
+                problem = "names no server";
+                return false;
+            }
+
+            if (server.StartsWith("(localdb)", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
+            {
+                problem = "names a LocalDB server, which has no TCP endpoint on Linux";
+                return false;
+            }
+
+            if (server.StartsWith("tcp:", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
+            {
+                server = server[4..];
+            }
+
+            var comma = server.IndexOf(',', StringComparison.Ordinal);
+            if (comma >= 0)
+            {
+                if (!int.TryParse(server[(comma + 1)..].Trim(), NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out port))
+                {
+                    problem = "names a server with an unreadable port";
+                    return false;
+                }
+
+                server = server[..comma];
+            }
+
+            host = server.Split('\\')[0].Trim();
+        }
+        else
+        {
+            var server = FirstValue(builder, "Host", "Server");
+            if (server is null)
+            {
+                problem = "names no host";
+                return false;
+            }
+
+            host = server.Split(',')[0].Trim();
+            var portText = FirstValue(builder, "Port");
+            if (portText is not null && !int.TryParse(portText, NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out port))
+            {
+                problem = "names an unreadable port";
+                return false;
+            }
+        }
+
+        if (host.Length == 0 || host == ".")
+        {
+            host = "localhost";
+        }
+
+        return true;
+    }
+
+    private static string? FirstValue(DbConnectionStringBuilder builder, params string[] keys)
+    {
+        foreach (var key in keys)
+        {
+            if (builder.TryGetValue(key, out var value) && value is not null && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value.ToString()))
+            {
+                return value.ToString()!.Trim();
+            }
+        }
+
+        return null;
+    }
+}
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-018: Real host boundary: process environment, file system, docker CLI (inspect and start only) and TCP.
+/// </summary>
+sealed class SystemProviderTestHost : IProviderTestHost
+{
+    /// <inheritdoc />
+    public string? GetVariable(string name) => Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(name);
+
+    /// <inheritdoc />
+    public void SetVariable(string name, string value) => Environment.SetEnvironmentVariable(name, value);
+
+    /// <inheritdoc />
+    public string? ReadFile(string path) => File.Exists(path) ? File.ReadAllText(path) : null;
+
+    /// <inheritdoc />
+    public ProviderTestContainerState GetContainerState(string containerName)
+    {
+        var (exitCode, output) = Docker("inspect", "--format", "{{.State.Status}}", containerName);
+        if (exitCode != 0)
+        {
+            return ProviderTestContainerState.Missing;
+        }
+
+        return string.Equals(output.Trim(), "running", StringComparison.Ordinal)
+            ? ProviderTestContainerState.Running
+            : ProviderTestContainerState.Stopped;
+    }
+
+    /// <inheritdoc />
+    public bool StartContainer(string containerName)
+        => Docker("start", containerName).ExitCode == 0
+           && GetContainerState(containerName) == ProviderTestContainerState.Running;
+
+    /// <inheritdoc />
+    public bool CanConnect(string host, int port, TimeSpan timeout)
+    {
+        try
+        {
+            using var client = new TcpClient();
+            return client.ConnectAsync(host, port).Wait(timeout) && client.Connected;
+        }
+        catch (Exception exception) when (exception is SocketException or AggregateException)
+        {
+            return false;
+        }
+    }
+
+    private static (int ExitCode, string Output) Docker(params string[] arguments)
+    {
+        var start = new ProcessStartInfo("docker") { RedirectStandardOutput = true, RedirectStandardError = true, UseShellExecute = false };
+        foreach (var argument in arguments)
+        {
+            start.ArgumentList.Add(argument);
+        }
+
+        using var process = Process.Start(start) ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Could not start docker.");
+        var output = process.StandardOutput.ReadToEndAsync();
+        var error = process.StandardError.ReadToEndAsync();
+        if (!process.WaitForExit(TimeSpan.FromMinutes(2)))
+        {
+            process.Kill(entireProcessTree: true);
+            return (-1, string.Empty);
+        }
+
+        _ = error.GetAwaiter().GetResult();
+        return (process.ExitCode, output.GetAwaiter().GetResult());
+    }
+}

+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019: Production <see cref="IOllamaInstallLayout"/>. The portable release assets are those the
+/// ollama/ollama releases ship (ollama-windows-amd64.zip with the executable at its root, ollama-linux-amd64.tar.zst
+/// with the executable under bin/); an installed executable is looked for on PATH first, then in the per-user Windows
+/// install, then at the portable location.
+/// </summary>
+sealed class OllamaInstallLayout : IOllamaInstallLayout
+{
+    /// <inheritdoc />
+    public string ReleaseAsset(IntegrationLaneHost host) => host switch
+    {
+        IntegrationLaneHost.Windows => "ollama-windows-amd64.zip",
+        IntegrationLaneHost.Linux => "ollama-linux-amd64.tar.zst",
+        _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(host), host, "Unknown integration lane host."),
+    };
+
+    /// <inheritdoc />
+    public string PortableExecutable(IntegrationLaneHost host, string testToolsDirectory) => host switch
+    {
+        IntegrationLaneHost.Windows => Path.Combine(testToolsDirectory, "ollama", "ollama.exe"),
+        IntegrationLaneHost.Linux => Path.Combine(testToolsDirectory, "ollama", "bin", "ollama"),
+        _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(host), host, "Unknown integration lane host."),
+    };
+
+    /// <inheritdoc />
+    public IReadOnlyList<string> Candidates(
+        IntegrationLaneHost host,
+        IReadOnlyList<string> pathDirectories,
+        string localApplicationData,
+        string testToolsDirectory)
+    {
+        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(pathDirectories);
+        var executableName = host == IntegrationLaneHost.Windows ? "ollama.exe" : "ollama";
+        var candidates = pathDirectories
+            .Where(directory => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(directory))
+            .Select(directory => Path.Combine(directory.Trim(), executableName))
+            .ToList();
+        if (host == IntegrationLaneHost.Windows)
+            candidates.Add(Path.Combine(localApplicationData, "Programs", "Ollama", "ollama.exe"));
+        candidates.Add(PortableExecutable(host, testToolsDirectory));
+        return candidates;
+    }
+}

+/// <summary>

+        IReadOnlyList<string> pathDirectories,
+        string localApplicationData,
+        string testToolsDirectory);
+}

diff --git a/build/ProvisioningDownload.cs b/build/ProvisioningDownload.cs
new file mode 100644

+++ b/build/ProvisioningDownload.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,63 @@
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019: the provisioning download. The client has no total time limit; the request completes at the
+/// response headers, the body is written to the destination as each read arrives (an unbuffered file), and the
+/// inactivity limit restarts after every read, so a large release asset that keeps arriving finishes while a stalled
+/// transfer fails. An error status or a stall fails with the URL in the message.
+/// </summary>
+sealed class ProvisioningDownload : IProvisioningDownload
+{
+    /// <summary>The inactivity limit the provisioning targets use (TR-MCP-NUKE-019: two minutes).</summary>
+    internal static readonly TimeSpan DefaultInactivityLimit = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(2);
+
+    /// <inheritdoc />
+    public HttpClient CreateClient(HttpMessageHandler handler)
+        => new(handler, disposeHandler: false) { Timeout = Timeout.InfiniteTimeSpan };
+
+    /// <inheritdoc />
+    public async Task DownloadAsync(
+        HttpMessageHandler handler,
+        Uri url,
+        string destinationPath,
+        TimeSpan inactivityLimit,
+        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
+    {
+        using var client = CreateClient(handler);
+        using var inactivity = CancellationTokenSource.CreateLinkedTokenSource(cancellationToken);
+        inactivity.CancelAfter(inactivityLimit);
+        try
+        {
+            using var response = await client
+                .GetAsync(url, HttpCompletionOption.ResponseHeadersRead, inactivity.Token)
+                .ConfigureAwait(false);
+            if (!response.IsSuccessStatusCode)
+            {
+                throw new HttpRequestException(
+                    $"Download of {url} failed: the server answered {(int)response.StatusCode} {response.ReasonPhrase}.",
+                    inner: null,
+                    response.StatusCode);
+            }
+
+            await using var source = await response.Content.ReadAsStreamAsync(inactivity.Token).ConfigureAwait(false);
+            await using var target = new FileStream(
+                destinationPath,
+                FileMode.Create,
+                FileAccess.Write,
+                FileShare.None,
+                bufferSize: 1,
+                useAsync: true);
+            var buffer = new byte[81920];
+            while (true)
+            {
+                inactivity.CancelAfter(inactivityLimit);
+                var read = await source.ReadAsync(buffer, inactivity.Token).ConfigureAwait(false);
+                if (read == 0)
+                    break;
+                await target.WriteAsync(buffer.AsMemory(0, read), cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);
+            }
+        }
+        catch (OperationCanceledException) when (inactivity.IsCancellationRequested && !cancellationToken.IsCancellationRequested)
+        {
+            throw new TimeoutException($"Download of {url} stopped: no data arrived for {inactivityLimit}.");
+        }
+    }
+}
diff --git a/build/ProvisioningDownloadContracts.cs b/build/ProvisioningDownloadContracts.cs

+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019: how a provisioning download (the portable Ollama release, the SQL Server LocalDB installer,
+/// the PostgreSQL binaries) reaches disk. The body is written to the destination file as it arrives and the download
+/// has no limit on its total duration; it fails, naming the URL, when the server answers an error status or when no
+/// data arrives for the inactivity limit.
+/// </summary>
+interface IProvisioningDownload
+{
+    /// <summary>Creates the HTTP client a download uses over <paramref name="handler"/>; it has no total time limit.</summary>
+    /// <param name="handler">The message handler the client sends through; the caller owns it.</param>
+    HttpClient CreateClient(HttpMessageHandler handler);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Downloads <paramref name="url"/> to <paramref name="destinationPath"/>, writing the body as it arrives. Throws,
+    /// with the URL in the message, on an error status or when no data arrives for <paramref name="inactivityLimit"/>.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="handler">The message handler the download sends through; the caller owns it.</param>
+    /// <param name="url">The file to download.</param>
+    /// <param name="destinationPath">The file the body is written to.</param>
+    /// <param name="inactivityLimit">The longest time without any data before the download fails.</param>
+    /// <param name="cancellationToken">Cancels the download.</param>
+    Task DownloadAsync(
+        HttpMessageHandler handler,
+        Uri url,
+        string destinationPath,
+        TimeSpan inactivityLimit,
+        CancellationToken cancellationToken);
+}

@@ -0,0 +1,41 @@
+// TR-MCP-REPL-001 AC-002: one parse rule for the REPL command timeouts configured through
+// MCPSERVER_REPL_COMMAND_TIMEOUT_SECONDS (agent stdio requests) and MCPSERVER_REPL_STREAM_COMMAND_TIMEOUT_SECONDS
+// (stream commands), so a misconfigured value never fails a request.
+
+using System.Globalization;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Repl.Core;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-REPL-001 AC-002: parses a configured REPL command timeout. A whole number of seconds from 1 to
+/// <see cref="LargestSeconds"/>, the largest delay <see cref="CancellationTokenSource.CancelAfter(TimeSpan)"/> accepts,
+/// is used; any other value (unset, empty, not a number, zero, negative or larger) gives <see cref="Default"/>.
+/// </summary>
+internal static class ReplCommandTimeout
+{
+    /// <summary>The timeout that applies when no valid value is configured.</summary>
+    internal static readonly TimeSpan Default = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(2);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// The largest whole number of seconds the cancellation timer accepts: its limit is 4294967294 milliseconds.
+    /// </summary>
+    internal const long LargestSeconds = 4294967;
+
+    /// <summary>Parses a configured value into the timeout that applies.</summary>
+    /// <param name="configured">The configured value, or null when the variable is unset.</param>
+    /// <returns>The configured timeout when it is valid; otherwise <see cref="Default"/>.</returns>
+    internal static TimeSpan Parse(string? configured) =>
+        long.TryParse(configured, NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var seconds) && seconds is >= 1 and <= LargestSeconds
+            ? TimeSpan.FromSeconds(seconds)
+            : Default;
+
+    /// <summary>Reads the variable that configures a timeout once and parses it; both call sites use this.</summary>
+    /// <param name="readVariable">Reads one environment variable by name; the call sites default to the process environment.</param>
+    /// <param name="variableName">The variable that configures the timeout.</param>
+    /// <returns>The timeout that applies.</returns>
+    internal static TimeSpan Read(Func<string, string?> readVariable, string variableName)
+    {
+        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(readVariable);
+        return Parse(readVariable(variableName));
+    }
+}

+

+}

+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-WS-005 / AC-TR-MCP-WS-005-001, TR-MCP-SESSIONLOG-006 / AC-TR-MCP-SESSIONLOG-006-009:
+/// one entry of a single directory listing, as the user-profile scans read it.
+/// </summary>

+/// <param name="IsDirectory">True when the entry is a directory, or a link that resolves to one.</param>
+/// <param name="IsLink">True when the entry is a link or other reparse point (symbolic link, junction, mount point).</param>

diff --git a/src/QBrainAi.Services/Services/DirectoryTreeWalker.cs b/src/QBrainAi.Services/Services/DirectoryTreeWalker.cs
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..08a2d74d
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/QBrainAi.Services/Services/DirectoryTreeWalker.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,160 @@
+namespace QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Services;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-WS-005 / AC-TR-MCP-WS-005-001, TR-MCP-SESSIONLOG-006 / AC-TR-MCP-SESSIONLOG-006-009:
+/// walks a directory tree under the user profile breadth first without following directory links or other
+/// reparse points, and skips the contents of any directory whose listing fails with an IO or access error
+/// (an untrusted mount point, an access-denied or vanished folder), so a scan never fails on what it cannot
+/// traverse.
+/// </summary>
+/// <remarks>
+/// A directory is yielded when its parent lists it, unless it is a link; its contents are walked only when its own
+/// listing succeeds. File links are yielded as files. Listing errors other than <see cref="IOException"/> and
+/// <see cref="UnauthorizedAccessException"/> propagate. A scan root below the user profile or home is walked only
+/// when <see cref="IsReachable"/> holds for it (hostile validation round 5 finding R5-F1; operator 2026-10-21:
+/// "Fix both, strengthen ACs").
+/// </remarks>
+internal sealed class DirectoryTreeWalker
+{
+    private static readonly StringComparison s_pathComparison =
+        OperatingSystem.IsWindows() ? StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase : StringComparison.Ordinal;
+
+    private static readonly EnumerationOptions s_singleLevel = new()
+    {
+        RecurseSubdirectories = false,
+        IgnoreInaccessible = false,
+        AttributesToSkip = 0,
+        ReturnSpecialDirectories = false,
+    };
+
+    private readonly Func<string, IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry>> _list;
+
+    /// <summary>Creates a walker that lists each directory through <paramref name="list"/>.</summary>
+    /// <param name="list">Lists one directory, or throws when it cannot be listed.</param>
+    internal DirectoryTreeWalker(Func<string, IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry>> list)
+    {
+        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(list);
+        _list = list;
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>The walker over the real file system.</summary>
+    internal static DirectoryTreeWalker FileSystem { get; } = new(ListFileSystem);
+
+    /// <summary>Every directory below <paramref name="root"/> that the walk reaches.</summary>
+    /// <param name="root">Directory to walk; it is listed but not yielded.</param>
+    /// <returns>The reached directories, breadth first.</returns>
+    internal IEnumerable<string> EnumerateDirectories(string root) => Walk(root, yieldDirectories: true);
+
+    /// <summary>Every file below <paramref name="root"/> that the walk reaches.</summary>
+    /// <param name="root">Directory to walk.</param>
+    /// <returns>The reached files, breadth first.</returns>
+    internal IEnumerable<string> EnumerateFiles(string root) => Walk(root, yieldDirectories: false);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// AC-TR-MCP-WS-005-001, AC-TR-MCP-SESSIONLOG-006-009: whether <paramref name="root"/> may be walked from
+    /// <paramref name="boundary"/>: the root and every folder between the boundary and it must each be listed by
+    /// its parent as a directory that is not a link or other reparse point. The boundary itself is trusted (a user
+    /// profile or home that is itself a link is still scanned) and its parent is never listed; a root that is the
+    /// boundary is reachable without any listing.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <remarks>
+    /// A folder whose listing fails with an <see cref="IOException"/> or <see cref="UnauthorizedAccessException"/>
+    /// cannot be traversed, so the root is not reachable; any other listing error propagates. A root that is not
+    /// below the boundary, or that a parent does not list as a directory, is not reachable.
+    /// </remarks>
+    /// <param name="boundary">The trusted folder the scan starts from (the user profile or home).</param>
+    /// <param name="root">The scan root below <paramref name="boundary"/>.</param>
+    /// <returns>True when the root may be walked.</returns>
+    internal bool IsReachable(string boundary, string root)
+    {
+        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(boundary);
+        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(root);
+        var target = Path.TrimEndingDirectorySeparator(root);
+        var current = Path.TrimEndingDirectorySeparator(boundary);
+        while (!string.Equals(current, target, s_pathComparison))
+        {
+            IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry> entries;
+            try
+            {
+                entries = _list(current);
+            }
+            catch (IOException)
+            {
+                return false;
+            }
+            catch (UnauthorizedAccessException)
+            {
+                return false;
+            }
+
+            var found = entries
+                .Where(entry => LeadsTo(entry.Path, target))
+                .Select(entry => (DirectoryListingEntry?)entry)
+                .FirstOrDefault();
+            if (found is not { } next || !next.IsDirectory || next.IsLink || next.Path.Length <= current.Length)
+                return false;
+            current = next.Path;
+        }
+
+        return true;
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>True when <paramref name="path"/> is <paramref name="target"/> or a folder above it.</summary>
+    private static bool LeadsTo(string path, string target) =>
+        target.StartsWith(path, s_pathComparison)
+        && (target.Length == path.Length
+            || target[path.Length] == Path.DirectorySeparatorChar
+            || target[path.Length] == Path.AltDirectorySeparatorChar);
+
+    /// <summary>Lists one real directory with each entry's directory and link flags.</summary>
+    /// <param name="directory">Directory to list.</param>
+    /// <returns>The entries of <paramref name="directory"/>.</returns>
+    internal static IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry> ListFileSystem(string directory) =>
+        new DirectoryInfo(directory)
+            .EnumerateFileSystemInfos("*", s_singleLevel)
+            .Select(info => new DirectoryListingEntry(
+                info.FullName,
+                info is DirectoryInfo,
+                (info.Attributes & FileAttributes.ReparsePoint) != 0))
+            .ToList();
+
+    private IEnumerable<string> Walk(string root, bool yieldDirectories)
+    {
+        var pending = new Queue<string>();
+        pending.Enqueue(root);
+        while (pending.Count > 0)
+        {
+            foreach (var entry in TryList(pending.Dequeue()))
+            {
+                if (entry.IsDirectory)
+                {
+                    if (entry.IsLink)
+                        continue;
+                    pending.Enqueue(entry.Path);
+                    if (yieldDirectories)
+                        yield return entry.Path;
+                }
+                else if (!yieldDirectories)
+                {
+                    yield return entry.Path;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    private IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry> TryList(string directory)
+    {
+        try
+        {
+            return _list(directory);
+        }
+        catch (IOException)
+        {
+            return [];
+        }
+        catch (UnauthorizedAccessException)
+        {
+            return [];
+        }
+    }
+}

diff --git a/tests/Build.Tests/IntegrationLaneFilterContract.cs b/tests/Build.Tests/IntegrationLaneFilterContract.cs
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..18c2ac4a
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/Build.Tests/IntegrationLaneFilterContract.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,93 @@
+namespace NukeBuild.Tests;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019 (AC-1): Contract cases and assertions for <see cref="IIntegrationLaneFilter"/>. The expected filters
+/// come from the acceptance criterion; a small evaluator of the trait grammar the lane uses (terms joined by '&amp;',
+/// Name=Value requires the trait, Name!=Value is met by any other value or by no trait) checks what each filter
+/// selects for an untagged case, an own-host case, an other-host case and a non-integration case. The same
+/// assertions run against the scripted filter (BDPv4 mock gate), wrong filters (negative controls), and the
+/// production filter.
+/// </summary>
+internal static class IntegrationLaneFilterContract
+{
+    /// <summary>Every host name, in the order the contract checks them.</summary>
+    internal static readonly string[] HostNames = ["windows", "linux"];
+
+    /// <summary>Parses a contract host name.</summary>
+    /// <param name="hostName">windows or linux.</param>
+    internal static IntegrationLaneHost Parse(string hostName) => hostName switch
+    {
+        "windows" => IntegrationLaneHost.Windows,
+        "linux" => IntegrationLaneHost.Linux,
+        _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(hostName), hostName, "Unknown contract host."),
+    };
+
+    /// <summary>The filter TR-MCP-NUKE-019 AC-1 requires for <paramref name="host"/>.</summary>
+    /// <param name="host">Host the lane runs on.</param>
+    internal static string ExpectedFilter(IntegrationLaneHost host) => host switch
+    {
+        IntegrationLaneHost.Windows => "Category=Integration&Platform!=Linux",
+        IntegrationLaneHost.Linux => "Category=Integration&Platform!=Windows",
+        _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(host), host, "Unknown host."),
+    };
+
+    /// <summary>Asserts that <paramref name="filter"/> meets the contract for the named host.</summary>
+    /// <param name="hostName">windows or linux.</param>
+    /// <param name="filter">Filter under test.</param>
+    internal static void AssertHost(string hostName, IIntegrationLaneFilter filter)
+    {
+        var host = Parse(hostName);
+        var actual = filter.Compose(host);
+
+        Assert.Equal(ExpectedFilter(host), actual);
+        AssertSelection(host, actual);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Asserts what <paramref name="filter"/> selects on <paramref name="host"/>: untagged and own-host integration
+    /// cases run; other-host and non-integration cases do not. Independent of the exact filter text, so the negative
+    /// controls prove each of these assertions rejects a wrong filter on its own.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="host">Host the lane runs on.</param>
+    /// <param name="filter">Filter text under test.</param>
+    internal static void AssertSelection(IntegrationLaneHost host, string filter)
+    {
+        var own = host == IntegrationLaneHost.Windows ? "Windows" : "Linux";
+        var other = host == IntegrationLaneHost.Windows ? "Linux" : "Windows";
+
+        Assert.True(Selects(filter, category: "Integration", platform: null), "An untagged integration case must run on every host.");
+        Assert.True(Selects(filter, category: "Integration", platform: own), $"A Platform={own} integration case must run on its own host.");
+        Assert.False(Selects(filter, category: "Integration", platform: other), $"A Platform={other} integration case must not run on a {own} host.");
+        Assert.False(Selects(filter, category: null, platform: null), "A case without Category=Integration must not run in the integration lane.");
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Evaluates the '&amp;'-joined Name=Value and Name!=Value terms against one case's traits.</summary>
+    /// <param name="filter">Filter text.</param>
+    /// <param name="category">The case's Category trait, or null.</param>
+    /// <param name="platform">The case's Platform trait, or null.</param>
+    internal static bool Selects(string filter, string? category, string? platform)
+    {
+        var traits = new Dictionary<string, string?>(StringComparer.Ordinal)
+        {
+            ["Category"] = category,
+            ["Platform"] = platform,
+        };
+        foreach (var term in filter.Split('&', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries | StringSplitOptions.TrimEntries))
+        {
+            var negated = term.Contains("!=", StringComparison.Ordinal);
+            var parts = term.Split(negated ? "!=" : "=", 2, StringSplitOptions.TrimEntries);
+            if (parts.Length != 2 || !traits.TryGetValue(parts[0], out var value))
+            {
+                throw new FormatException($"Unsupported filter term '{term}'.");
+            }
+
+            var equal = string.Equals(value, parts[1], StringComparison.Ordinal);
+            if (negated ? equal : !equal)
+            {
+                return false;
+            }
+        }
+
+        return true;
+    }
+}

+namespace NukeBuild.Tests;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019 (AC-1), BDPv4 mock gate: proves the <see cref="IntegrationLaneFilterContract"/> assertions accept a
+/// scripted filter that produces the contract-derived filters, and reject deliberately wrong filters (negative
+/// controls), before any production filter code is written.
+/// </summary>
+public sealed class IntegrationLaneFilterMockGateTests
+{
+    /// <summary>TR-MCP-NUKE-019: every host passes against the scripted filter.</summary>
+    /// <param name="host">Host name from <see cref="IntegrationLaneFilterContract.HostNames"/>.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("windows")]
+    [InlineData("linux")]
+    public void ScriptedFilter_PassesHost(string host)
+        => IntegrationLaneFilterContract.AssertHost(host, new ScriptedFilter(Fault.None));
+
+    /// <summary>TR-MCP-NUKE-019: the theory above covers every contract host.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ScriptedFilterTheory_CoversEveryHost()
+        => Assert.Equal(2, IntegrationLaneFilterContract.HostNames.Length);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// TR-MCP-NUKE-019 negative controls: each wrong filter is rejected (the other host's cases admitted, untagged
+    /// cases dropped, hosts swapped, the Category=Integration term dropped).
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="host">Host the fault is checked on.</param>
+    /// <param name="fault">Injected fault.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("windows", Fault.AdmitsOtherHost)]
+    [InlineData("linux", Fault.AdmitsOtherHost)]
+    [InlineData("windows", Fault.DropsUntagged)]
+    [InlineData("linux", Fault.DropsUntagged)]
+    [InlineData("windows", Fault.SwapsHosts)]
+    [InlineData("linux", Fault.SwapsHosts)]
+    [InlineData("windows", Fault.DropsCategory)]
+    [InlineData("linux", Fault.DropsCategory)]
+    public void WrongFilter_IsRejected(string host, Fault fault)
+        => Assert.ThrowsAny<Xunit.Sdk.XunitException>(
+            () => IntegrationLaneFilterContract.AssertHost(host, new ScriptedFilter(fault)));
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// TR-MCP-NUKE-019 negative controls: the selection assertions alone, without the exact-text check, reject each
+    /// wrong filter. Each fault breaks a different assertion (other host admitted, untagged dropped, own host
+    /// excluded, non-integration admitted), so every selection assertion is shown to discriminate.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="host">Host the fault is checked on.</param>
+    /// <param name="fault">Injected fault.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("windows", Fault.AdmitsOtherHost)]
+    [InlineData("linux", Fault.AdmitsOtherHost)]
+    [InlineData("windows", Fault.DropsUntagged)]
+    [InlineData("linux", Fault.DropsUntagged)]
+    [InlineData("windows", Fault.SwapsHosts)]
+    [InlineData("linux", Fault.SwapsHosts)]
+    [InlineData("windows", Fault.DropsCategory)]
+    [InlineData("linux", Fault.DropsCategory)]
+    public void WrongFilter_FailsSelection(string host, Fault fault)
+    {
+        var parsed = IntegrationLaneFilterContract.Parse(host);
+        var filter = new ScriptedFilter(fault).Compose(parsed);
+        Assert.ThrowsAny<Xunit.Sdk.XunitException>(() => IntegrationLaneFilterContract.AssertSelection(parsed, filter));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>TR-MCP-NUKE-019: the contract evaluator refuses a term it cannot read instead of guessing.</summary>
+    /// <param name="filter">Filter with an unsupported term.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("Category~Integration")]
+    [InlineData("Category=Integration&Owner!=Linux")]
+    public void Evaluator_RejectsUnsupportedTerm(string filter)
+        => Assert.Throws<FormatException>(() => IntegrationLaneFilterContract.Selects(filter, "Integration", null));
+
+    /// <summary>Faults injected into the scripted filter for negative controls.</summary>
+    public enum Fault
+    {
+        /// <summary>No fault: the contract-derived filter.</summary>
+        None,
+
+        /// <summary>Selects every integration case, including the other host's.</summary>
+        AdmitsOtherHost,
+
+        /// <summary>Selects only cases tagged for the host, dropping untagged integration cases.</summary>
+        DropsUntagged,
+
+        /// <summary>Excludes the host's own cases instead of the other host's.</summary>
+        SwapsHosts,
+
+        /// <summary>Drops the Category=Integration term.</summary>
+        DropsCategory,
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Scripted filter: the contract-derived filters, with an optional injected fault.</summary>
+    private sealed class ScriptedFilter(Fault fault) : IIntegrationLaneFilter
+    {
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public string Compose(IntegrationLaneHost host)
+        {
+            var own = host == IntegrationLaneHost.Windows ? "Windows" : "Linux";
+            var other = host == IntegrationLaneHost.Windows ? "Linux" : "Windows";
+            return fault switch
+            {
+                Fault.None => $"Category=Integration&Platform!={other}",
+                Fault.AdmitsOtherHost => "Category=Integration",
+                Fault.DropsUntagged => $"Category=Integration&Platform={own}",
+                Fault.SwapsHosts => $"Category=Integration&Platform!={own}",
+                Fault.DropsCategory => $"Platform!={other}",
+                _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(fault), fault, "Unknown fault."),
+            };
+        }
+    }
+}

diff --git a/tests/Build.Tests/IntegrationLanePreconditionContract.cs b/tests/Build.Tests/IntegrationLanePreconditionContract.cs

index 00000000..91d0a858

+namespace NukeBuild.Tests;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019: contract scenarios and assertions for <see cref="IIntegrationLanePrecondition"/>. The
+/// expected outcomes come from the criterion: on Windows the lane runs only from an interactive desktop session
+/// and otherwise fails before any test, naming the missing session; on Linux no session requirement applies. The
+/// same assertions run against the scripted precondition (BDPv4 mock gate), wrong preconditions (negative
+/// controls), and the production precondition.
+/// </summary>
+internal static class IntegrationLanePreconditionContract
+{
+    /// <summary>Every scenario name, in the order the contract checks them.</summary>
+    internal static readonly string[] ScenarioNames =
+    [
+        "windows-interactive",
+        "windows-noninteractive",
+        "linux-interactive",
+        "linux-noninteractive",
+    ];
+
+    /// <summary>Asserts that <paramref name="precondition"/> meets the named scenario.</summary>
+    /// <param name="scenario">Scenario name from <see cref="ScenarioNames"/>.</param>
+    /// <param name="precondition">Precondition under test.</param>
+    internal static void AssertScenario(string scenario, IIntegrationLanePrecondition precondition)
+    {
+        var (host, interactive, mayRun) = scenario switch
+        {
+            "windows-interactive" => (IntegrationLaneHost.Windows, true, true),
+            "windows-noninteractive" => (IntegrationLaneHost.Windows, false, false),
+            "linux-interactive" => (IntegrationLaneHost.Linux, true, true),
+            "linux-noninteractive" => (IntegrationLaneHost.Linux, false, true),
+            _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(scenario), scenario, "Unknown contract scenario."),
+        };
+
+        var readiness = precondition.Evaluate(host, interactive);
+
+        Assert.Equal(mayRun, readiness.CanRun);
+        if (mayRun)
+        {
+            Assert.Null(readiness.FailureMessage);
+            return;
+        }
+
+        Assert.False(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(readiness.FailureMessage), "A lane that cannot run must say why instead of stopping silently.");
+        Assert.Contains("interactive desktop session", readiness.FailureMessage, StringComparison.Ordinal);
+        Assert.Contains("MigrationIntegrationTests", readiness.FailureMessage, StringComparison.Ordinal);
+    }
+}

+namespace NukeBuild.Tests;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019, BDPv4 mock gate: proves the <see cref="IntegrationLanePreconditionContract"/> assertions
+/// accept a scripted precondition that produces the criterion's outcomes, and reject deliberately wrong
+/// preconditions (negative controls), before any production precondition code is written.
+/// </summary>
+public sealed class IntegrationLanePreconditionMockGateTests
+{
+    /// <summary>TR-MCP-NUKE-019: every scenario passes against the scripted precondition.</summary>
+    /// <param name="scenario">Scenario name from <see cref="IntegrationLanePreconditionContract.ScenarioNames"/>.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("windows-interactive")]
+    [InlineData("windows-noninteractive")]
+    [InlineData("linux-interactive")]
+    [InlineData("linux-noninteractive")]
+    public void ScriptedPrecondition_PassesScenario(string scenario)
+        => IntegrationLanePreconditionContract.AssertScenario(scenario, new ScriptedPrecondition(Fault.None));
+
+    /// <summary>TR-MCP-NUKE-019: the theory above covers every contract scenario.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ScriptedTheory_CoversEveryScenario()
+        => Assert.Equal(4, IntegrationLanePreconditionContract.ScenarioNames.Length);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// TR-MCP-NUKE-019 negative controls: a precondition that runs a non-interactive Windows session, stops
+    /// without a message (a silent skip), names no session in its message, or blocks a Linux host, is rejected.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="scenario">Scenario the fault breaks.</param>
+    /// <param name="fault">Injected fault.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("windows-noninteractive", Fault.RunsWhenNonInteractive)]
+    [InlineData("windows-noninteractive", Fault.StopsSilently)]
+    [InlineData("windows-noninteractive", Fault.MessageOmitsSession)]
+    [InlineData("linux-noninteractive", Fault.BlocksLinux)]
+    public void WrongPrecondition_IsRejected(string scenario, Fault fault)
+        => Assert.ThrowsAny<Xunit.Sdk.XunitException>(
+            () => IntegrationLanePreconditionContract.AssertScenario(scenario, new ScriptedPrecondition(fault)));
+
+    /// <summary>Faults injected into the scripted precondition for negative controls.</summary>
+    public enum Fault
+    {
+        /// <summary>No fault: the criterion's outcomes.</summary>
+        None,
+
+        /// <summary>Lets a non-interactive Windows session run the lane.</summary>
+        RunsWhenNonInteractive,
+
+        /// <summary>Refuses the lane but gives no message, so the run would stop silently.</summary>
+        StopsSilently,
+
+        /// <summary>Refuses the lane with a message that does not name the missing session.</summary>
+        MessageOmitsSession,
+
+        /// <summary>Applies the Windows session requirement to a Linux host.</summary>
+        BlocksLinux,
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Scripted precondition: the criterion's outcomes, with an optional injected fault.</summary>
+    private sealed class ScriptedPrecondition(Fault fault) : IIntegrationLanePrecondition
+    {
+        private const string Message = "MigrationIntegrationTests needs an interactive desktop session on Windows.";
+
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public IntegrationLaneReadiness Evaluate(IntegrationLaneHost host, bool userInteractive)
+        {
+            if (fault == Fault.BlocksLinux && !userInteractive)
+                return new IntegrationLaneReadiness(false, Message);
+            if (host != IntegrationLaneHost.Windows || userInteractive)
+                return new IntegrationLaneReadiness(true, null);
+
+            return fault switch
+            {
+                Fault.RunsWhenNonInteractive => new IntegrationLaneReadiness(true, null),
+                Fault.StopsSilently => new IntegrationLaneReadiness(false, null),
+                Fault.MessageOmitsSession => new IntegrationLaneReadiness(false, "MigrationIntegrationTests cannot run here."),
+                _ => new IntegrationLaneReadiness(false, Message),
+            };
+        }
+    }
+}

+namespace NukeBuild.Tests;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019 (AC-2): Contract cases and assertions for <see cref="IIntegrationLaneProjects"/>. The expected
+/// selection comes from the acceptance criterion: QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests, QBrainAi.McpAgent.Tests,
+/// QBrainAi.Repl.Core.Tests, QBrainAi.QBAgent.Tests, Build.Tests and QBrainAi.Repl.IntegrationTests, in that order, and
+/// nothing else; QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.IntegrationTests, QBrainAi.Acid.IntegrationTests and
+/// QBrainAi.TransactionSecurity.IntegrationTests stay out until PLAN-SESSIONTRIAGEFIX-001 merges (TODO
+/// IMPL-TXNINTEGRATION-001), and the .Validation projects, the AI review projects and QBrainAi.PluginIntegration.Tests
+/// are not lane projects. The solution row is the 48 project names of QBrainAi.sln as of plan section 22, slice A, in
+/// solution order; the binding tests (IntegrationLaneProjectsTests) check that row against the real solution file. The
+/// same assertions run against the scripted selector (BDPv4 mock gate), wrong selectors (negative controls), and the
+/// production selector.
+/// </summary>
+internal static class IntegrationLaneProjectsContract
+{
+    /// <summary>The six lane projects AC-2 names, in run order.</summary>
+    internal static readonly string[] LaneProjects =
+    [
+        "QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests",
+        "QBrainAi.McpAgent.Tests",
+        "QBrainAi.Repl.Core.Tests",
+        "QBrainAi.QBAgent.Tests",
+        "Build.Tests",
+        "QBrainAi.Repl.IntegrationTests",
+    ];
+
+    /// <summary>The integration projects AC-2 keeps out of the lane until PLAN-SESSIONTRIAGEFIX-001 merges.</summary>
+    internal static readonly string[] HeldOutProjects =
+    [
+        "QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.IntegrationTests",
+        "QBrainAi.Acid.IntegrationTests",
+        "QBrainAi.TransactionSecurity.IntegrationTests",
+    ];
+
+    /// <summary>The project names of QBrainAi.sln as of slice A, in solution order.</summary>
+    internal static readonly string[] Solution =
+    [
+        "QBrainAi.Common.AgentCli", "QBrainAi.ServiceDefaults", "QBrainAi.Support.Mcp", "QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests",
+        "QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.IntegrationTests", "QBrainAi.Client", "QBrainAi.Client.Tests", "QBrainAi.Cqrs.Mvvm",
+        "QBrainAi.Cqrs", "QBrainAi.Cqrs.Tests", "QBrainAi.Launcher", "QBrainAi.Launcher.Tests", "QBrainAi.Storage",
+        "QBrainAi.Services", "QBrainAi.GraphRag", "QBrainAi.Context.Validation", "QBrainAi.GitHub.Validation",
+        "QBrainAi.Repo.Validation", "QBrainAi.SessionLog.Validation", "QBrainAi.Todo.Validation",
+        "QBrainAi.ToolRegistry.Validation", "QBrainAi.Workspace.Validation", "QBrainAi.McpAgent",
+        "QBrainAi.McpAgent.SampleHost", "QBrainAi.McpAgent.Tests", "QBrainAi.Storage.SqliteMigrations",
+        "QBrainAi.Storage.PostgreSqlMigrations", "QBrainAi.Storage.SqlServerMigrations", "QBrainAi.Repl.Core",
+        "QBrainAi.Repl.Host", "QBrainAi.Repl.Core.Tests", "QBrainAi.Repl.IntegrationTests", "_build", "Build.Tests",
+        "QBrainAi.PlanReview.Tests", "QBrainAi.TransactionSecurity", "QBrainAi.KeyServer", "QBrainAi.Subscriber",
+        "QBrainAi.TransactionSecurity.IntegrationTests", "QBrainAi.Acid.IntegrationTests", "QBrainAi.QBAgent",
+        "QBrainAi.QBAgent.Tests", "QBrainAi.QBAgent.Tools", "QBrainAi.QBAgent.Skills", "QBrainAi.Review.Tests",
+        "QBrainAi.SessionLog.Transcripts", "QBrainAi.PluginIntegration.Tests", "QBrainAi.ProcessTree.TestHelper",
+    ];
+
+    /// <summary>The selection AC-2 requires for <see cref="Solution"/>: the six lane projects, in run order.</summary>
+    internal static readonly string[] SolutionSelection = [.. LaneProjects];
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// The contract rows: a row name, the solution's project names, and the selection AC-2 requires: today's solution;
+    /// a new .IntegrationTests project, which does not join the lane; a name that holds IntegrationTests without ending
+    /// in it; and today's solution in reverse order. Every row selects the same six projects in the same order.
+    /// </summary>
+    internal static readonly (string Name, string[] Projects, string[] Expected)[] Rows =
+    [
+        ("the solution as of slice A", Solution, SolutionSelection),
+        ("a new IntegrationTests project is not a lane project", [.. Solution, "QBrainAi.Zeta.IntegrationTests"], SolutionSelection),
+        ("a name holding IntegrationTests without ending in it", [.. Solution, "QBrainAi.IntegrationTestsSupport"], SolutionSelection),
+        ("the solution in reverse order", [.. Enumerable.Reverse(Solution)], SolutionSelection),
+    ];
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Asserts that <paramref name="selector"/> gives the required selection on every row, never selects a held-out
+    /// project, and refuses a solution that lacks Build.Tests or QBrainAi.Repl.IntegrationTests, naming the missing one.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="selector">Selector under test.</param>
+    internal static void AssertContract(IIntegrationLaneProjects selector)
+    {
+        foreach (var (name, projects, expected) in Rows)
+        {
+            var actual = selector.Select(projects);
+            Assert.True(expected.SequenceEqual(actual), $"Row '{name}': expected [{string.Join(", ", expected)}] but got [{string.Join(", ", actual)}].");
+            var heldOut = actual.Intersect(HeldOutProjects, StringComparer.Ordinal).ToArray();
+            Assert.True(heldOut.Length == 0, $"Row '{name}': held-out projects selected: [{string.Join(", ", heldOut)}].");
+        }
+
+        foreach (var required in new[] { "Build.Tests", "QBrainAi.Repl.IntegrationTests" })
+        {
+            var without = Solution.Where(project => project != required).ToArray();
+            var refusal = Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => selector.Select(without));
+            Assert.Contains(required, refusal.Message, StringComparison.Ordinal);
+        }
+    }
+}

+namespace NukeBuild.Tests;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019 (AC-2), BDPv4 mock gate: proves the <see cref="IntegrationLaneProjectsContract"/> assertions accept a
+/// scripted selector that produces the contract-derived selection, and reject deliberately wrong selectors (negative
+/// controls), before any production selector code is written.
+/// </summary>
+public sealed class IntegrationLaneProjectsMockGateTests
+{
+    /// <summary>TR-MCP-NUKE-019 AC-2: the scripted selector satisfies every contract row and both refusals.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ScriptedSelector_SatisfiesTheContract()
+        => IntegrationLaneProjectsContract.AssertContract(new ScriptedSelector(Fault.None));
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// TR-MCP-NUKE-019 AC-2 negative controls, each rejected inside a passing harness:
+    /// - the five projects without QBrainAi.Repl.IntegrationTests (the lane before slice A);
+    /// - every .IntegrationTests project (the superseded rule);
+    /// - QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.IntegrationTests added;
+    /// - QBrainAi.PluginIntegration.Tests added;
+    /// - the .Validation projects added;
+    /// - Build.Tests dropped;
+    /// - QBrainAi.Repl.IntegrationTests run first;
+    /// - the lane projects returned in solution order;
+    /// - a missing lane project ignored.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="fault">Injected fault.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData(Fault.FixedFiveOnly)]
+    [InlineData(Fault.EveryIntegrationProject)]
+    [InlineData(Fault.TakesSupportMcpIntegration)]
+    [InlineData(Fault.TakesPluginIntegration)]
+    [InlineData(Fault.TakesValidation)]
+    [InlineData(Fault.DropsBuildTests)]
+    [InlineData(Fault.ReplIntegrationFirst)]
+    [InlineData(Fault.KeepsSolutionOrder)]
+    [InlineData(Fault.IgnoresMissingProject)]
+    public void WrongSelector_IsRejected(Fault fault)
+        => Assert.ThrowsAny<Xunit.Sdk.XunitException>(
+            () => IntegrationLaneProjectsContract.AssertContract(new ScriptedSelector(fault)));
+
+    /// <summary>Faults injected into the scripted selector for negative controls.</summary>
+    public enum Fault
+    {
+        /// <summary>No fault: the contract-derived selection.</summary>
+        None,
+
+        /// <summary>Selects the five unit and build projects only, without QBrainAi.Repl.IntegrationTests.</summary>
+        FixedFiveOnly,
+
+        /// <summary>Selects the five projects and then every .IntegrationTests project in ordinal order.</summary>
+        EveryIntegrationProject,
+
+        /// <summary>Also selects QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.IntegrationTests.</summary>
+        TakesSupportMcpIntegration,
+
+        /// <summary>Also selects QBrainAi.PluginIntegration.Tests.</summary>
+        TakesPluginIntegration,
+
+        /// <summary>Also selects the .Validation projects.</summary>
+        TakesValidation,
+
+        /// <summary>Leaves out Build.Tests.</summary>
+        DropsBuildTests,
+
+        /// <summary>Runs QBrainAi.Repl.IntegrationTests before the other lane projects.</summary>
+        ReplIntegrationFirst,
+
+        /// <summary>Returns the lane projects in the solution's order instead of the run order.</summary>
+        KeepsSolutionOrder,
+
+        /// <summary>Selects what is present instead of refusing a solution that lacks a lane project.</summary>
+        IgnoresMissingProject,
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Scripted selector: the contract-derived selection, with an optional injected fault.</summary>
+    private sealed class ScriptedSelector(Fault fault) : IIntegrationLaneProjects
+    {
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public IReadOnlyList<string> Select(IReadOnlyList<string> solutionProjectNames)
+        {
+            var lane = IntegrationLaneProjectsContract.LaneProjects
+                .Where(project => fault != Fault.DropsBuildTests || project != "Build.Tests")
+                .Where(project => fault is not (Fault.FixedFiveOnly or Fault.EveryIntegrationProject) || project != "QBrainAi.Repl.IntegrationTests")
+                .ToList();
+            var missing = lane.Where(project => !solutionProjectNames.Contains(project)).ToList();
+            if (missing.Count > 0)
+            {
+                if (fault != Fault.IgnoresMissingProject)
+                    throw new InvalidOperationException("The solution lacks lane projects: " + string.Join(", ", missing));
+                lane = lane.Except(missing).ToList();
+            }
+
+            return fault switch
+            {
+                Fault.EveryIntegrationProject => [.. lane, .. solutionProjectNames.Where(name => name.EndsWith(".IntegrationTests", StringComparison.Ordinal)).OrderBy(name => name, StringComparer.Ordinal)],
+                Fault.TakesSupportMcpIntegration => [.. lane, "QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.IntegrationTests"],
+                Fault.TakesPluginIntegration => [.. lane, "QBrainAi.PluginIntegration.Tests"],
+                Fault.TakesValidation => [.. lane, .. solutionProjectNames.Where(name => name.EndsWith(".Validation", StringComparison.Ordinal))],
+                Fault.ReplIntegrationFirst => ["QBrainAi.Repl.IntegrationTests", .. lane.Where(project => project != "QBrainAi.Repl.IntegrationTests")],
+                Fault.KeepsSolutionOrder => [.. solutionProjectNames.Where(lane.Contains)],
+                _ => lane,
+            };
+        }
+    }
+}

+using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
+
+namespace NukeBuild.Tests;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019 (AC-2): Binds the <see cref="IntegrationLaneProjectsContract"/> cases and assertions, unchanged
+/// from the BDPv4 mock gate, to the production <see cref="IntegrationLaneProjects"/>, and checks the contract's
+/// solution row against the real QBrainAi.sln (read only), so the row cannot drift from the solution the lane selects
+/// from. Build-infrastructure test, accountable through TR only.
+/// </summary>
+public sealed class IntegrationLaneProjectsTests
+{
+    private static readonly Regex ProjectLine = new(
+        "^Project\\(\"\\{[^}]+\\}\"\\) = \"(?<name>[^\"]+)\", \"(?<path>[^\"]+\\.csproj)\"",
+        RegexOptions.Multiline | RegexOptions.CultureInvariant);
+
+    private readonly IntegrationLaneProjects selector = new();
+
+    /// <summary>TR-MCP-NUKE-019 AC-2: the production selector satisfies every contract row and both refusals.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ProductionSelector_SatisfiesTheContract()
+        => IntegrationLaneProjectsContract.AssertContract(selector);
+
+    /// <summary>TR-MCP-NUKE-019 AC-2: the contract's solution row is QBrainAi.sln's project list, in solution order.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void SolutionRow_IsTheSolutionFile()
+        => Assert.Equal(IntegrationLaneProjectsContract.Solution, ReadSolutionProjectNames());
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// TR-MCP-NUKE-019 AC-2: on QBrainAi.sln the production selector gives the six lane projects in run order
+    /// (QBrainAi.Repl.IntegrationTests last) and none of the three held-out .IntegrationTests projects.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ProductionSelector_OnTheSolutionFile_SelectsTheLaneProjects()
+        => Assert.Equal(IntegrationLaneProjectsContract.SolutionSelection, selector.Select(ReadSolutionProjectNames()));
+
+    private static string[] ReadSolutionProjectNames()
+    {
+        var solution = File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(FindRepositoryRoot(), "QBrainAi.sln"));
+        return ProjectLine.Matches(solution).Select(match => match.Groups["name"].Value).ToArray();
+    }
+
+    private static string FindRepositoryRoot()
+    {
+        var directory = new DirectoryInfo(AppContext.BaseDirectory);
+        while (directory is not null)
+        {
+            if (File.Exists(Path.Combine(directory.FullName, "QBrainAi.sln")))
+                return directory.FullName;
+
+            directory = directory.Parent;
+        }
+
+        throw new DirectoryNotFoundException("Could not find repository root containing QBrainAi.sln.");
+    }
+}

+namespace NukeBuild.Tests;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019: Contract cases and assertions for <see cref="IOllamaInstallLayout"/>. The expected values
+/// come from the acceptance criterion (an installed executable is used, otherwise the host's portable release is
+/// placed in the user's QBrainAi test-tools folder) and from the release itself: the latest ollama/ollama release,
+/// v0.40.11 (2026-10-24), ships ollama-windows-amd64.zip, whose executable sits at the archive root, and
+/// ollama-linux-amd64.tar.zst, whose executable sits under bin/. The same assertions run against the scripted layout
+/// (BDPv4 mock gate), wrong layouts (negative controls), and the production layout. Paths are built with
+/// <see cref="Path.Combine(string[])"/> from relative literals on both sides, so the contract reads no host folder and
+/// holds on every host.
+/// </summary>
+internal static class OllamaInstallLayoutContract
+{
+    /// <summary>The scripted test-tools folder.</summary>
+    internal const string TestTools = "tools-root";
+
+    /// <summary>The scripted local application data folder.</summary>
+    internal const string LocalApplicationData = "local-app-data";
+
+    /// <summary>The scripted PATH directories: a plain entry, a blank entry, and an entry with trailing blanks.</summary>
+    internal static readonly string[] PathDirectories = ["path-a", "  ", "path-b "];
+
+    /// <summary>The release asset the release requires for <paramref name="host"/>.</summary>
+    /// <param name="host">Host the lane runs on.</param>
+    internal static string ExpectedAsset(IntegrationLaneHost host) => host switch
+    {
+        IntegrationLaneHost.Windows => "ollama-windows-amd64.zip",
+        IntegrationLaneHost.Linux => "ollama-linux-amd64.tar.zst",
+        _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(host), host, "Unknown host."),
+    };
+
+    /// <summary>The portable executable for <paramref name="host"/> inside <see cref="TestTools"/>.</summary>
+    /// <param name="host">Host the lane runs on.</param>
+    internal static string ExpectedPortable(IntegrationLaneHost host) => host switch
+    {
+        IntegrationLaneHost.Windows => Path.Combine(TestTools, "ollama", "ollama.exe"),
+        IntegrationLaneHost.Linux => Path.Combine(TestTools, "ollama", "bin", "ollama"),
+        _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(host), host, "Unknown host."),
+    };
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// The candidates for <paramref name="host"/>, in order: one per non-blank PATH directory (trimmed), then on Windows
+    /// the usual per-user install, then the portable executable.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="host">Host the lane runs on.</param>
+    internal static string[] ExpectedCandidates(IntegrationLaneHost host) => host switch
+    {
+        IntegrationLaneHost.Windows =>
+        [
+            Path.Combine("path-a", "ollama.exe"),
+            Path.Combine("path-b", "ollama.exe"),
+            Path.Combine(LocalApplicationData, "Programs", "Ollama", "ollama.exe"),
+            ExpectedPortable(IntegrationLaneHost.Windows),
+        ],
+        IntegrationLaneHost.Linux =>
+        [
+            Path.Combine("path-a", "ollama"),
+            Path.Combine("path-b", "ollama"),
+            ExpectedPortable(IntegrationLaneHost.Linux),
+        ],
+        _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(host), host, "Unknown host."),
+    };
+
+    /// <summary>Asserts that <paramref name="layout"/> meets the contract for the named host.</summary>
+    /// <param name="hostName">windows or linux.</param>
+    /// <param name="layout">Layout under test.</param>
+    internal static void AssertHost(string hostName, IOllamaInstallLayout layout)
+    {
+        var host = IntegrationLaneFilterContract.Parse(hostName);
+
+        Assert.Equal(ExpectedAsset(host), layout.ReleaseAsset(host));
+        Assert.Equal(ExpectedPortable(host), layout.PortableExecutable(host, TestTools));
+        Assert.Equal(ExpectedCandidates(host), layout.Candidates(host, PathDirectories, LocalApplicationData, TestTools));
+    }
+}

+namespace NukeBuild.Tests;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019, BDPv4 mock gate: proves the <see cref="OllamaInstallLayoutContract"/> assertions accept a
+/// scripted layout that produces the contract-derived values, and reject deliberately wrong layouts (negative
+/// controls), before any production layout code is written.
+/// </summary>
+public sealed class OllamaInstallLayoutMockGateTests
+{
+    /// <summary>TR-MCP-NUKE-019: every host passes against the scripted layout.</summary>
+    /// <param name="host">Host name from <see cref="OllamaInstallLayoutContract"/>.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("windows")]
+    [InlineData("linux")]
+    public void ScriptedLayout_PassesHost(string host)
+        => OllamaInstallLayoutContract.AssertHost(host, new ScriptedLayout(Fault.None));
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// TR-MCP-NUKE-019 negative controls, each rejected on the host it breaks: Windows names on Linux, a Linux
+    /// portable executable without bin/, the Windows asset everywhere, PATH ignored, the portable executable tried
+    /// first, and a blank PATH entry kept.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="host">Host the fault is checked on.</param>
+    /// <param name="fault">Injected fault.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("linux", Fault.WindowsNameOnLinux)]
+    [InlineData("linux", Fault.PortableWithoutBin)]
+    [InlineData("linux", Fault.WindowsAssetEverywhere)]
+    [InlineData("windows", Fault.IgnoresPath)]
+    [InlineData("linux", Fault.IgnoresPath)]
+    [InlineData("windows", Fault.PortableFirst)]
+    [InlineData("linux", Fault.PortableFirst)]
+    [InlineData("windows", Fault.KeepsBlankEntry)]
+    [InlineData("linux", Fault.KeepsBlankEntry)]
+    public void WrongLayout_IsRejected(string host, Fault fault)
+        => Assert.ThrowsAny<Xunit.Sdk.XunitException>(
+            () => OllamaInstallLayoutContract.AssertHost(host, new ScriptedLayout(fault)));
+
+    /// <summary>Faults injected into the scripted layout for negative controls.</summary>
+    public enum Fault
+    {
+        /// <summary>No fault: the contract-derived layout.</summary>
+        None,
+
+        /// <summary>Uses the Windows executable name on Linux.</summary>
+        WindowsNameOnLinux,
+
+        /// <summary>Places the Linux portable executable at the archive root instead of under bin/.</summary>
+        PortableWithoutBin,
+
+        /// <summary>Uses the Windows zip on every host.</summary>
+        WindowsAssetEverywhere,
+
+        /// <summary>Tries no PATH directory.</summary>
+        IgnoresPath,
+
+        /// <summary>Tries the portable executable before PATH.</summary>
+        PortableFirst,
+
+        /// <summary>Keeps blank PATH entries and does not trim them.</summary>
+        KeepsBlankEntry,
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Scripted layout: the contract-derived values, with an optional injected fault.</summary>
+    private sealed class ScriptedLayout(Fault fault) : IOllamaInstallLayout
+    {
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public string ReleaseAsset(IntegrationLaneHost host)
+            => host == IntegrationLaneHost.Windows || fault == Fault.WindowsAssetEverywhere
+                ? "ollama-windows-amd64.zip"
+                : "ollama-linux-amd64.tar.zst";
+
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public string PortableExecutable(IntegrationLaneHost host, string testToolsDirectory)
+            => host == IntegrationLaneHost.Windows
+                ? Path.Combine(testToolsDirectory, "ollama", "ollama.exe")
+                : fault == Fault.PortableWithoutBin
+                    ? Path.Combine(testToolsDirectory, "ollama", "ollama")
+                    : Path.Combine(testToolsDirectory, "ollama", "bin", Name(host));
+
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public IReadOnlyList<string> Candidates(
+            IntegrationLaneHost host,
+            IReadOnlyList<string> pathDirectories,
+            string localApplicationData,
+            string testToolsDirectory)
+        {
+            var portable = PortableExecutable(host, testToolsDirectory);
+            var fromPath = fault == Fault.IgnoresPath
+                ? new List<string>()
+                : pathDirectories
+                    .Where(directory => fault == Fault.KeepsBlankEntry || !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(directory))
+                    .Select(directory => Path.Combine(fault == Fault.KeepsBlankEntry ? directory : directory.Trim(), Name(host)))
+                    .ToList();
+            var candidates = new List<string>();
+            if (fault == Fault.PortableFirst)
+                candidates.Add(portable);
+            candidates.AddRange(fromPath);
+            if (host == IntegrationLaneHost.Windows)
+                candidates.Add(Path.Combine(localApplicationData, "Programs", "Ollama", "ollama.exe"));
+            if (fault != Fault.PortableFirst)
+                candidates.Add(portable);
+            return candidates;
+        }
+
+        private string Name(IntegrationLaneHost host)
+            => host == IntegrationLaneHost.Windows || fault == Fault.WindowsNameOnLinux ? "ollama.exe" : "ollama";
+    }
+}

diff --git a/tests/Build.Tests/ProviderTestProvisioningContract.cs b/tests/Build.Tests/ProviderTestProvisioningContract.cs
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..6bdf1eac
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/Build.Tests/ProviderTestProvisioningContract.cs
