+namespace NukeBuild.Tests;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-018: Contract scenarios and assertions for <see cref="IProviderTestProvisioner"/>.
+/// A recording fake host stands in for the process environment, the test-database env file, Docker and TCP. The
+/// expectations come from the acceptance criteria; the fixture secrets are fake markers that must never appear in
+/// actions or errors. The same assertions run against the scripted mock (BDPv4 mock gate), wrong mocks (negative
+/// controls), and the production provisioner.
+/// </summary>
+internal static class ProviderTestProvisioningContract
+{
+    /// <summary>Every scenario name, in the order the contract checks them.</summary>
+    internal static readonly string[] ScenarioNames =
+        ["load-from-file", "adopt-set", "file-missing", "variable-missing-in-file", "stopped-container", "missing-container", "unreachable", "localdb-on-linux"];
+
+    /// <summary>Env file path used by every scenario.</summary>
+    internal const string EnvFile = "/home/fixture/.config/qbrain-test/test-db.env";
+
+    /// <summary>Fake PostgreSQL secret marker.</summary>
+    internal const string PostgresSecret = "PG_SECRET_VALUE_7";
+
+    /// <summary>Fake SQL Server secret marker.</summary>
+    internal const string SqlServerSecret = "SQL_SECRET_VALUE_9";
+
+    /// <summary>PostgreSQL connection string in the env file.</summary>
+    internal const string PostgresValue = "Host=localhost;Port=55432;Username=tester;Password=" + PostgresSecret + ";Database=postgres";
+
+    /// <summary>SQL Server connection string in the env file.</summary>
+    internal const string SqlServerValue = "Server=localhost,51433;User ID=sa;Password=" + SqlServerSecret + ";TrustServerCertificate=True";
+
+    /// <summary>Env file defining both variables, with a comment, an export prefix and a quoted value.</summary>
+    internal const string FullEnvFile =
+        "# QBrain.AI provider test servers\n"
+        + "export " + ProviderTestNames.PostgresVariable + "=\"" + PostgresValue + "\"\n"
+        + "\n"
+        + ProviderTestNames.SqlServerVariable + "=" + SqlServerValue + "\n";
+
+    /// <summary>Builds the fake host of a named scenario.</summary>
+    internal static FakeProviderTestHost CreateHost(string scenario)
+    {
+        var host = new FakeProviderTestHost(scenario);
+        host.Containers[ProviderTestNames.PostgresContainer] = ProviderTestContainerState.Running;
+        host.Containers[ProviderTestNames.SqlServerContainer] = ProviderTestContainerState.Running;
+        host.Containers["sql-server-developer"] = ProviderTestContainerState.Running;
+        host.Files[EnvFile] = FullEnvFile;
+        switch (scenario)
+        {
+            case "load-from-file":
+            case "unreachable":
+                if (scenario == "unreachable")
+                {
+                    host.Unreachable.Add("localhost:51433");
+                }
+
+                break;
+            case "adopt-set":
+                host.Variables[ProviderTestNames.PostgresVariable] = "Host=db.example;Port=6543;Password=" + PostgresSecret;
+                host.Variables[ProviderTestNames.SqlServerVariable] = "Server=tcp:sql.example,1444;Password=" + SqlServerSecret;
+                host.Files.Remove(EnvFile);
+                break;
+            case "file-missing":
+                host.Files.Remove(EnvFile);
+                break;
+            case "variable-missing-in-file":
+                host.Files[EnvFile] = ProviderTestNames.PostgresVariable + "=" + PostgresValue + "\n";
+                break;
+            case "stopped-container":
+                host.Containers[ProviderTestNames.PostgresContainer] = ProviderTestContainerState.Stopped;
+                break;
+            case "missing-container":
+                host.Containers.Remove(ProviderTestNames.SqlServerContainer);
+                break;
+            case "localdb-on-linux":
+                host.Variables[ProviderTestNames.SqlServerVariable] = "Server=(localdb)\\MSSQLLocalDB;Integrated Security=True";
+                break;
+            default:
+                throw new InvalidOperationException("Unknown scenario " + scenario);
+        }
+
+        return host;
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Runs one scenario against the provisioner and asserts its contract.</summary>
+    internal static void AssertScenario(string scenario, IProviderTestProvisioner provisioner)
+    {
+        var host = CreateHost(scenario);
+        var result = provisioner.Provision(EnvFile, host);
+        var text = string.Join("\n", result.Actions.Concat(result.Errors));
+
+        Assert.DoesNotContain(PostgresSecret, text, StringComparison.Ordinal);
+        Assert.DoesNotContain(SqlServerSecret, text, StringComparison.Ordinal);
+        Assert.All(host.ContainerCalls, name => Assert.StartsWith("qbrain-test-", name, StringComparison.Ordinal));
+
+        switch (scenario)
+        {
+            case "load-from-file":
+                Assert.True(result.Succeeded, string.Join("; ", result.Errors));
+                Assert.Equal(PostgresValue, host.Variables[ProviderTestNames.PostgresVariable]);
+                Assert.Equal(SqlServerValue, host.Variables[ProviderTestNames.SqlServerVariable]);
+                Assert.Empty(host.Started);
+                Assert.Contains("localhost:55432", host.Probes);
+                Assert.Contains("localhost:51433", host.Probes);
+                Assert.Contains("load " + ProviderTestNames.PostgresVariable + " from " + EnvFile, result.Actions);
+                Assert.Contains("load " + ProviderTestNames.SqlServerVariable + " from " + EnvFile, result.Actions);
+                break;
+            case "adopt-set":
+                Assert.True(result.Succeeded, string.Join("; ", result.Errors));
+                Assert.Equal(0, host.ReadFileCalls);
+                Assert.Empty(host.ContainerCalls);
+                Assert.Empty(host.SetCalls);
+                Assert.Contains("db.example:6543", host.Probes);
+                Assert.Contains("sql.example:1444", host.Probes);
+                Assert.Contains("adopt " + ProviderTestNames.PostgresVariable + " (already set)", result.Actions);
+                break;
+            case "file-missing":
+                Assert.False(result.Succeeded);
+                Assert.Contains(result.Errors, error => error.Contains(EnvFile, StringComparison.Ordinal));
+                Assert.Empty(host.SetCalls);
+                Assert.Empty(host.Probes);
+                break;
+            case "variable-missing-in-file":
+                Assert.False(result.Succeeded);
+                Assert.Contains(result.Errors, error => error.Contains(ProviderTestNames.SqlServerVariable, StringComparison.Ordinal) && error.Contains(EnvFile, StringComparison.Ordinal));
+                Assert.Equal(PostgresValue, host.Variables[ProviderTestNames.PostgresVariable]);
+                break;
+            case "stopped-container":
+                Assert.True(result.Succeeded, string.Join("; ", result.Errors));
+                Assert.Equal(new[] { ProviderTestNames.PostgresContainer }, host.Started);
+                Assert.Contains("start " + ProviderTestNames.PostgresContainer, result.Actions);
+                break;
+            case "missing-container":
+                Assert.False(result.Succeeded);
+                Assert.Contains(result.Errors, error => error.Contains(ProviderTestNames.SqlServerContainer, StringComparison.Ordinal));
+                Assert.DoesNotContain(ProviderTestNames.SqlServerContainer, host.Started);
+                break;
+            case "unreachable":
+                Assert.False(result.Succeeded);
+                Assert.Contains(result.Errors, error => error.Contains("localhost:51433", StringComparison.Ordinal));
+                break;
+            case "localdb-on-linux":
+                Assert.False(result.Succeeded);
+                Assert.Contains(result.Errors, error => error.Contains(ProviderTestNames.SqlServerVariable, StringComparison.Ordinal) && error.Contains("LocalDB", StringComparison.Ordinal));
+                break;
+            default:
+                throw new InvalidOperationException("Unknown scenario " + scenario);
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Recording fake of <see cref="IProviderTestHost"/>.</summary>
+    internal sealed class FakeProviderTestHost(string scenario) : IProviderTestHost
+    {
+        /// <summary>Scenario name (read by the scripted mock only).</summary>
+        public string Scenario { get; } = scenario;
+
+        /// <summary>Process environment.</summary>
+        public Dictionary<string, string> Variables { get; } = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);
+
+        /// <summary>Files by path.</summary>
+        public Dictionary<string, string> Files { get; } = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);
+
+        /// <summary>Container states by name.</summary>
+        public Dictionary<string, ProviderTestContainerState> Containers { get; } = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);
+
+        /// <summary>host:port endpoints that refuse connections.</summary>
+        public HashSet<string> Unreachable { get; } = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);
+
+        /// <summary>Variables set through the boundary.</summary>
+        public List<string> SetCalls { get; } = [];
+
+        /// <summary>Container names passed to GetContainerState or StartContainer.</summary>
+        public List<string> ContainerCalls { get; } = [];
+
+        /// <summary>Containers started.</summary>
+        public List<string> Started { get; } = [];
+
+        /// <summary>host:port endpoints probed.</summary>
+        public List<string> Probes { get; } = [];
+
+        /// <summary>Number of file reads.</summary>
+        public int ReadFileCalls { get; private set; }
+
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public string? GetVariable(string name) => Variables.TryGetValue(name, out var value) ? value : null;
+
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public void SetVariable(string name, string value)
+        {
+            SetCalls.Add(name);
+            Variables[name] = value;
+        }
+
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public string? ReadFile(string path)
+        {
+            ReadFileCalls++;
+            return Files.TryGetValue(path, out var text) ? text : null;
+        }
+
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public ProviderTestContainerState GetContainerState(string containerName)
+        {
+            ContainerCalls.Add(containerName);
+            return Containers.TryGetValue(containerName, out var state) ? state : ProviderTestContainerState.Missing;
+        }
+
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public bool StartContainer(string containerName)
+        {
+            ContainerCalls.Add(containerName);
+            if (!Containers.TryGetValue(containerName, out var state) || state == ProviderTestContainerState.Missing)
+            {
+                return false;
+            }
+
+            Started.Add(containerName);
+            Containers[containerName] = ProviderTestContainerState.Running;
+            return true;
+        }
+
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public bool CanConnect(string host, int port, TimeSpan timeout)
+        {
+            var endpoint = host + ":" + port.ToString(System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
+            Probes.Add(endpoint);
+            return !Unreachable.Contains(endpoint);
+        }
+    }
+}

+namespace NukeBuild.Tests;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-018, BDPv4 mock gate: proves the <see cref="ProviderTestProvisioningContract"/>
+/// assertions accept a scripted provisioner that produces the contract-derived effects, and reject deliberately wrong
+/// provisioners (negative controls), before any production provisioning code is written.
+/// </summary>
+public sealed class ProviderTestProvisioningMockGateTests
+{
+    /// <summary>TR-MCP-NUKE-018: every scenario passes against the scripted provisioner.</summary>
+    /// <param name="scenario">Scenario name from <see cref="ProviderTestProvisioningContract.ScenarioNames"/>.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("load-from-file")]
+    [InlineData("adopt-set")]
+    [InlineData("file-missing")]
+    [InlineData("variable-missing-in-file")]
+    [InlineData("stopped-container")]
+    [InlineData("missing-container")]
+    [InlineData("unreachable")]
+    [InlineData("localdb-on-linux")]
+    public void ScriptedProvisioner_PassesScenario(string scenario)
+        => ProviderTestProvisioningContract.AssertScenario(scenario, new ScriptedProvisioner(Fault.None));
+
+    /// <summary>TR-MCP-NUKE-018: the theory above covers every contract scenario.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ScriptedProvisionerTheory_CoversEveryScenario()
+        => Assert.Equal(8, ProviderTestProvisioningContract.ScenarioNames.Length);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// TR-MCP-NUKE-018 negative controls: each wrong provisioner is rejected by the scenario it violates
+    /// (a value leak (AC-4), a foreign container, a file read although set, a missing file ignored,
+    /// a probe skipped).
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="scenario">Scenario the fault violates.</param>
+    /// <param name="fault">Injected fault.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("load-from-file", Fault.LeaksValue)]
+    [InlineData("load-from-file", Fault.TouchesOtherContainer)]
+    [InlineData("adopt-set", Fault.ReadsFileWhenSet)]
+    [InlineData("file-missing", Fault.IgnoresMissingFile)]
+    [InlineData("unreachable", Fault.SkipsProbe)]
+    public void WrongProvisioner_IsRejected(string scenario, Fault fault)
+        => Assert.ThrowsAny<Xunit.Sdk.XunitException>(
+            () => ProviderTestProvisioningContract.AssertScenario(scenario, new ScriptedProvisioner(fault)));
+
+    /// <summary>Faults injected into the scripted provisioner for negative controls.</summary>
+    public enum Fault
+    {
+        /// <summary>No fault: the contract-derived behavior.</summary>
+        None,
+
+        /// <summary>Writes a connection-string value into an action.</summary>
+        LeaksValue,
+
+        /// <summary>Inspects a container other than the two qbrain-test containers.</summary>
+        TouchesOtherContainer,
+
+        /// <summary>Reads the env file although both variables are set.</summary>
+        ReadsFileWhenSet,
+
+        /// <summary>Reports no error when the env file is missing.</summary>
+        IgnoresMissingFile,
+
+        /// <summary>Reports success without probing the endpoints.</summary>
+        SkipsProbe,
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Scripted mock: produces the expected effects of the fake host's scenario, with one optional fault.</summary>
+    private sealed class ScriptedProvisioner(Fault fault) : IProviderTestProvisioner
+    {
+        public ProviderTestProvisioningResult Provision(string envFilePath, IProviderTestHost host)
+        {
+            var fake = (ProviderTestProvisioningContract.FakeProviderTestHost)host;
+            var actions = new List<string>();
+            var errors = new List<string>();
+            const string pg = ProviderTestNames.PostgresVariable;
+            const string sql = ProviderTestNames.SqlServerVariable;
+            switch (fake.Scenario)
+            {
+                case "load-from-file":
+                case "unreachable":
+                case "stopped-container":
+                    host.ReadFile(envFilePath);
+                    host.SetVariable(pg, ProviderTestContractValues.Postgres);
+                    host.SetVariable(sql, ProviderTestContractValues.SqlServer);
+                    actions.Add("load " + pg + " from " + envFilePath);
+                    actions.Add("load " + sql + " from " + envFilePath);
+                    if (fake.Scenario == "stopped-container" && host.GetContainerState(ProviderTestNames.PostgresContainer) == ProviderTestContainerState.Stopped)
+                    {
+                        host.StartContainer(ProviderTestNames.PostgresContainer);
+                        actions.Add("start " + ProviderTestNames.PostgresContainer);
+                    }
+
+                    if (fault == Fault.TouchesOtherContainer)
+                    {
+                        host.GetContainerState("sql-server-developer");
+                    }
+
+                    if (fault == Fault.LeaksValue)
+                    {
+                        actions.Add("loaded value " + ProviderTestContractValues.Postgres);
+                    }
+
+                    if (fault != Fault.SkipsProbe)
+                    {
+                        var pgUp = host.CanConnect("localhost", 55432, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1));
+                        var sqlUp = host.CanConnect("localhost", 51433, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1));
+                        if (!pgUp)
+                        {
+                            errors.Add("endpoint localhost:55432 for " + pg + " is not accepting connections");
+                        }
+
+                        if (!sqlUp)
+                        {
+                            errors.Add("endpoint localhost:51433 for " + sql + " is not accepting connections");
+                        }
+                    }
+
+                    break;
+                case "adopt-set":
+                    if (fault == Fault.ReadsFileWhenSet)
+                    {
+                        host.ReadFile(envFilePath);
+                    }
+
+                    actions.Add("adopt " + pg + " (already set)");
+                    actions.Add("adopt " + sql + " (already set)");
+                    host.CanConnect("db.example", 6543, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1));
+                    host.CanConnect("sql.example", 1444, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1));
+                    break;
+                case "file-missing":
+                    host.ReadFile(envFilePath);
+                    if (fault != Fault.IgnoresMissingFile)
+                    {
+                        errors.Add("test-database env file not found: " + envFilePath);
+                    }
+
+                    break;
+                case "variable-missing-in-file":
+                    host.ReadFile(envFilePath);
+                    host.SetVariable(pg, ProviderTestContractValues.Postgres);
+                    errors.Add(sql + " is not defined in " + envFilePath);
+                    break;
+                case "missing-container":
+                    host.ReadFile(envFilePath);
+                    host.SetVariable(pg, ProviderTestContractValues.Postgres);
+                    host.SetVariable(sql, ProviderTestContractValues.SqlServer);
+                    host.GetContainerState(ProviderTestNames.SqlServerContainer);
+                    errors.Add("container " + ProviderTestNames.SqlServerContainer + " not found");
+                    break;
+                case "localdb-on-linux":
+                    host.ReadFile(envFilePath);
+                    host.SetVariable(pg, ProviderTestContractValues.Postgres);
+                    errors.Add(sql + " names a LocalDB server, which has no TCP endpoint on Linux");
+                    break;
+                default:
+                    throw new InvalidOperationException("Unknown scenario " + fake.Scenario);
+            }
+
+            return new ProviderTestProvisioningResult(actions, errors);
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Contract values the scripted mock sets.</summary>
+    private static class ProviderTestContractValues
+    {
+        public const string Postgres = ProviderTestProvisioningContract.PostgresValue;
+        public const string SqlServer = ProviderTestProvisioningContract.SqlServerValue;
+    }
+}

diff --git a/tests/Build.Tests/ProvisioningDownloadContract.cs b/tests/Build.Tests/ProvisioningDownloadContract.cs
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..8652eaf1
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/Build.Tests/ProvisioningDownloadContract.cs

+using System.Net;
+
+namespace NukeBuild.Tests;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019: Contract cases and assertions for <see cref="IProvisioningDownload"/>. The expected
+/// behavior comes from the acceptance criterion: the body is written to the destination as it arrives, the download
+/// has no limit on its total duration, and it fails naming the URL on an error status or when no data arrives for the
+/// inactivity limit. Every case runs through a scripted <see cref="HttpMessageHandler"/> in memory (no network) and
+/// writes under a temporary folder it removes. The same assertions run against the scripted download (BDPv4 mock
+/// gate), wrong downloads (negative controls), and the production download.
+/// </summary>
+/// <remarks>
+/// The timed cases keep wide margins so machine load cannot flip them: chunks arrive every 100 ms against a 1 s
+/// inactivity limit, the steady transfer runs about 2.5 s (two and a half inactivity limits), and a download that does
+/// not fail a stalled transfer is cut off by the harness after 10 s. The client check catches a client that keeps
+/// HttpClient's default 100 s total limit, which no short transfer could reveal.
+/// </remarks>
+internal static class ProvisioningDownloadContract
+{
+    /// <summary>The URL every case downloads.</summary>
+    internal static readonly Uri Url = new("https://downloads.example.test/provisioning/release-asset.bin");
+
+    /// <summary>The inactivity limit every timed case passes.</summary>
+    internal static readonly TimeSpan InactivityLimit = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1);
+
+    /// <summary>How long the harness waits for a stalled download to fail before it rejects the download.</summary>
+    internal static readonly TimeSpan StallCutoff = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10);
+
+    /// <summary>Asserts every contract case against <paramref name="download"/>.</summary>
+    /// <param name="download">Download under test.</param>
+    internal static async Task AssertDownloadAsync(IProvisioningDownload download)
+    {
+        AssertClientHasNoTotalLimit(download);
+        await AssertWritesAsItArrivesAsync(download).ConfigureAwait(true);
+        await AssertSteadySlowTransferCompletesAsync(download).ConfigureAwait(true);
+        await AssertStalledTransferFailsNamingUrlAsync(download).ConfigureAwait(true);
+        await AssertErrorStatusFailsNamingUrlAsync(download).ConfigureAwait(true);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>The client a download creates has no total time limit.</summary>
+    internal static void AssertClientHasNoTotalLimit(IProvisioningDownload download)
+    {
+        using var handler = new ScriptedHandler(HttpStatusCode.OK, () => new ChunkStream(1, TimeSpan.Zero, null));
+        using var client = download.CreateClient(handler);
+        Assert.Equal(Timeout.InfiniteTimeSpan, client.Timeout);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Eight 64 KB chunks: the destination holds every byte, and part of it was on disk before the body ended.</summary>
+    internal static async Task AssertWritesAsItArrivesAsync(IProvisioningDownload download)
+    {
+        using var folder = new TemporaryFolder();
+        var destination = Path.Combine(folder.Path, "asset.bin");
+        ChunkStream? stream = null;
+        using var handler = new ScriptedHandler(HttpStatusCode.OK, () => stream = new ChunkStream(8, TimeSpan.Zero, destination));
+
+        await AssertCompletesAsync(download, handler, destination).ConfigureAwait(true);
+
+        Assert.NotNull(stream);
+        Assert.Equal(ChunkStream.Expected(8), await File.ReadAllBytesAsync(destination).ConfigureAwait(true));
+        Assert.True(stream!.SawPartialFileBeforeEnd, "The body was not written to the destination as it arrived.");
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>25 chunks 100 ms apart (about 2.5 inactivity limits in all) download to completion.</summary>
+    internal static async Task AssertSteadySlowTransferCompletesAsync(IProvisioningDownload download)
+    {
+        using var folder = new TemporaryFolder();
+        var destination = Path.Combine(folder.Path, "asset.bin");
+        using var handler = new ScriptedHandler(HttpStatusCode.OK, () => new ChunkStream(25, TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(100), null));
+
+        await AssertCompletesAsync(download, handler, destination).ConfigureAwait(true);
+
+        Assert.Equal(ChunkStream.Expected(25), await File.ReadAllBytesAsync(destination).ConfigureAwait(true));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>A download that must complete: any exception it throws fails the contract with that exception's text.</summary>
+    private static async Task AssertCompletesAsync(IProvisioningDownload download, HttpMessageHandler handler, string destination)
+    {
+        var failure = await Record.ExceptionAsync(
+            () => download.DownloadAsync(handler, Url, destination, InactivityLimit, CancellationToken.None)).ConfigureAwait(true);
+        Assert.True(failure is null, $"The download did not complete: {failure?.GetType().Name}: {failure?.Message}");
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>One chunk, then no data: the download fails within the cutoff with the URL in the message.</summary>
+    internal static async Task AssertStalledTransferFailsNamingUrlAsync(IProvisioningDownload download)
+    {
+        using var folder = new TemporaryFolder();
+        var destination = Path.Combine(folder.Path, "asset.bin");
+        using var handler = new ScriptedHandler(HttpStatusCode.OK, () => new ChunkStream(1, TimeSpan.Zero, null, stallAfter: true));
+        using var cutoff = new CancellationTokenSource(StallCutoff);
+
+        var attempt = download.DownloadAsync(handler, Url, destination, InactivityLimit, cutoff.Token);
+        var failure = await Record.ExceptionAsync(() => attempt).ConfigureAwait(true);
+
+        Assert.NotNull(failure);
+        Assert.False(cutoff.IsCancellationRequested, "A stalled transfer did not fail within the inactivity limit.");
+        Assert.Contains(Url.ToString(), failure!.Message, StringComparison.Ordinal);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>A 404 answer fails the download with the URL in the message.</summary>
+    internal static async Task AssertErrorStatusFailsNamingUrlAsync(IProvisioningDownload download)
+    {
+        using var folder = new TemporaryFolder();
+        var destination = Path.Combine(folder.Path, "asset.bin");
+        using var handler = new ScriptedHandler(HttpStatusCode.NotFound, () => new ChunkStream(1, TimeSpan.Zero, null));
+
+        var failure = await Record.ExceptionAsync(
+            () => download.DownloadAsync(handler, Url, destination, InactivityLimit, CancellationToken.None)).ConfigureAwait(true);
+
+        Assert.NotNull(failure);
+        Assert.Contains(Url.ToString(), failure!.Message, StringComparison.Ordinal);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Answers every request with the status and a fresh body stream.</summary>
+    internal sealed class ScriptedHandler(HttpStatusCode status, Func<Stream> body) : HttpMessageHandler
+    {
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        protected override Task<HttpResponseMessage> SendAsync(HttpRequestMessage request, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
+            => Task.FromResult(new HttpResponseMessage(status) { Content = new StreamContent(body()), RequestMessage = request });
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// A body of 64 KB chunks with an optional delay before each chunk and an optional stall after the last one. When
+    /// given the destination path, it records whether the file already held data when a later chunk was read.
+    /// </summary>
+    internal sealed class ChunkStream(int chunks, TimeSpan delay, string? destination, bool stallAfter = false) : Stream
+    {
+        /// <summary>The size of each chunk.</summary>
+        internal const int ChunkSize = 64 * 1024;
+
+        private int _chunk;
+        private int _offset;
+
+        /// <summary>Gets whether the destination already held data when a later chunk was read.</summary>
+        internal bool SawPartialFileBeforeEnd { get; private set; }
+
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public override bool CanRead => true;
+
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public override bool CanSeek => false;
+
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public override bool CanWrite => false;
+
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public override long Length => throw new NotSupportedException();
+
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public override long Position
+        {
+            get => throw new NotSupportedException();
+            set => throw new NotSupportedException();
+        }
+
+        /// <summary>The bytes the body holds for <paramref name="chunks"/> chunks.</summary>
+        internal static byte[] Expected(int chunks)
+        {
+            var bytes = new byte[chunks * ChunkSize];
+            for (var i = 0; i < bytes.Length; i++)
+                bytes[i] = (byte)((i / ChunkSize) + 1);
+            return bytes;
+        }
+
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public override int Read(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count)
+            => ReadAsync(buffer.AsMemory(offset, count)).AsTask().GetAwaiter().GetResult();
+
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public override async ValueTask<int> ReadAsync(Memory<byte> buffer, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
+        {
+            if (_chunk >= chunks)
+            {
+                if (stallAfter)
+                    await Task.Delay(Timeout.Infinite, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);
+                return 0;
+            }
+
+            if (_offset == 0)
+            {
+                if (_chunk >= 2 && destination is not null && File.Exists(destination) && new FileInfo(destination).Length > 0)
+                    SawPartialFileBeforeEnd = true;
+                if (delay > TimeSpan.Zero)
+                    await Task.Delay(delay, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);
+            }
+
+            var count = Math.Min(buffer.Length, ChunkSize - _offset);
+            buffer.Span[..count].Fill((byte)(_chunk + 1));
+            _offset += count;
+            if (_offset == ChunkSize)
+            {
+                _offset = 0;
+                _chunk++;
+            }
+
+            return count;
+        }
+
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public override void Flush()
+        {
+        }
+
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public override long Seek(long offset, SeekOrigin origin) => throw new NotSupportedException();
+
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public override void SetLength(long value) => throw new NotSupportedException();
+
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public override void Write(byte[] buffer, int offset, int count) => throw new NotSupportedException();
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>A temporary folder removed on dispose.</summary>
+    private sealed class TemporaryFolder : IDisposable
+    {
+        public TemporaryFolder()
+        {
+            Path = System.IO.Path.Combine(System.IO.Path.GetTempPath(), "provisioning-download-" + Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));
+            Directory.CreateDirectory(Path);
+        }
+
+        public string Path { get; }
+
+        public void Dispose()
+        {
+            if (Directory.Exists(Path))
+                Directory.Delete(Path, recursive: true);
+        }
+    }
+}
diff --git a/tests/Build.Tests/ProvisioningDownloadMockGateTests.cs b/tests/Build.Tests/ProvisioningDownloadMockGateTests.cs
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..268e11ce
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/Build.Tests/ProvisioningDownloadMockGateTests.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,106 @@
+namespace NukeBuild.Tests;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019, BDPv4 mock gate: proves the <see cref="ProvisioningDownloadContract"/> assertions accept a
+/// scripted download that streams to disk with no total limit and an inactivity limit, and reject deliberately wrong
+/// downloads (negative controls), before any production download code is written. Every case runs in memory through
+/// a scripted message handler; no network is used.
+/// </summary>
+public sealed class ProvisioningDownloadMockGateTests
+{
+    /// <summary>TR-MCP-NUKE-019: the scripted download passes every contract case.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public async Task ScriptedDownload_PassesContract()
+        => await ProvisioningDownloadContract.AssertDownloadAsync(new ScriptedDownload(Fault.None)).ConfigureAwait(true);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// TR-MCP-NUKE-019 negative controls, each rejected inside this passing test: a download that buffers the whole
+    /// body before writing, a client with HttpClient's default 100 s total limit, an inactivity deadline that is never
+    /// reset, no inactivity limit at all, and failures that do not name the URL.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="fault">Injected fault.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData(Fault.BuffersWholeBody)]
+    [InlineData(Fault.DefaultClientTimeout)]
+    [InlineData(Fault.DeadlineNotReset)]
+    [InlineData(Fault.NoInactivityLimit)]
+    [InlineData(Fault.UnnamedFailure)]
+    public async Task WrongDownload_IsRejected(Fault fault)
+        => await Assert.ThrowsAnyAsync<Xunit.Sdk.XunitException>(
+            () => ProvisioningDownloadContract.AssertDownloadAsync(new ScriptedDownload(fault))).ConfigureAwait(true);
+
+    /// <summary>Faults injected into the scripted download for negative controls.</summary>
+    public enum Fault
+    {
+        /// <summary>No fault: streams to disk, no total limit, the inactivity limit reset on every read.</summary>
+        None,
+
+        /// <summary>Reads the whole body into memory (HttpClient's default completion) before writing the file.</summary>
+        BuffersWholeBody,
+
+        /// <summary>Keeps HttpClient's default 100 s total limit.</summary>
+        DefaultClientTimeout,
+
+        /// <summary>Sets the inactivity deadline once for the whole transfer instead of after every read.</summary>
+        DeadlineNotReset,
+
+        /// <summary>Has no inactivity limit, so a stalled transfer waits forever.</summary>
+        NoInactivityLimit,
+
+        /// <summary>Fails without the URL in the message.</summary>
+        UnnamedFailure,
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Scripted download: the contract-derived behavior, with an optional injected fault.</summary>
+    private sealed class ScriptedDownload(Fault fault) : IProvisioningDownload
+    {
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public HttpClient CreateClient(HttpMessageHandler handler)
+            => fault == Fault.DefaultClientTimeout
+                ? new HttpClient(handler, disposeHandler: false)
+                : new HttpClient(handler, disposeHandler: false) { Timeout = Timeout.InfiniteTimeSpan };
+
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public async Task DownloadAsync(
+            HttpMessageHandler handler,
+            Uri url,
+            string destinationPath,
+            TimeSpan inactivityLimit,
+            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
+        {
+            using var client = CreateClient(handler);
+            using var inactivity = CancellationTokenSource.CreateLinkedTokenSource(cancellationToken);
+            var limited = fault != Fault.NoInactivityLimit;
+            if (limited)
+                inactivity.CancelAfter(inactivityLimit);
+            try
+            {
+                var completion = fault == Fault.BuffersWholeBody
+                    ? HttpCompletionOption.ResponseContentRead
+                    : HttpCompletionOption.ResponseHeadersRead;
+                using var response = await client.GetAsync(url, completion, inactivity.Token).ConfigureAwait(false);
+                if (fault == Fault.UnnamedFailure)
+                    response.EnsureSuccessStatusCode();
+                else if (!response.IsSuccessStatusCode)
+                    throw new HttpRequestException($"Download of {url} failed: the server answered {(int)response.StatusCode}.");
+
+                await using var source = await response.Content.ReadAsStreamAsync(inactivity.Token).ConfigureAwait(false);
+                await using var target = new FileStream(destinationPath, FileMode.Create, FileAccess.Write, FileShare.None, bufferSize: 1, useAsync: true);
+                var buffer = new byte[81920];
+                while (true)
+                {
+                    if (limited && fault != Fault.DeadlineNotReset)
+                        inactivity.CancelAfter(inactivityLimit);
+                    var read = await source.ReadAsync(buffer, inactivity.Token).ConfigureAwait(false);
+                    if (read == 0)
+                        break;
+                    await target.WriteAsync(buffer.AsMemory(0, read), cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);
+                }
+            }
+            catch (OperationCanceledException) when (inactivity.IsCancellationRequested && !cancellationToken.IsCancellationRequested && fault != Fault.UnnamedFailure)
+            {
+                throw new TimeoutException($"Download of {url} stopped: no data arrived for {inactivityLimit}.");
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}

+{

+    /// An in-memory configuration supplies the legacy section only, with a host-native workspace path.
+    /// </summary>

+            ["McpServer:McpAgent:ApiKey"] = "legacy-token",
+            ["McpServer:McpAgent:WorkspacePath"] = workspacePath,
+        }).Build();

+        services.AddSingleton<IConfiguration>(configuration);
+        services.AddQBrainAiMcpAgent();
+
+        using var provider = services.BuildServiceProvider();
+        var options = provider.GetRequiredService<IOptions<McpAgentOptions>>().Value;
+
+        Assert.Equal("legacy-token", options.ApiKey);
+        Assert.Equal(workspacePath, options.WorkspacePath);

diff --git a/tests/QBrainAi.Repl.Core.Tests/Host/MarkerWalkContract.cs b/tests/QBrainAi.Repl.Core.Tests/Host/MarkerWalkContract.cs
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..4b840673

+using QBrainAi.Repl.Host;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Repl.Core.Tests.Host;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-REPL-008 (AC-1 to AC-4): Contract cases and assertions for the marker walk and its diagnostics, one named
+/// case per criterion. The expected values come from the criteria: the walk goes from the start folder up to the file
+/// system root, the nearest marker on the way up wins, walking up to a parent workspace is intended, and a walk that
+/// finds nothing lists every folder it searched in walk order. Every case runs over an in-memory file system rooted at
+/// the root of the host's temporary folder path (only the path string is used; no folder is read), so no case depends
+/// on what the host holds. The same assertions run against a scripted walk (BDPv4 mock gate), wrong walks (negative
+/// controls), and the production resolver.
+/// </summary>
+internal static class MarkerWalkContract
+{
+    /// <summary>The marker file name the walk looks for.</summary>
+    internal const string MarkerFileName = "AGENTS-README-FIRST.yaml";
+
+    /// <summary>The file system root of the scripted tree.</summary>
+    internal static readonly string Root = Path.GetPathRoot(Path.GetTempPath())!;
+
+    /// <summary>The scripted workspace folder under the root.</summary>
+    internal static readonly string Workspace = Path.Combine(Root, "w");
+
+    /// <summary>The scripted source folder under the workspace.</summary>
+    internal static readonly string Source = Path.Combine(Workspace, "src");
+
+    /// <summary>The folder every case starts its walk from.</summary>
+    internal static readonly string Start = Path.Combine(Source, "nested");
+
+    /// <summary>A marker walk: the marker found from <paramref name="startPath"/>, or null, and the folders searched.</summary>
+    internal delegate string? Walk(string startPath, IMarkerFileSystem fileSystem, out IReadOnlyList<string> searched);
+
+    /// <summary>A diagnosed resolution: whether it succeeded and, when it did not, its error.</summary>
+    internal delegate bool Diagnose(string startPath, IMarkerFileSystem fileSystem, out string error);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// AC-1 FindMarkerFile_NoMarkerOnTheWayUp_ListsEveryFolderToTheRoot: no marker anywhere, so no marker is returned
+    /// and the searched folders are the start, its parents, and the root, in that order.
+    /// </summary>
+    internal static void AssertNoMarkerOnTheWayUp(Walk walk)
+    {
+        var marker = walk(Start, new InMemoryMarkerFileSystem(), out var searched);
+
+        Assert.Null(marker);
+        Assert.Equal([Start, Source, Workspace, Root], searched);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// AC-2 FindMarkerFile_MarkerInParent_IsFound: a marker only in the workspace folder is found, as intended, and the
+    /// walk stops there.
+    /// </summary>
+    internal static void AssertMarkerInParentIsFound(Walk walk)
+    {
+        var marker = walk(Start, new InMemoryMarkerFileSystem(Workspace), out var searched);
+
+        Assert.Equal(Path.Combine(Workspace, MarkerFileName), marker);
+        Assert.Equal([Start, Source, Workspace], searched);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// AC-3 FindMarkerFile_MarkersInStartAndParent_NearestWins: with markers in the start folder and the workspace
+    /// folder, the start folder's marker is returned and only the start folder is searched.
+    /// </summary>
+    internal static void AssertNearestMarkerWins(Walk walk)
+    {
+        var marker = walk(Start, new InMemoryMarkerFileSystem(Start, Workspace), out var searched);
+
+        Assert.Equal(Path.Combine(Start, MarkerFileName), marker);
+        Assert.Equal([Start], searched);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// AC-4 TryResolveWithDiagnostics_NoMarkerOnTheWayUp_NamesEveryFolderSearched: with no marker anywhere the
+    /// resolution fails, and its error names the marker file and every folder searched.
+    /// </summary>
+    internal static void AssertDiagnosticsNameEveryFolder(Diagnose diagnose)
+    {
+        var ok = diagnose(Start, new InMemoryMarkerFileSystem(), out var error);
+
+        Assert.False(ok);
+        Assert.Contains(MarkerFileName, error, StringComparison.Ordinal);
+        foreach (var folder in new[] { Start, Source, Workspace, Root })
+            Assert.Contains(folder, error, StringComparison.Ordinal);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Asserts the three walk cases (AC-1 to AC-3) against <paramref name="walk"/>.</summary>
+    internal static void AssertWalk(Walk walk)
+    {
+        AssertNoMarkerOnTheWayUp(walk);
+        AssertMarkerInParentIsFound(walk);
+        AssertNearestMarkerWins(walk);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>An in-memory file system holding a marker in each named folder and nothing else.</summary>
+    internal sealed class InMemoryMarkerFileSystem(params string[] markerFolders) : IMarkerFileSystem
+    {
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public bool FileExists(string path)
+            => markerFolders.Any(folder => string.Equals(Path.Combine(folder, MarkerFileName), path, StringComparison.Ordinal));
+
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public string[] ReadAllLines(string path)
+            => throw new FileNotFoundException("The in-memory file system holds marker presence only; no case reads a marker.", path);
+    }
+}
diff --git a/tests/QBrainAi.Repl.Core.Tests/Host/MarkerWalkMockGateTests.cs b/tests/QBrainAi.Repl.Core.Tests/Host/MarkerWalkMockGateTests.cs
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..49733ab2

+++ b/tests/QBrainAi.Repl.Core.Tests/Host/MarkerWalkMockGateTests.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,137 @@
+using QBrainAi.Repl.Host;
+using Xunit.Sdk;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Repl.Core.Tests.Host;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-REPL-008 (AC-1 to AC-4), BDPv4 mock gate: proves the <see cref="MarkerWalkContract"/> assertions accept a
+/// scripted walk and a scripted diagnosis that follow the criteria, and reject deliberately wrong ones (negative
+/// controls), before the resolver reads through <see cref="IMarkerFileSystem"/>. Every case runs over an in-memory
+/// file system; no host folder is read.
+/// </summary>
+public sealed class MarkerWalkMockGateTests
+{
+    /// <summary>TR-MCP-REPL-008 AC-1 to AC-3: the scripted walk passes every walk case.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ScriptedWalk_PassesContract()
+        => MarkerWalkContract.AssertWalk(ScriptedWalk(WalkFault.None));
+
+    /// <summary>TR-MCP-REPL-008 AC-4: the scripted diagnosis passes the diagnostics case.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ScriptedDiagnosis_PassesContract()
+        => MarkerWalkContract.AssertDiagnosticsNameEveryFolder(ScriptedDiagnosis(DiagnosisFault.None));
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// TR-MCP-REPL-008 AC-1 to AC-3 negative controls, each rejected inside this passing test: a walk that never
+    /// leaves the start folder, one that skips the start folder, one where the farthest marker wins, one that stops
+    /// before the root, and one that reports the searched folders in reverse.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="fault">Injected fault.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData(WalkFault.StartOnly)]
+    [InlineData(WalkFault.SkipsStart)]
+    [InlineData(WalkFault.FarthestWins)]
+    [InlineData(WalkFault.StopsBeforeRoot)]
+    [InlineData(WalkFault.ReversedOrder)]
+    public void WrongWalk_IsRejected(WalkFault fault)
+        => Assert.ThrowsAny<XunitException>(() => MarkerWalkContract.AssertWalk(ScriptedWalk(fault)));
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// TR-MCP-REPL-008 AC-4 negative controls, each rejected inside this passing test: an error that omits the
+    /// searched folders, one that omits the marker file name, and a diagnosis that reports success.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="fault">Injected fault.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData(DiagnosisFault.OmitsSearchedFolders)]
+    [InlineData(DiagnosisFault.OmitsMarkerName)]
+    [InlineData(DiagnosisFault.ReportsSuccess)]
+    public void WrongDiagnosis_IsRejected(DiagnosisFault fault)
+        => Assert.ThrowsAny<XunitException>(
+            () => MarkerWalkContract.AssertDiagnosticsNameEveryFolder(ScriptedDiagnosis(fault)));
+
+    /// <summary>Faults injected into the scripted walk.</summary>
+    public enum WalkFault
+    {
+        /// <summary>No fault: the walk the criteria describe.</summary>
+        None,
+
+        /// <summary>Checks only the start folder.</summary>
+        StartOnly,
+
+        /// <summary>Starts at the start folder's parent.</summary>
+        SkipsStart,
+
+        /// <summary>Keeps walking after a marker and returns the farthest one.</summary>
+        FarthestWins,
+
+        /// <summary>Stops one folder below the root.</summary>
+        StopsBeforeRoot,
+
+        /// <summary>Reports the searched folders root first.</summary>
+        ReversedOrder,
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Faults injected into the scripted diagnosis.</summary>
+    public enum DiagnosisFault
+    {
+        /// <summary>No fault: the diagnosis the criteria describe.</summary>
+        None,
+
+        /// <summary>The error does not list the searched folders.</summary>
+        OmitsSearchedFolders,
+
+        /// <summary>The error does not name the marker file.</summary>
+        OmitsMarkerName,
+
+        /// <summary>Reports success with no marker.</summary>
+        ReportsSuccess,
+    }
+
+    private static MarkerWalkContract.Walk ScriptedWalk(WalkFault fault)
+        => (string startPath, IMarkerFileSystem fileSystem, out IReadOnlyList<string> searched) =>
+        {
+            var folders = new List<string>();
+            string? found = null;
+            var current = new DirectoryInfo(startPath);
+            if (fault == WalkFault.SkipsStart)
+                current = current.Parent;
+            while (current is not null)
+            {
+                if (fault == WalkFault.StopsBeforeRoot && current.Parent is null)
+                    break;
+                folders.Add(current.FullName);
+                var candidate = Path.Combine(current.FullName, MarkerWalkContract.MarkerFileName);
+                if (fileSystem.FileExists(candidate))
+                {
+                    found = candidate;
+                    if (fault != WalkFault.FarthestWins)
+                        break;
+                }
+
+                if (fault == WalkFault.StartOnly)
+                    break;
+                current = current.Parent;
+            }
+
+            if (fault == WalkFault.ReversedOrder)
+                folders.Reverse();
+            searched = folders;
+            return found;
+        };
+
+    private static MarkerWalkContract.Diagnose ScriptedDiagnosis(DiagnosisFault fault)
+        => (string startPath, IMarkerFileSystem fileSystem, out string error) =>
+        {
+            var marker = ScriptedWalk(WalkFault.None)(startPath, fileSystem, out var searched);
+            if (marker is not null || fault == DiagnosisFault.ReportsSuccess)
+            {
+                error = string.Empty;
+                return true;
+            }
+
+            var name = fault == DiagnosisFault.OmitsMarkerName ? "the marker file" : $"'{MarkerWalkContract.MarkerFileName}'";
+            var list = fault == DiagnosisFault.OmitsSearchedFolders ? "the workspace folders" : string.Join("; ", searched);
+            error = $"Marker file {name} not found. Searched: {list}.";
+            return false;
+        };
+}

+using QBrainAi.Repl.Host;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Repl.Core.Tests.Host;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-REPL-008 (AC-1 to AC-4): Binds the <see cref="MarkerWalkContract"/> cases and assertions, unchanged from the
+/// BDPv4 mock gate, to the production <see cref="MarkerFileClientOptionsResolver"/>, one test per named case, over an
+/// in-memory file system. No host folder is read, so the cases hold on every host whatever folders sit above its
+/// temporary folder.
+/// </summary>
+public sealed class MarkerWalkTests
+{
+    /// <summary>TR-MCP-REPL-008 AC-1: no marker on the way up; every folder to the root is listed in walk order.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void FindMarkerFile_NoMarkerOnTheWayUp_ListsEveryFolderToTheRoot()
+        => MarkerWalkContract.AssertNoMarkerOnTheWayUp(ProductionWalk);
+
+    /// <summary>TR-MCP-REPL-008 AC-2: a marker only in a parent folder is found, as intended.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void FindMarkerFile_MarkerInParent_IsFound()
+        => MarkerWalkContract.AssertMarkerInParentIsFound(ProductionWalk);
+
+    /// <summary>TR-MCP-REPL-008 AC-3: with markers in the start folder and a parent, the nearest wins.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void FindMarkerFile_MarkersInStartAndParent_NearestWins()
+        => MarkerWalkContract.AssertNearestMarkerWins(ProductionWalk);
+
+    /// <summary>TR-MCP-REPL-008 AC-4: with no marker on the way up, the error names the marker file and every folder.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void TryResolveWithDiagnostics_NoMarkerOnTheWayUp_NamesEveryFolderSearched()
+        => MarkerWalkContract.AssertDiagnosticsNameEveryFolder(ProductionDiagnosis);
+
+    private static string? ProductionWalk(string startPath, IMarkerFileSystem fileSystem, out IReadOnlyList<string> searched)
+        => MarkerFileClientOptionsResolver.FindMarkerFile(startPath, out searched, fileSystem);
+
+    private static bool ProductionDiagnosis(string startPath, IMarkerFileSystem fileSystem, out string error)
+        => MarkerFileClientOptionsResolver.TryResolveWithDiagnostics(startPath, null, out _, out error, agent: null, fileSystem);
+}

diff --git a/tests/QBrainAi.Repl.Core.Tests/ReplCommandTimeoutCallSiteTests.cs b/tests/QBrainAi.Repl.Core.Tests/ReplCommandTimeoutCallSiteTests.cs
new file mode 100644

@@ -0,0 +1,344 @@
+// TR-MCP-REPL-001 AC-002: the two call sites that apply a configured REPL command timeout
+
+using System.Globalization;
+using System.Text;
+using NSubstitute;
+using Xunit;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Repl.Core.Tests;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Tests the two places that apply a configured REPL command timeout: the agent stdio protocol, which reads
+/// MCPSERVER_REPL_COMMAND_TIMEOUT_SECONDS for each request, and the TODO stream commands of the dispatcher, which read
+/// MCPSERVER_REPL_STREAM_COMMAND_TIMEOUT_SECONDS for each stream. Each call site reads only its own variable, once, and
+/// applies the one parse rule bound in <see cref="ReplCommandTimeoutTests"/>: a value the rule refuses never fails the
+/// request, and the timeout message names the timeout that applied.
+/// </summary>
+/// <remarks>
+/// Requirement coverage: TR-MCP-REPL-001 AC-002 (round 8 self-evaluation: the parse rule was bound, but neither call
+/// site was, so the message clause and the call-site no-failure clause had no test), and through it
+/// TR-MCP-USECASE-018 AC-006 and TR-MCP-USECASE-019 AC-008. Test data: contract rows, each a pair of scripted variable
+/// values given through a reader that records every name it is asked for (never the process environment) and a command
+/// that either answers at once or runs until cancelled, with the outcome the call site must give. The contract is proved
+/// on a scripted reference call site, with wrong call sites rejected inside a passing harness, before it is bound to
+/// the production call sites.
+/// </remarks>
+public sealed class ReplCommandTimeoutCallSiteTests
+{
+    /// <summary>The variable the agent stdio protocol reads for each request.</summary>
+    internal const string CommandVariable = "MCPSERVER_REPL_COMMAND_TIMEOUT_SECONDS";
+
+    /// <summary>The variable the TODO stream commands read for each stream.</summary>
+    internal const string StreamVariable = "MCPSERVER_REPL_STREAM_COMMAND_TIMEOUT_SECONDS";
+
+    /// <summary>The protocol's timeout message, with the applied timeout in whole seconds.</summary>
+    internal const string CommandMessageFormat = "Command timed out after {0} seconds.";
+
+    /// <summary>The stream's timeout message, with the applied timeout in whole seconds.</summary>
+    internal const string StreamMessageFormat = "Stream command timed out after {0} seconds.";
+
+    /// <summary>The outcome kind of a command that finished normally.</summary>
+    internal const string Completed = "completed";
+
+    /// <summary>The outcome kind of a command stopped by the configured timeout.</summary>
+    internal const string TimedOut = "timed-out";
+
+    /// <summary>What one run of a call site produced: its outcome kind and, for a timeout, the message.</summary>
+    /// <param name="Kind"><see cref="Completed"/>, <see cref="TimedOut"/>, or a description of anything else.</param>
+    /// <param name="Message">The timeout message, when the kind is <see cref="TimedOut"/>.</param>
+    internal sealed record CallSiteResult(string Kind, string? Message);
+
+    /// <summary>One contract row: the call site's own variable value, the other variable's value, the command, and the outcome.</summary>
+    private sealed record CallSiteRow(
+        string Label,
+        string? OwnValue,
+        string? OtherValue,
+        bool RunsUntilCancelled,
+        string ExpectedKind,
+        long? ExpectedSeconds);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// The contract rows. A valid own value stops a command that runs until cancelled after that many seconds and the
+    /// message names it; a value the rule refuses (above the timer's limit, the round 7 value 2147483647, zero, not a
+    /// number) or an unset own value never fails a command that answers at once, and neither does the largest valid
+    /// value; the other call site's variable is never read.
+    /// </summary>
+    private static readonly CallSiteRow[] Rows =
+    [
+        new("own 1, command runs until cancelled", "1", null, true, TimedOut, 1),
+        new("own 2, command runs until cancelled", "2", null, true, TimedOut, 2),
+        new("own 2147483647, command answers at once", "2147483647", null, false, Completed, null),
+        new("own 4294968, command answers at once", "4294968", null, false, Completed, null),
+        new("own 4294967, command answers at once", "4294967", null, false, Completed, null),
+        new("own 0, command answers at once", "0", null, false, Completed, null),
+        new("own not-a-number, command answers at once", "not-a-number", null, false, Completed, null),
+        new("own unset and other 1, command answers at once", null, "1", false, Completed, null),
+    ];
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Asserts that <paramref name="run"/> gives the expected outcome for every contract row and reads exactly one
+    /// variable, <paramref name="ownVariable"/>, once per run.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="ownVariable">The variable this call site must read.</param>
+    /// <param name="otherVariable">The other call site's variable, which this call site must not read.</param>
+    /// <param name="messageFormat">This call site's timeout message format.</param>
+    /// <param name="run">Runs the call site once with a variable reader and a command that runs until cancelled or not.</param>
+    internal static async Task AssertCallSiteContract(
+        string ownVariable,
+        string otherVariable,
+        string messageFormat,
+        Func<Func<string, string?>, bool, Task<CallSiteResult>> run)
+    {
+        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(run);
+        var wrong = new List<string>();
+        foreach (var row in Rows)
+        {
+            var values = new Dictionary<string, string?>(StringComparer.Ordinal)
+            {
+                [ownVariable] = row.OwnValue,
+                [otherVariable] = row.OtherValue,
+            };
+            var namesRead = new List<string>();
+            string? Read(string name)
+            {
+                lock (namesRead)
+                {
+                    namesRead.Add(name);
+                }
+
+                return values.GetValueOrDefault(name);
+            }
+
+            CallSiteResult result;
+            try
+            {
+                result = await run(Read, row.RunsUntilCancelled).ConfigureAwait(true);
+            }
+            catch (Exception ex)
+            {
+                result = new CallSiteResult("exception " + ex.GetType().Name, ex.Message);
+            }
+
+            var expectedMessage = row.ExpectedSeconds is { } seconds
+                ? string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, messageFormat, seconds)
+                : null;
+            if (result.Kind != row.ExpectedKind || (expectedMessage is not null && result.Message != expectedMessage))
+            {
+                wrong.Add($"{row.Label}: gave {result.Kind} '{result.Message}' instead of {row.ExpectedKind} '{expectedMessage}'");
+            }
+
+            string[] read;
+            lock (namesRead)
+            {
+                read = [.. namesRead];
+            }
+
+            if (read.Length != 1 || read[0] != ownVariable)
+            {
+                wrong.Add($"{row.Label}: read [{string.Join(", ", read)}] instead of [{ownVariable}] once");
+            }
+        }
+
+        Assert.Empty(wrong);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Mock gate positive control: a scripted reference call site (one read of its own variable, the scripted contract
+    /// parse, the runtime's cancellation timer, the message naming the applied timeout) satisfies the contract for
+    /// both call sites.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public async Task ScriptedReferenceCallSite_SatisfiesTheContract_ForBothCallSites()
+    {
+        await AssertCallSiteContract(
+            CommandVariable,
+            StreamVariable,
+            CommandMessageFormat,
+            (read, runs) => ScriptedCallSite(read, runs, CommandVariable, CommandMessageFormat, ReplCommandTimeoutTests.ScriptedReference)).ConfigureAwait(true);
+        await AssertCallSiteContract(
+            StreamVariable,
+            CommandVariable,
+            StreamMessageFormat,
+            (read, runs) => ScriptedCallSite(read, runs, StreamVariable, StreamMessageFormat, ReplCommandTimeoutTests.ScriptedReference)).ConfigureAwait(true);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Mock gate negative controls, each rejected inside a passing harness for each call site: the unbounded parse from
+    /// before the R7-F1 fix (its timer throws for 2147483647), a call site that reads the other variable, one that reads
+    /// its variable twice, one whose message names the two-minute default instead of the applied timeout, and one whose
+    /// message always names one second.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="callSite">"command" for the protocol's contract, "stream" for the stream commands' contract.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("command")]
+    [InlineData("stream")]
+    public async Task WrongCallSites_AreRejectedInsidePassingHarness(string callSite)
+    {
+        var (own, other, format) = callSite == "command"
+            ? (CommandVariable, StreamVariable, CommandMessageFormat)
+            : (StreamVariable, CommandVariable, StreamMessageFormat);
+        Func<string?, TimeSpan> parse = ReplCommandTimeoutTests.ScriptedReference;
+        var wrongCallSites = new (string Label, Func<Func<string, string?>, bool, Task<CallSiteResult>> Run)[]
+        {
+            ("unbounded parse, as before the R7-F1 fix", (read, runs) => ScriptedCallSite(read, runs, own, format, ReplCommandTimeoutTests.Unbounded)),
+            ("reads the other variable", (read, runs) => ScriptedCallSite(read, runs, other, format, parse)),
+            ("reads its variable twice", (read, runs) =>
+            {
+                _ = read(own);
+                return ScriptedCallSite(read, runs, own, format, parse);
+            }),
+            ("message names the default", async (read, runs) => WithMessage(await ScriptedCallSite(read, runs, own, format, parse).ConfigureAwait(true), format, 120)),
+            ("message always names one second", async (read, runs) => WithMessage(await ScriptedCallSite(read, runs, own, format, parse).ConfigureAwait(true), format, 1)),
+        };
+
+        foreach (var (label, run) in wrongCallSites)
+        {
+            var failure = await Record.ExceptionAsync(() => AssertCallSiteContract(own, other, format, run)).ConfigureAwait(true);
+            Assert.True(failure is not null, $"The {callSite} contract accepted a wrong call site: {label}.");
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Binding: the agent stdio protocol, given the scripted reader through its <c>ReadVariable</c> seam and a
+    /// dispatcher whose command answers at once or runs until cancelled, satisfies the same contract rows the scripted
+    /// reference passed and the wrong call sites failed. The two timeout rows wait 1 and 2 seconds of real time.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public async Task ProductionProtocol_SatisfiesTheContract() =>
+        await AssertCallSiteContract(CommandVariable, StreamVariable, CommandMessageFormat, RunProtocolAsync).ConfigureAwait(true);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Binding: a TODO stream command (workflow.todo.streamStatus) through the dispatcher, given the scripted reader
+    /// through its <c>ReadVariable</c> seam and a workflow whose stream ends at once or runs until cancelled, satisfies
+    /// the same contract rows. The two timeout rows wait 1 and 2 seconds of real time.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public async Task ProductionStreamCommand_SatisfiesTheContract() =>
+        await AssertCallSiteContract(StreamVariable, CommandVariable, StreamMessageFormat, RunStreamCommandAsync).ConfigureAwait(true);
+
+    private static async Task<CallSiteResult> RunProtocolAsync(Func<string, string?> read, bool runsUntilCancelled)
+    {
+        var serializer = new YamlSerializer();
+        var sut = new AgentStdioProtocol(serializer, new ScriptedDispatcher(runsUntilCancelled)) { ReadVariable = read };
+        var input = new StringBuilder()
+            .AppendLine("type: request")
+            .AppendLine("payload:")
+            .AppendLine("  requestId: req-callsite-timeout")
+            .AppendLine("  method: client.todo.QueryAsync")
+            .AppendLine()
+            .ToString();
+        using var reader = new StringReader(input);
+        using var writer = new StringWriter();
+        await sut.RunAsync(reader, writer, TestContext.Current.CancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(true);
+
+        var response = serializer.Deserialize(FirstDocument(writer.ToString()));
+        return response.Payload switch
+        {
+            ErrorPayload { Code: "command_timeout" } error => new CallSiteResult(TimedOut, error.Message),
+            ErrorPayload error => new CallSiteResult("error " + error.Code, error.Message),
+            ResultPayload => new CallSiteResult(Completed, null),
+            _ => new CallSiteResult("envelope " + response.Type, null),
+        };
+    }
+
+    private static async Task<CallSiteResult> RunStreamCommandAsync(Func<string, string?> read, bool runsUntilCancelled)
+    {
+        var todo = Substitute.For<ITodoWorkflow>();
+        todo.StreamStatusAsync("MCP-TIMEOUT-001", Arg.Any<Func<IStreamingEvent, Task>>(), Arg.Any<CancellationToken>())
+            .Returns(call => runsUntilCancelled
+                ? Task.Delay(Timeout.Infinite, call.ArgAt<CancellationToken>(2))
+                : Task.CompletedTask);
+        var sut = new ReplCommandDispatcher(Substitute.For<IGenericClientPassthrough>(), todoWorkflow: todo) { ReadVariable = read };
+        var envelope = new YamlEnvelope
+        {
+            Type = "request",
+            Payload = new RequestPayload
+            {
+                RequestId = "req-callsite-stream",
+                Method = TodoCommandShapes.StreamStatusMethod,
+                Params = new Dictionary<string, object?> { ["id"] = "MCP-TIMEOUT-001" },
+            },
+        };
+
+        var response = await sut.DispatchAsync(envelope, TestContext.Current.CancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(true);
+        if (response.Payload is ErrorPayload error)
+        {
+            return new CallSiteResult("error " + error.Code, error.Message);
+        }
+
+        if (response.Payload is not ResultPayload { Result: IReadOnlyList<IStreamingEvent> events })
+        {
+            return new CallSiteResult("envelope " + response.Type, null);
+        }
+
+        var timeout = events.LastOrDefault(evt => evt.EventType == "stream.timeout");
+        return timeout is null
+            ? new CallSiteResult(Completed, null)
+            : new CallSiteResult(TimedOut, (timeout.Data as IReadOnlyDictionary<string, object?>)?.GetValueOrDefault("message") as string);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>The first YAML document of the protocol's output, which frames each envelope with a "---" line.</summary>
+    private static string FirstDocument(string output)
+    {
+        var document = new StringBuilder();
+        foreach (var line in output.Replace("\r\n", "\n", StringComparison.Ordinal).Split('\n'))
+        {
+            if (line.Trim() == "---")
+            {
+                if (document.Length == 0)
+                {
+                    continue;
+                }
+
+                break;
+            }
+
+            document.AppendLine(line);
+        }
+
+        return document.ToString();
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>A dispatcher whose one command answers at once, or runs until its token is cancelled.</summary>
+    private sealed class ScriptedDispatcher(bool runsUntilCancelled) : IReplCommandDispatcher
+    {
+        public async Task<IYamlEnvelope> DispatchAsync(IYamlEnvelope envelope, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
+        {
+            if (runsUntilCancelled)
+            {
+                await Task.Delay(Timeout.Infinite, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(true);
+            }
+
+            return new YamlEnvelope
+            {
+                Type = "result",
+                Payload = new ResultPayload { RequestId = "req-callsite-timeout", Result = "answered" },
+            };
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// A scripted call site: reads <paramref name="variable"/> once, parses it with <paramref name="parse"/>, arms the
+    /// runtime's cancellation timer with the result (which throws for an out-of-range delay, as the real timer does), and
+    /// reports a timeout naming the applied seconds for a command that runs until cancelled.
+    /// </summary>
+    private static Task<CallSiteResult> ScriptedCallSite(
+        Func<string, string?> read,
+        bool runsUntilCancelled,
+        string variable,
+        string messageFormat,
+        Func<string?, TimeSpan> parse)
+    {
+        var timeout = parse(read(variable));
+        using var timer = new CancellationTokenSource();
+        timer.CancelAfter(timeout);
+        return Task.FromResult(runsUntilCancelled
+            ? new CallSiteResult(TimedOut, string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, messageFormat, timeout.TotalSeconds))
+            : new CallSiteResult(Completed, null));
+    }
+
+    private static CallSiteResult WithMessage(CallSiteResult result, string messageFormat, long seconds) =>
+        result.Kind == TimedOut
+            ? result with { Message = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, messageFormat, seconds) }
+            : result;
+}

+using System.Globalization;
+using Xunit;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Repl.Core.Tests;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Tests the parse rule for the REPL command timeouts configured through MCPSERVER_REPL_COMMAND_TIMEOUT_SECONDS and
+/// MCPSERVER_REPL_STREAM_COMMAND_TIMEOUT_SECONDS: a whole number of seconds from 1 to the largest delay the runtime's
+/// cancellation timer accepts is used, and any other value gives the two-minute default.
+/// </summary>
+/// <remarks>
+/// Requirement coverage: TR-MCP-REPL-001 AC-002 (hostile validation round 7: both variables at 2147483647 made 19 REPL
+/// unit cases throw ArgumentOutOfRangeException from CancellationTokenSource.CancelAfter;
+/// triage-report-c993b9446bb8464ba375f74fabd07dad), and through it TR-MCP-USECASE-018 AC-006 and TR-MCP-USECASE-019
+/// AC-008 (a unit test may reach code that reads an environment variable only where its outcome does not change).
+/// Test data: a table of scripted configured values with the timeout each must give, run against a parse function
+/// passed in, so the contract is proved on a scripted reference parser with negative controls before the production
+/// parser exists; no test reads or writes the process environment.
+/// </remarks>
+public sealed class ReplCommandTimeoutTests
+{
+    private static readonly TimeSpan DefaultTimeout = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(2);
+
+    private const long LargestSeconds = 4294967;
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// The contract rows: each configured value and the timeout it must give. Unset, empty, not a number, zero,
+    /// negative and larger than the timer's limit give the default; 1 to the limit, surrounding blanks allowed as
+    /// before, give that many seconds.
+    /// </summary>
+    private static readonly (string? Configured, TimeSpan Expected)[] ContractRows =
+    [
+        (null, DefaultTimeout),
+        (string.Empty, DefaultTimeout),
+        ("   ", DefaultTimeout),
+        ("not-a-number", DefaultTimeout),
+        ("1.5", DefaultTimeout),
+        ("0", DefaultTimeout),
+        ("-1", DefaultTimeout),
+        ("-2147483648", DefaultTimeout),
+        ("1", TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1)),
+        ("120", TimeSpan.FromSeconds(120)),
+        (" 30 ", TimeSpan.FromSeconds(30)),
+        ("4294966", TimeSpan.FromSeconds(4294966)),
+        ("4294967", TimeSpan.FromSeconds(LargestSeconds)),
+        ("4294968", DefaultTimeout),
+        ("2147483647", DefaultTimeout),
+        ("99999999999", DefaultTimeout),
+    ];
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Asserts that <paramref name="parse"/> gives the expected timeout for every contract row, and that every timeout
+    /// it gives is accepted by <see cref="CancellationTokenSource.CancelAfter(TimeSpan)"/>.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="parse">The parse function under test.</param>
+    internal static void AssertParseContract(Func<string?, TimeSpan> parse)
+    {
+        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(parse);
+        var wrong = new List<string>();
+        foreach (var (configured, expected) in ContractRows)
+        {
+            var actual = parse(configured);
+            if (actual != expected)
+            {
+                wrong.Add($"'{configured ?? "(unset)"}' gave {actual} instead of {expected}");
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            using var source = new CancellationTokenSource();
+            source.CancelAfter(actual);
+        }
+
+        Assert.Empty(wrong);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Runtime check: the cancellation timer accepts the largest whole-second delay and rejects one more.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void CancelAfter_AcceptsTheLargestSecondsAndRejectsOneMore()
+    {
+        using var source = new CancellationTokenSource();
+        source.CancelAfter(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(LargestSeconds));
+        Assert.Throws<ArgumentOutOfRangeException>(() => source.CancelAfter(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(LargestSeconds + 1L)));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Binding: the production parser, <see cref="ReplCommandTimeout.Parse"/>, satisfies the same contract rows the
+    /// scripted reference passed and the wrong parsers failed, and its default and limit are the contract's.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ProductionParser_SatisfiesTheContract()
+    {
+        Assert.Equal(DefaultTimeout, ReplCommandTimeout.Default);
+        Assert.Equal(LargestSeconds, ReplCommandTimeout.LargestSeconds);
+        AssertParseContract(ReplCommandTimeout.Parse);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Mock gate positive control: the scripted reference parser satisfies every contract row.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ScriptedReferenceParser_SatisfiesTheContract() =>
+        AssertParseContract(ScriptedReference);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Mock gate negative controls, each rejected inside a passing harness: the parser before this fix (no upper bound,
+    /// so 4294968 and 2147483647 pass through), one that caps a larger value at the limit instead of using the default,
+    /// one that refuses the limit itself, and one that refuses 1.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void WrongParsers_AreRejectedInsidePassingHarness()
+    {
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertParseContract(Unbounded)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertParseContract(Capping)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertParseContract(configured => Bounded(configured, 1, LargestSeconds - 1))));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertParseContract(configured => Bounded(configured, 2, LargestSeconds))));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>The scripted reference parse, also used by <see cref="ReplCommandTimeoutCallSiteTests"/>.</summary>
+    internal static TimeSpan ScriptedReference(string? configured) => Bounded(configured, 1, LargestSeconds);
+
+    private static TimeSpan Bounded(string? configured, long smallest, long largest) =>
+        long.TryParse(configured, NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var seconds) && seconds >= smallest && seconds <= largest
+            ? TimeSpan.FromSeconds(seconds)
+            : DefaultTimeout;
+
+    /// <summary>The parse from before the R7-F1 fix, a negative control here and in <see cref="ReplCommandTimeoutCallSiteTests"/>.</summary>
+    internal static TimeSpan Unbounded(string? configured) =>
+        int.TryParse(configured, out var seconds) && seconds > 0 ? TimeSpan.FromSeconds(seconds) : DefaultTimeout;
+
+    private static TimeSpan Capping(string? configured) =>
+        long.TryParse(configured, NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var seconds) && seconds > 0
+            ? TimeSpan.FromSeconds(Math.Min(seconds, LargestSeconds))
+            : DefaultTimeout;
+}
