+using QBrainAi.TestSupport.TemporaryInstance;
+using Xunit;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Repl.Core.Tests.TestSupport;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Tests the checks an integration case uses to prove it runs on a temporary instance it owns: the REPL host's start
+/// arguments name a workspace and a marker inside the instance's own root, and the instance's health answer is 200 and
+/// echoes the request's nonce. Also tests the decision to start a new instance on a new port when the instance lost
+/// its port at start (TR-MCP-NUKE-019).
+/// </summary>
+/// <remarks>
+/// Requirement coverage: TR-MCP-NUKE-019 AC-6 (every integration case MigrationIntegrationTests runs uses a temporary
+/// instance it owns, never the live server, its marker or its API key, and asserts on that instance's answers instead
+/// of catching a failure and passing; hostile validation round 8b, policy 7.9; the live-marker defect is
+/// triage-report-81596036a11249d3b79afac1b53d53d9). Test data: scripted argument lists and health answers with the
+/// verdict each must get, as literal strings with both separator styles, run against checks passed in, so the
+/// contract is proved on scripted reference checks with negative controls before the shared checks exist; no test
+/// touches the file system, the network or the process environment.
+/// </remarks>
+public sealed class TemporaryInstanceChecksTests
+{
+    private const string OwnedRoot = "/scratch/owned";
+    private const string WindowsOwnedRoot = @"C:\scratch\owned";
+    private const string Nonce = "nonce-r8b-1";
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// The start-argument contract rows: a row name, the REPL host's arguments, the owned root, and whether the check
+    /// must accept them. Only a workspace and a marker strictly inside the owned root are accepted.
+    /// </summary>
+    internal static readonly (string Name, string[] Arguments, string Root, bool Accepted)[] StartRows =
+    [
+        ("owned layout", Start("/scratch/owned/workspace", "/scratch/owned/workspace/AGENTS-README-FIRST.yaml"), OwnedRoot, true),
+        ("owned layout with Windows separators", Start(@"C:\scratch\owned\workspace", @"C:\scratch\owned\workspace\AGENTS-README-FIRST.yaml"), WindowsOwnedRoot, true),
+        ("the root checkout's marker", Start("/scratch/owned/workspace", "/home/user/github/McpServer/AGENTS-README-FIRST.yaml"), OwnedRoot, false),
+        ("a marker in another temporary root", Start("/scratch/owned/workspace", "/scratch/other/workspace/AGENTS-README-FIRST.yaml"), OwnedRoot, false),
+        ("a sibling root sharing the prefix", Start("/scratch/owned/workspace", "/scratch/owned2/workspace/AGENTS-README-FIRST.yaml"), OwnedRoot, false),
+        ("a parent-segment escape", Start("/scratch/owned/workspace", "/scratch/owned/../live/AGENTS-README-FIRST.yaml"), OwnedRoot, false),
+        ("the owned root itself as the marker", Start("/scratch/owned/workspace", "/scratch/owned"), OwnedRoot, false),
+        ("no marker argument", ["host.dll", "--agent-stdio", "--workspace-path", "/scratch/owned/workspace"], OwnedRoot, false),
+        ("a marker argument without a value", ["host.dll", "--agent-stdio", "--workspace-path", "/scratch/owned/workspace", "--marker-file"], OwnedRoot, false),
+        ("a workspace outside the root", Start("/home/user/github/McpServer", "/scratch/owned/workspace/AGENTS-README-FIRST.yaml"), OwnedRoot, false),
+        ("no workspace argument", ["host.dll", "--agent-stdio", "--marker-file", "/scratch/owned/workspace/AGENTS-README-FIRST.yaml"], OwnedRoot, false),
+    ];
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// The health-answer contract rows: a row name, the status code, the body, and whether the check must accept the
+    /// answer for <see cref="Nonce"/>. Only a 200 answer whose body holds the nonce is accepted.
+    /// </summary>
+    internal static readonly (string Name, int Status, string? Body, bool Accepted)[] HealthRows =
+    [
+        ("200 echoing the nonce", 200, "Healthy " + Nonce, true),
+        ("503 echoing the nonce", 503, "Unhealthy " + Nonce, false),
+        ("200 without the nonce", 200, "Healthy", false),
+        ("200 with an empty body", 200, string.Empty, false),
+        ("200 with no body", 200, null, false),
+        ("200 echoing a different nonce", 200, "Healthy nonce-r8b-2", false),
+    ];
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// The restart contract rows (TR-MCP-NUKE-019): a row name, the starts made so far, whether the instance
+    /// process has exited, its output, and whether the case must start a new instance on a new port. Only an exited
+    /// instance whose output says its address was already in use, within three starts in all, is restarted. The bind
+    /// failure text is the one Kestrel wrote in run ug-r8b-expanded-lane-1 (Iteration5IntegrationTests, port 49974).
+    /// </summary>
+    internal static readonly (string Name, int Starts, bool Exited, string Output, bool Restart)[] RestartRows =
+    [
+        ("first start lost its port", 1, true, BindFailure, true),
+        ("second start lost its port", 2, true, BindFailure, true),
+        ("third start lost its port", 3, true, BindFailure, false),
+        ("only the inner exception line", 1, true, "---> System.Net.Sockets.SocketException (98): Address already in use", true),
+        ("the bind failure in capitals", 1, true, "FAILED TO BIND TO ADDRESS HTTP://[::]:49974: ADDRESS ALREADY IN USE.", true),
+        ("instance still running", 1, false, BindFailure, false),
+        ("another start failure", 1, true, "fail: QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Services.TodoBootstrapImporter[0] TodoBootstrapImporter failed", false),
+        ("a file in use", 1, true, "The database file is in use by another process.", false),
+        ("no output", 1, true, string.Empty, false),
+    ];
+
+    private const string BindFailure = "Unhandled exception. System.IO.IOException: Failed to bind to address http://[::]:49974: address already in use.";
+
+    /// <summary>Returns the names of the restart rows on which <paramref name="check"/> gives the wrong verdict.</summary>
+    /// <param name="check">The restart decision under test: the starts so far, whether the process exited and its output.</param>
+    internal static List<string> RestartContractFailures(Func<int, bool, string, bool> check)
+    {
+        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(check);
+        return RestartRows
+            .Where(row => check(row.Starts, row.Exited, row.Output) != row.Restart)
+            .Select(row => row.Name)
+            .ToList();
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Mock gate positive control (TR-MCP-NUKE-019): the scripted restart decision satisfies every row.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ScriptedRestartDecision_SatisfiesTheContract() =>
+        Assert.Empty(RestartContractFailures(ScriptedRestart));
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Mock gate negative controls on the restart contract (TR-MCP-NUKE-019), each rejected inside a passing
+    /// harness on the row named: a decision with no bound on starts, one that ignores whether the process exited, one
+    /// that matches any "in use" text, one that matches case-sensitively, and one that never restarts.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="wrong">The name of the wrong restart decision.</param>
+    /// <param name="failingRow">The contract row that must reject it.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("unbounded", "third start lost its port")]
+    [InlineData("ignores exit", "instance still running")]
+    [InlineData("any in use", "a file in use")]
+    [InlineData("case-sensitive", "the bind failure in capitals")]
+    [InlineData("never restarts", "first start lost its port")]
+    public void WrongRestartDecisions_AreRejectedInsidePassingHarness(string wrong, string failingRow) =>
+        Assert.Contains(failingRow, RestartContractFailures(WrongRestartCheck(wrong)));
+
+    private static bool ScriptedRestart(int starts, bool exited, string output) =>
+        starts < 3 && exited && output.Contains("address already in use", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
+
+    private static Func<int, bool, string, bool> WrongRestartCheck(string wrong) => wrong switch
+    {
+        "unbounded" => (_, exited, output) => exited && output.Contains("address already in use", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase),
+        "ignores exit" => (starts, _, output) => starts < 3 && output.Contains("address already in use", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase),
+        "any in use" => (starts, exited, output) => starts < 3 && exited && output.Contains("in use", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase),
+        "case-sensitive" => (starts, exited, output) => starts < 3 && exited && output.Contains("address already in use", StringComparison.Ordinal),
+        "never restarts" => (_, _, _) => false,
+        _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(wrong), wrong, "Unknown wrong restart decision."),
+    };
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Returns the names of the start-argument rows on which <paramref name="check"/> gives the wrong verdict (a
+    /// violation message for an accepted row, or null for a rejected row).
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="check">The start-argument check under test: the arguments and the owned root to a violation or null.</param>
+    internal static List<string> StartContractFailures(Func<IReadOnlyList<string>, string, string?> check)
+    {
+        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(check);
+        return StartRows
+            .Where(row => (check(row.Arguments, row.Root) is null) != row.Accepted)
+            .Select(row => row.Name)
+            .ToList();
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Returns the names of the health rows on which <paramref name="check"/> gives the wrong verdict.</summary>
+    /// <param name="check">The health check under test: the status, the body and the nonce to a violation or null.</param>
+    internal static List<string> HealthContractFailures(Func<int, string?, string, string?> check)
+    {
+        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(check);
+        return HealthRows
+            .Where(row => (check(row.Status, row.Body, Nonce) is null) != row.Accepted)
+            .Select(row => row.Name)
+            .ToList();
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Binding: the shared checks, <see cref="TemporaryInstanceChecks.CheckReplStartArguments"/>,
+    /// <see cref="TemporaryInstanceChecks.CheckHealthAnswer"/> and
+    /// <see cref="TemporaryInstanceChecks.ShouldRestartOnNewPort"/> (TR-MCP-NUKE-019), satisfy the same rows the
+    /// scripted references passed and the wrong checks failed.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void SharedChecks_SatisfyTheContract()
+    {
+        Assert.Empty(StartContractFailures(TemporaryInstanceChecks.CheckReplStartArguments));
+        Assert.Empty(HealthContractFailures(TemporaryInstanceChecks.CheckHealthAnswer));
+        Assert.Empty(RestartContractFailures(TemporaryInstanceChecks.ShouldRestartOnNewPort));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Mock gate positive control: the scripted reference checks satisfy every row of both contracts.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ScriptedReferenceChecks_SatisfyTheContract()
+    {
+        Assert.Empty(StartContractFailures(ScriptedStart));
+        Assert.Empty(HealthContractFailures(ScriptedHealth));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Mock gate negative controls, each rejected inside a passing harness on the row named: a check that accepts
+    /// anything, one that checks only the workspace, one that compares a string prefix without a segment boundary, one
+    /// that does not refuse a parent segment, and one that accepts a missing marker.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="wrong">The name of the wrong start-argument check.</param>
+    /// <param name="failingRow">The contract row that must reject it.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("accepts anything", "the root checkout's marker")]
+    [InlineData("workspace only", "the root checkout's marker")]
+    [InlineData("string prefix", "a sibling root sharing the prefix")]
+    [InlineData("no parent-segment refusal", "a parent-segment escape")]
+    [InlineData("missing marker allowed", "no marker argument")]
+    public void WrongStartChecks_AreRejectedInsidePassingHarness(string wrong, string failingRow) =>
+        Assert.Contains(failingRow, StartContractFailures(WrongStartCheck(wrong)));
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Mock gate negative controls on the health contract: a check that reads only the status and one that reads only
+    /// the body, each rejected on the row named.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="wrong">The name of the wrong health check.</param>
+    /// <param name="failingRow">The contract row that must reject it.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("status only", "200 without the nonce")]
+    [InlineData("body only", "503 echoing the nonce")]
+    public void WrongHealthChecks_AreRejectedInsidePassingHarness(string wrong, string failingRow) =>
+        Assert.Contains(failingRow, HealthContractFailures(WrongHealthCheck(wrong)));
+
+    private static string[] Start(string workspace, string marker) =>
+        ["host.dll", "--agent-stdio", "--agent", "default", "--workspace-path", workspace, "--marker-file", marker];
+
+    private static string? ScriptedStart(IReadOnlyList<string> arguments, string root)
+    {
+        var workspace = ValueAfter(arguments, "--workspace-path");
+        var marker = ValueAfter(arguments, "--marker-file");
+        if (workspace is null)
+            return "The REPL host was started without --workspace-path.";
+        if (marker is null)
+            return "The REPL host was started without --marker-file.";
+        if (!ScriptedInside(workspace, root))
+            return $"The workspace '{workspace}' is outside the owned root '{root}'.";
+        return ScriptedInside(marker, root) ? null : $"The marker '{marker}' is outside the owned root '{root}'.";
+    }
+
+    private static string? ScriptedHealth(int status, string? body, string nonce) =>
+        status == 200 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(nonce) && body is not null && body.Contains(nonce, StringComparison.Ordinal)
+            ? null
+            : $"The health answer was {status} and did not echo the nonce '{nonce}'.";
+
+    private static bool ScriptedInside(string path, string root)
+    {
+        var pathSegments = Segments(path);
+        var rootSegments = Segments(root);
+        if (pathSegments.Any(segment => segment is "." or ".."))
+            return false;
+        return pathSegments.Length > rootSegments.Length
+            && rootSegments.SequenceEqual(pathSegments.Take(rootSegments.Length), StringComparer.Ordinal);
+    }
+
+    private static string[] Segments(string path) =>
+        path.Split(['/', '\\'], StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
+
+    private static string? ValueAfter(IReadOnlyList<string> arguments, string name)
+    {
+        for (var index = 0; index < arguments.Count - 1; index++)
+        {
+            if (string.Equals(arguments[index], name, StringComparison.Ordinal))
+                return arguments[index + 1];
+        }
+
+        return null;
+    }
+
+    private static Func<IReadOnlyList<string>, string, string?> WrongStartCheck(string wrong) => wrong switch
+    {
+        "accepts anything" => (_, _) => null,
+        "workspace only" => (arguments, root) =>
+            ValueAfter(arguments, "--workspace-path") is { } workspace && ScriptedInside(workspace, root) ? null : "workspace outside",
+        "string prefix" => (arguments, root) =>
+            ValueAfter(arguments, "--workspace-path") is { } workspace
+            && ValueAfter(arguments, "--marker-file") is { } marker
+            && workspace.StartsWith(root, StringComparison.Ordinal)
+            && marker.StartsWith(root, StringComparison.Ordinal)
+            && marker.Length > root.Length
+            && !marker.Contains("..", StringComparison.Ordinal)
+                ? null
+                : "outside",
+        "no parent-segment refusal" => (arguments, root) =>
+            ValueAfter(arguments, "--workspace-path") is { } workspace
+            && ValueAfter(arguments, "--marker-file") is { } marker
+            && SegmentPrefix(workspace, root)
+            && SegmentPrefix(marker, root)
+                ? null
+                : "outside",
+        "missing marker allowed" => (arguments, root) =>
+            ValueAfter(arguments, "--marker-file") is null ? null : ScriptedStart(arguments, root),
+        _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(wrong), wrong, "Unknown wrong start check."),
+    };
+
+    private static Func<int, string?, string, string?> WrongHealthCheck(string wrong) => wrong switch
+    {
+        "status only" => (status, _, _) => status == 200 ? null : "not 200",
+        "body only" => (_, body, nonce) => body is not null && body.Contains(nonce, StringComparison.Ordinal) ? null : "no nonce",
+        _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(wrong), wrong, "Unknown wrong health check."),
+    };
+
+    private static bool SegmentPrefix(string path, string root)
+    {
+        var pathSegments = Segments(path);
+        var rootSegments = Segments(root);
+        return pathSegments.Length > rootSegments.Length
+            && rootSegments.SequenceEqual(pathSegments.Take(rootSegments.Length), StringComparer.Ordinal);
+    }
+}

+    /// scripted sources yield exactly the lines that hold a skip form in code (never inside a string, comment
+    /// or <c>nameof</c>), including an attribute whose arguments span lines, a qualified or listed attribute,

+    /// </summary>

+    [InlineData("[Trait(\"a\", \"b\"), Theory(Skip = \"x\")]\npublic void A(int x) { }", "1")]
+    [InlineData("[Theory]\n[InlineData(1, Skip = \"row\")]\npublic void A(int x) { }", "2")]
+    [InlineData("[SkippableFactAttribute]\npublic void A() { }", "1")]
+    [InlineData("[Fact(SkipExceptions = new[] { typeof(InvalidOperationException) })]\npublic void A() { }", "1")]
+    [InlineData("Skip.If(true);\nSkip.IfNot(false);", "1,2")]
+    [InlineData("throw new Xunit.Sdk.SkipException(\"x\");", "1")]

+    [InlineData("// Assert.Skip(\"x\");", "")]

+    [InlineData("var name = nameof(SkipException);", "")]

+    [InlineData("[Fact]\npublic void Skip() { var Skip = 1; }", "")]
+    public void FindSkipLines_FindsEverySkipFormOutsideLiterals(string source, string expectedLines)
+    {
+        var expected = expectedLines.Length == 0
+            ? Array.Empty<int>()
+            : expectedLines.Split(',').Select(value => int.Parse(value, System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)).ToArray();
+        Assert.Equal(expected, UnitSuitePlatformScanner.FindSkipLines(source));
+    }

+    /// <summary>
+    /// TR-MCP-USECASE-018 (AC-006) negative controls: the single-line patterns this guard used before
+    /// hostile validation round 1 (finding F3) missed a skip attribute split across lines and a qualified
+    /// skip attribute, and the pattern used before 2026-10-19 also missed <c>Assert.SkipUnless</c> and
+    /// <c>Assert.SkipWhen</c>; text matching counts a skip that only appears inside a string, which the
+    /// compiler-parsed definition does not.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void SkipGuardNegativeControls_AreRejectedInsidePassingHarness()
+    {
+        var original = new Regex(
+            @"\[(?:Fact|Theory)\s*\([^\]]*\bSkip\s*=|\bSkipException\b|\bAssert\.Skip\b|\bSkippable(?:Fact|Theory)\b",
+            RegexOptions.CultureInvariant);
+        var roundOne = new Regex(
+            @"\[(?:Fact|Theory)\s*\([^\]]*\bSkip\s*=|\bSkipException\b|\bAssert\s*\.\s*Skip(?:When|Unless)?\b|\bSkippable(?:Fact|Theory)\b",
+            RegexOptions.CultureInvariant);
+        Assert.DoesNotMatch(original, "Assert.SkipUnless(OperatingSystem.IsWindows(), \"w\");");
+        Assert.DoesNotMatch(original, "Assert.SkipWhen(true, \"w\");");
+        foreach (var line in "[Fact(\n    Skip = \"later\")]".Split('\n'))
+        {
+            Assert.DoesNotMatch(roundOne, line);
+        }
+
+        Assert.DoesNotMatch(roundOne, "[Xunit.Fact(Skip = \"later\")]");
+        Assert.Equal(new[] { 1 }, UnitSuitePlatformScanner.FindSkipLines("[Xunit.Fact(Skip = \"later\")]\npublic void A() { }"));
+        Assert.Matches(roundOne, "var text = \"Assert.SkipUnless(true)\";");
+        Assert.Empty(UnitSuitePlatformScanner.FindSkipLines("var text = \"Assert.SkipUnless(true)\";"));
+    }

+using QBrainAi.Tests.Shared;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests.Configuration;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-USECASE-018 (AC-006), TR-MCP-USECASE-019 (AC-008), BDPv4 mock gate for the trait policy of
+/// <see cref="TestRepositoryRoot"/>, the one repository-root locator: scripted trait sets (mock data) pass only when
+/// they hold <c>Category=Integration</c>, matching the scanner's ordinal trait test; negative controls show the shared
+/// assertion rejects a policy that always passes, one that always refuses, and one that refuses with another reason.
+/// The ambient case shows a unit test is refused before the checkout is touched; the integration case is the
+/// production binding and reads the checkout.
+/// </summary>
+public sealed class TestRepositoryRootPolicyTests
+{
+    private static readonly Dictionary<string, (IReadOnlyDictionary<string, IReadOnlyCollection<string>>? Traits, bool Allowed)> Cases =
+        new(StringComparer.Ordinal)
+        {
+            ["integration"] = (Traits(("Category", ["Integration"])), true),
+            ["integration-with-platform"] = (Traits(("Category", ["Integration"]), ("Platform", ["Linux"])), true),
+            ["integration-among-categories"] = (Traits(("Category", ["Slow", "Integration"])), true),
+            ["unit-category"] = (Traits(("Category", ["Unit"])), false),
+            ["no-traits"] = (Traits(), false),
+            ["no-test-context"] = (null, false),
+            ["platform-only"] = (Traits(("Platform", ["Windows"])), false),
+            ["lowercase-key"] = (Traits(("category", ["Integration"])), false),
+            ["lowercase-value"] = (Traits(("Category", ["integration"])), false),
+        };
+
+    /// <summary>Every scripted trait set is allowed or refused as the rule requires.</summary>
+    /// <param name="name">Case name.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("integration")]
+    [InlineData("integration-with-platform")]
+    [InlineData("integration-among-categories")]
+    [InlineData("unit-category")]
+    [InlineData("no-traits")]
+    [InlineData("no-test-context")]
+    [InlineData("platform-only")]
+    [InlineData("lowercase-key")]
+    [InlineData("lowercase-value")]
+    public void ScriptedTraits_AreAllowedOnlyForIntegration(string name)
+    {
+        var (traits, allowed) = Cases[name];
+        AssertPolicy(TestRepositoryRoot.EnsureIntegration, traits, allowed);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>The theory above covers every scripted case.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ScriptedTheory_CoversEveryCase() => Assert.Equal(9, Cases.Count);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Negative controls: the shared assertion rejects a policy that lets a unit test through, one that refuses an
+    /// integration test, and one that refuses a unit test for a reason other than the rule.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void PolicyAssertionNegativeControls_AreRejectedInsidePassingHarness()
+    {
+        var unit = Cases["unit-category"].Traits;
+        var integration = Cases["integration"].Traits;
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertPolicy(_ => { }, unit, allowed: false)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertPolicy(_ => throw new InvalidOperationException("refused"), integration, allowed: true)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertPolicy(_ => throw new InvalidOperationException("another reason"), unit, allowed: false)));
+        Assert.Null(Record.Exception(() => AssertPolicy(TestRepositoryRoot.EnsureIntegration, unit, allowed: false)));
+        Assert.Null(Record.Exception(() => AssertPolicy(TestRepositoryRoot.EnsureIntegration, integration, allowed: true)));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>A unit test that reaches the locator is refused before the checkout is located or read.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void Resolve_InAUnitTest_IsRefusedWithTheRule()
+    {
+        var resolve = Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => TestRepositoryRoot.Resolve());
+        Assert.Contains("may not read the repository checkout", resolve.Message, StringComparison.Ordinal);
+        var combine = Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => TestRepositoryRoot.Combine("tests"));
+        Assert.Contains("may not read the repository checkout", combine.Message, StringComparison.Ordinal);
+        var metadata = Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => TestRepositoryRoot.BuildMetadataRoot);
+        Assert.Contains("may not read the repository checkout", metadata.Message, StringComparison.Ordinal);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Production binding: in an integration test the locator returns the checkout root.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    [Trait("Category", "Integration")]
+    public void Resolve_InAnIntegrationTest_ReturnsTheCheckoutRoot()
+    {
+        var root = TestRepositoryRoot.Resolve();
+        Assert.True(File.Exists(Path.Combine(root, "QBrainAi.sln")), root);
+        Assert.Equal(Path.Combine(root, "tests"), TestRepositoryRoot.Combine("tests"));
+        Assert.True(File.Exists(TestRepositoryRoot.Combine("tests", "Shared", "TestRepositoryRoot.cs")));
+        Assert.False(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(TestRepositoryRoot.BuildMetadataRoot));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Assertion shared by the scripted cases and the negative controls.</summary>
+    /// <param name="policy">The policy under test.</param>
+    /// <param name="traits">Scripted traits.</param>
+    /// <param name="allowed">Whether the rule allows them.</param>
+    private static void AssertPolicy(
+        Action<IReadOnlyDictionary<string, IReadOnlyCollection<string>>?> policy,
+        IReadOnlyDictionary<string, IReadOnlyCollection<string>>? traits,
+        bool allowed)
+    {
+        if (allowed)
+        {
+            Assert.Null(Record.Exception(() => policy(traits)));
+            return;
+        }
+
+        var refusal = Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => policy(traits));
+        Assert.Contains("may not read the repository checkout", refusal.Message, StringComparison.Ordinal);
+    }
+
+    private static IReadOnlyDictionary<string, IReadOnlyCollection<string>> Traits(params (string Name, string[] Values)[] traits) =>
+        traits.ToDictionary(trait => trait.Name, trait => (IReadOnlyCollection<string>)trait.Values, StringComparer.Ordinal);
+}

diff --git a/tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/Configuration/UnitSuitePlatformScanner.cs b/tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/Configuration/UnitSuitePlatformScanner.cs

+using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
+using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;
+using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp.Syntax;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests.Configuration;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-USECASE-018 (AC-006), TR-MCP-USECASE-019 (AC-008): finds platform dependence in unit tests by reading
+/// the C# syntax tree. A test that depends on the platform is an integration test (operator 2026-10-19: "If it
+/// depends on the environment, it's an integration test."), so an operating-system check, an OS-platform
+/// attribute, an xUnit skip, or a binding to the real process host is a finding unless the test method, its
+/// test class, or an enclosing class carries <c>[Trait("Category", "Integration")]</c>. The compiler's parser
+/// separates code from strings, comments and documentation and keeps the code inside interpolation holes, so a
+/// member access split across lines, a qualified or global-qualified name, a name used through a using alias
+/// whose target names a type, a <c>using static</c> import that names a type, a method group, and an attribute
+/// anywhere in an attribute list are read like a plain call or attribute; a name inside <c>nameof</c> is not a
+/// use. Aliases and static imports resolve in the lexical scope of the code that uses them, in the compiler's
+/// scope order: the file's directives, then each enclosing namespace's, an inner alias hiding an outer one, so a
+/// directive in one namespace does not reach another. Every conditional-compilation branch is read: the file
+/// is parsed once for each combination of the symbols its <c>#if</c> and <c>#elif</c> directives name, and a
+/// file that names more than <see cref="MaxConditionalSymbols"/> symbols fails the scan instead of leaving a
+/// branch unread. Test classes are types whose name ends in <c>Tests</c> or that declare a method with an
+/// attribute whose name ends in <c>Fact</c> or <c>Theory</c>; other helper types (for example the host-neutral
+/// <c>UnitTestPaths</c> fixture) are not test classes. An OS-platform attribute on the assembly or module is a
+/// finding for the whole project. Limits: the scan reads one file's syntax, so dependence reached through a
+/// helper type defined in another file, a global using or alias declared in another file, reflection, or
+/// operating-system behavior of a base-library call such as <c>Path.GetFullPath</c> is outside it. An alias
+/// is resolved one step, to the rightmost name of its target, so an alias whose target is another alias (an
+/// alias chain such as <c>using Host = OS;</c>) and a <c>using static</c> that names an alias are not resolved:
+/// a declared limit (hostile validation round 4 finding R4-F1; operator 2026-10-21 "Declare the limit, no fix"),
+/// with the scanner work tracked in TODO IMPL-SCANNERALIAS-001. Repository
+/// reads are covered by construction instead: <see cref="FindRepositoryLocators"/> keeps the shared
+/// <c>TestRepositoryRoot</c> the only repository-root locator (DRY), and that locator refuses to resolve outside
+/// an integration test, so a unit test that reads the checkout through any helper fails the unit run.
+/// </summary>
+internal static class UnitSuitePlatformScanner
+{
+    /// <summary>Most distinct conditional-compilation symbols one file may name; every combination is parsed.</summary>
+    internal const int MaxConditionalSymbols = 8;
+
+    private const string AssemblyOwner = "<assembly>";
+
+    private const string AttributeSuffix = "Attribute";
+
+    private static readonly CSharpParseOptions ParseOptions = new(LanguageVersion.Preview, DocumentationMode.None);
+
+    private static readonly string[] KindOrder = ["os-check", "os-attribute", "skip", "process-host"];
+
+    /// <summary>Calls, property reads and method groups on a type, matched by the type's rightmost name.</summary>
+    private static readonly MemberRule[] MemberRules =
+    [
+        new("os-check", "OperatingSystem", static name => name.Length > 2 && name.StartsWith("Is", StringComparison.Ordinal) && name[2] is >= 'A' and <= 'Z'),
+        new("os-check", "RuntimeInformation", static name => name == "IsOSPlatform"),
+        new("skip", "Assert", static name => name is "Skip" or "SkipWhen" or "SkipUnless"),
+        new("skip", "Skip", static name => name is "If" or "IfNot"),
+        new("process-host", "SystemWorkspaceHostEnvironment", static name => name == "Instance"),
+        new("process-host", "WorkspacePathNormalizer", static name => name == "Process"),
+    ];
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// TR-MCP-USECASE-018 (AC-006): the one definition of an xUnit skip shared by this scanner and the skip guard.
+    /// Returns the 1-based lines, in any conditional-compilation branch and in any type or none, that hold a skip
+    /// call (<c>Assert.Skip</c>, <c>Assert.SkipWhen</c>, <c>Assert.SkipUnless</c>, <c>Skip.If</c>,
+    /// <c>Skip.IfNot</c>), a use of <c>SkipException</c>, a <c>SkippableFact</c> or <c>SkippableTheory</c>
+    /// attribute, or an attribute argument that sets a property whose name starts with <c>Skip</c> (for example
+    /// <c>Skip</c>, <c>SkipUnless</c>, <c>SkipWhen</c> or <c>SkipExceptions</c> on a test or data attribute); an
+    /// attribute is reported on the line of its name.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="source">C# source text.</param>
+    internal static IReadOnlyList<int> FindSkipLines(string source)
+    {
+        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(source);
+        return ParseBranches(source)
+            .SelectMany(FindHits)
+            .Where(hit => hit.Kind == "skip")
+            .Select(hit => LineOf(hit.Node))
+            .Distinct()
+            .Order()
+            .ToArray();
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// TR-MCP-USECASE-018 (AC-006), TR-MCP-USECASE-019 (AC-008), DRY guard for repository reads: returns the 1-based
+    /// lines, in any type and any conditional-compilation branch, of a repository-root locator other than the shared
+    /// <c>TestRepositoryRoot</c>: a walk up (<c>.Parent</c>, <c>?.Parent</c>, <c>Directory.GetParent</c>) in a member
+    /// that starts from <c>AppContext.BaseDirectory</c> or a <c>[CallerFilePath]</c> parameter (reported at the first
+    /// step), a <c>Path.Combine</c> or <c>Path.Join</c> of <c>AppContext.BaseDirectory</c> with a <c>..</c> segment,
+    /// or a read of the <c>McpRepositoryRoot</c> build metadata (the key and <c>AssemblyMetadataAttribute</c> in one
+    /// member; the key alone, as in a comparison, is not a read).
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="source">C# source text.</param>
+    internal static IReadOnlyList<int> FindRepositoryLocators(string source)
+    {
+        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(source);
+        return ParseBranches(source)
+            .SelectMany(RepositoryLocatorNodes)
+            .Select(LineOf)
+            .Distinct()
+            .Order()
+            .ToArray();
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Scans one C# source file and returns its findings in line order, then rule order.</summary>
+    /// <param name="relativePath">Path reported with each finding.</param>
+    /// <param name="source">File text.</param>
+    internal static IReadOnlyList<UnitSuitePlatformFinding> Scan(string relativePath, string source)
+    {
+        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(relativePath);
+        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(source);
+        var findings = new HashSet<(int Line, string Kind, string TestClass)>();
+        foreach (var root in ParseBranches(source))
+        {
+            foreach (var (node, kind) in FindHits(root))
+            {
+                var testClass = FindingOwner(node);
+                if (testClass is not null)
+                    findings.Add((LineOf(node), kind, testClass));
+            }
+        }
+
+        return findings
+            .OrderBy(finding => finding.Line)
+            .ThenBy(finding => Array.IndexOf(KindOrder, finding.Kind))
+            .ThenBy(finding => finding.TestClass, StringComparer.Ordinal)
+            .Select(finding => new UnitSuitePlatformFinding(relativePath, finding.Line, finding.Kind, finding.TestClass))
+            .ToArray();
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Parses the source once for every combination of the conditional-compilation symbols it names, so code in
+    /// every <c>#if</c>, <c>#elif</c> and <c>#else</c> branch reachable by some combination is in a tree.
+    /// </summary>
+    private static IReadOnlyList<CompilationUnitSyntax> ParseBranches(string source)
+    {
+        var none = Parse(source, []);
+        var symbols = new SortedSet<string>(SymbolsOf(none), StringComparer.Ordinal);
+        var known = 0;
+        while (symbols.Count != known && symbols.Count <= MaxConditionalSymbols)
+        {
+            // A directive nested in a branch that is inactive with no symbols defined names its symbols here.
+            known = symbols.Count;
+            symbols.UnionWith(SymbolsOf(Parse(source, symbols)).ToArray());
+        }
+
+        if (symbols.Count > MaxConditionalSymbols)
+        {
+            throw new InvalidOperationException(
+                $"The source names {symbols.Count} conditional-compilation symbols; the platform scan reads every branch and supports at most {MaxConditionalSymbols}.");
+        }
+
+        var names = symbols.ToArray();
+        var roots = new List<CompilationUnitSyntax> { none };
+        for (var mask = 1; mask < 1 << names.Length; mask++)
+            roots.Add(Parse(source, names.Where((_, index) => (mask & (1 << index)) != 0)));
+
+        return roots;
+    }
+
+    private static CompilationUnitSyntax Parse(string source, IEnumerable<string> symbols) =>
+        CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(source, ParseOptions.WithPreprocessorSymbols(symbols)).GetCompilationUnitRoot();
+
+    private static IEnumerable<string> SymbolsOf(CompilationUnitSyntax root) =>
+        root.DescendantNodes(descendIntoTrivia: true)
+            .OfType<ConditionalDirectiveTriviaSyntax>()
+            .SelectMany(directive => directive.Condition.DescendantNodesAndSelf().OfType<IdentifierNameSyntax>())
+            .Select(identifier => identifier.Identifier.ValueText);
+
+    /// <summary>Every rule hit in one tree, with the node it is reported on.</summary>
+    private static IEnumerable<(SyntaxNode Node, string Kind)> FindHits(CompilationUnitSyntax root)
+    {
+        var scopes = new Dictionary<SyntaxNode, ImportScope>();
+        foreach (var node in root.DescendantNodes(child => child is not UsingDirectiveSyntax))
+        {
+            IReadOnlyList<string> kinds = node switch
+            {
+                AttributeSyntax attribute => AttributeKinds(attribute, ScopeOf(attribute, scopes).Aliases),
+                MemberAccessExpressionSyntax access => MemberKinds(TypeName(access.Expression, ScopeOf(access, scopes).Aliases), access.Name.Identifier.ValueText),
+                IdentifierNameSyntax { Identifier.ValueText: "SkipException" } => ["skip"],
+                IdentifierNameSyntax identifier when IsUnqualified(identifier) => StaticImportKinds(identifier.Identifier.ValueText, ScopeOf(identifier, scopes).StaticTypes),
+                _ => [],
+            };
+            if (kinds.Count > 0 && (node is AttributeSyntax || !InNameOf(node)))
+            {
+                foreach (var kind in kinds)
+                    yield return (node, kind);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>The using aliases and static imports in effect at <paramref name="node"/>.</summary>
+    private static ImportScope ScopeOf(SyntaxNode node, Dictionary<SyntaxNode, ImportScope> scopes) =>
+        ScopeFor(node.Ancestors().First(IsImportContainer), scopes);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// The using aliases and static imports in effect inside <paramref name="container"/> (the file or a namespace),
+    /// in the compiler's scope order: the file's directives, then each enclosing namespace's from the outermost in,
+    /// an inner alias hiding an outer alias of the same name. Each alias and static import keeps the rightmost name
+    /// of its target as written, so an alias chain or a static import that names an alias is not resolved (the
+    /// declared limit in the class summary). Cached per container.
+    /// </summary>
+    private static ImportScope ScopeFor(SyntaxNode container, Dictionary<SyntaxNode, ImportScope> scopes)
+    {
+        if (scopes.TryGetValue(container, out var known))
+            return known;
+
+        var outer = container.Ancestors().FirstOrDefault(IsImportContainer);
+        var enclosing = outer is null ? ImportScope.Empty : ScopeFor(outer, scopes);
+        var aliases = new Dictionary<string, string>(enclosing.Aliases, StringComparer.Ordinal);
+        var staticTypes = new HashSet<string>(enclosing.StaticTypes, StringComparer.Ordinal);
+        var usings = container is BaseNamespaceDeclarationSyntax space ? space.Usings : ((CompilationUnitSyntax)container).Usings;
+        foreach (var directive in usings)
+        {
+            var target = RightmostName(directive.NamespaceOrType);
+            if (target is null)
+                continue;
+            if (directive.Alias is not null)
+                aliases[directive.Alias.Name.Identifier.ValueText] = target;
+            else if (directive.StaticKeyword.IsKind(SyntaxKind.StaticKeyword))
+                staticTypes.Add(target);
+        }
+
+        var scope = new ImportScope(aliases, staticTypes);
+        scopes[container] = scope;
+        return scope;
+    }
+
+    private static bool IsImportContainer(SyntaxNode node) => node is CompilationUnitSyntax or BaseNamespaceDeclarationSyntax;
+
+    private static IEnumerable<SyntaxNode> RepositoryLocatorNodes(CompilationUnitSyntax root)
+    {
+        foreach (var member in root.DescendantNodes().OfType<MemberDeclarationSyntax>()
+                     .Where(member => member is not BaseTypeDeclarationSyntax and not BaseNamespaceDeclarationSyntax and not DelegateDeclarationSyntax))
+        {
+            var nodes = member.DescendantNodes().ToArray();
+            if (nodes.Any(IsBaseDirectory) || nodes.OfType<ParameterSyntax>().Any(HasCallerFilePath))
+            {
+                var step = nodes.FirstOrDefault(IsParentStep);
+                if (step is not null)
+                    yield return step;
+            }
+
+            foreach (var walk in nodes.OfType<InvocationExpressionSyntax>().Where(IsRelativeWalkUp))
+                yield return walk;
+
+            // A read of the build metadata names the attribute type and the key in the same member.
+            if (nodes.OfType<SimpleNameSyntax>().Any(name => WithoutAttributeSuffix(name.Identifier.ValueText) == "AssemblyMetadata"))
+            {
+                foreach (var literal in nodes.OfType<LiteralExpressionSyntax>().Where(literal => IsStringLiteral(literal, "McpRepositoryRoot")))
+                    yield return literal;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    private static bool IsBaseDirectory(SyntaxNode node) =>
+        node is MemberAccessExpressionSyntax { Name.Identifier.ValueText: "BaseDirectory" } access
+        && RightmostName(access.Expression) == "AppContext";
+
+    private static bool HasCallerFilePath(ParameterSyntax parameter) =>
+        parameter.AttributeLists.SelectMany(list => list.Attributes)
+            .Any(attribute => WithoutAttributeSuffix(RightmostName(attribute.Name)) == "CallerFilePath");
+
+    private static bool IsParentStep(SyntaxNode node) => node switch
+    {
+        MemberAccessExpressionSyntax { Name.Identifier.ValueText: "Parent" } => true,
+        MemberBindingExpressionSyntax { Name.Identifier.ValueText: "Parent" } => true,
+        InvocationExpressionSyntax { Expression: MemberAccessExpressionSyntax { Name.Identifier.ValueText: "GetParent" } } => true,
+        _ => false,
+    };
+
+    private static bool IsRelativeWalkUp(InvocationExpressionSyntax invocation) =>
+        RightmostName(invocation.Expression) is "Combine" or "Join"
+        && invocation.ArgumentList.Arguments.Any(argument => IsBaseDirectory(argument.Expression))
+        && invocation.ArgumentList.Arguments.Any(argument =>
+            argument.Expression is LiteralExpressionSyntax literal
+            && literal.IsKind(SyntaxKind.StringLiteralExpression)
+            && literal.Token.ValueText.StartsWith("..", StringComparison.Ordinal));
+
+    private static IReadOnlyList<string> AttributeKinds(AttributeSyntax attribute, IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> aliases)
+    {
+        var name = WithoutAttributeSuffix(TypeName(attribute.Name, aliases));
+        var kinds = new List<string>();
+        if (name is "SupportedOSPlatform" or "UnsupportedOSPlatform")
+            kinds.Add("os-attribute");
+        if (name is "SkippableFact" or "SkippableTheory" || SetsSkipProperty(attribute))
+            kinds.Add("skip");
+        return kinds;
+    }
+
+    private static IReadOnlyList<string> MemberKinds(string? typeName, string member) =>
+        MemberRules.Where(rule => rule.Type == typeName && rule.Matches(member)).Select(rule => rule.Kind).ToArray();
+
+    private static IReadOnlyList<string> StaticImportKinds(string member, IReadOnlySet<string> staticTypes) =>
+        MemberRules.Where(rule => staticTypes.Contains(rule.Type) && rule.Matches(member)).Select(rule => rule.Kind).ToArray();
+
+    private static bool SetsSkipProperty(AttributeSyntax attribute) =>
+        attribute.ArgumentList is { } arguments
+        && arguments.Arguments.Any(argument =>
+            argument.NameEquals is { } nameEquals && nameEquals.Name.Identifier.ValueText.StartsWith("Skip", StringComparison.Ordinal));
+
+    /// <summary>The unit test class a hit counts against, or null when it is in no test class or in an integration test.</summary>
+    private static string? FindingOwner(SyntaxNode node)
+    {
+        if (node is AttributeSyntax { Parent: AttributeListSyntax { Target.Identifier.ValueText: "assembly" or "module" } })
+            return AssemblyOwner;
+
+        var testClass = node.Ancestors().OfType<TypeDeclarationSyntax>().FirstOrDefault(IsTestClass);
+        if (testClass is null
+            || testClass.AncestorsAndSelf().OfType<TypeDeclarationSyntax>().Any(type => HasIntegrationTrait(type.AttributeLists)))
+        {
+            return null;
+        }
+
+        var method = node.Ancestors().OfType<MethodDeclarationSyntax>().FirstOrDefault();
+        if (method is not null && IsTestMethod(method) && HasIntegrationTrait(method.AttributeLists))
+            return null;
+
+        return testClass.Identifier.ValueText;
+    }
+
+    private static bool IsTestClass(TypeDeclarationSyntax type) =>
+        type.Identifier.ValueText.EndsWith("Tests", StringComparison.Ordinal)
+        || type.Members.OfType<MethodDeclarationSyntax>().Any(IsTestMethod);
+
+    private static bool IsTestMethod(MethodDeclarationSyntax method) =>
+        method.AttributeLists.SelectMany(list => list.Attributes).Any(attribute =>
+            WithoutAttributeSuffix(RightmostName(attribute.Name)) is { } name
+            && (name.EndsWith("Fact", StringComparison.Ordinal) || name.EndsWith("Theory", StringComparison.Ordinal)));
+
+    private static bool HasIntegrationTrait(SyntaxList<AttributeListSyntax> lists) =>
+        lists.SelectMany(list => list.Attributes).Any(attribute =>
+            WithoutAttributeSuffix(RightmostName(attribute.Name)) == "Trait"
+            && attribute.ArgumentList is { Arguments.Count: >= 2 } arguments
+            && IsStringLiteral(arguments.Arguments[0].Expression, "Category")
+            && IsStringLiteral(arguments.Arguments[1].Expression, "Integration"));
+
+    private static bool IsStringLiteral(ExpressionSyntax expression, string value) =>
+        expression is LiteralExpressionSyntax literal
+        && literal.IsKind(SyntaxKind.StringLiteralExpression)
+        && literal.Token.ValueText == value;
+
+    /// <summary>True for a name that is not the member or right-hand part of a qualified name or access.</summary>
+    private static bool IsUnqualified(IdentifierNameSyntax identifier) => identifier.Parent switch
+    {
+        MemberAccessExpressionSyntax access => access.Name != identifier,
+        QualifiedNameSyntax qualified => qualified.Right != identifier,
+        AliasQualifiedNameSyntax => false,
+        MemberBindingExpressionSyntax => false,
+        NameEqualsSyntax or NameColonSyntax => false,
+        _ => true,
+    };
+
+    private static bool InNameOf(SyntaxNode node) =>
+        node.Ancestors().Any(ancestor =>
+            ancestor is InvocationExpressionSyntax { Expression: IdentifierNameSyntax { Identifier.ValueText: "nameof" } });
+
+    /// <summary>The rightmost name of a type or receiver expression, resolving a using alias in scope.</summary>
+    private static string? TypeName(SyntaxNode node, IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> aliases) =>
+        node is IdentifierNameSyntax identifier && aliases.TryGetValue(identifier.Identifier.ValueText, out var target)
+            ? target
+            : RightmostName(node);
+
+    private static string? RightmostName(SyntaxNode? node) => node switch
+    {
+        SimpleNameSyntax simple => simple.Identifier.ValueText,
+        QualifiedNameSyntax qualified => qualified.Right.Identifier.ValueText,
+        AliasQualifiedNameSyntax aliasQualified => aliasQualified.Name.Identifier.ValueText,
+        MemberAccessExpressionSyntax access => access.Name.Identifier.ValueText,
+        _ => null,
+    };
+
+    private static string? WithoutAttributeSuffix(string? name) =>
+        name is not null && name.Length > AttributeSuffix.Length && name.EndsWith(AttributeSuffix, StringComparison.Ordinal)
+            ? name[..^AttributeSuffix.Length]
+            : name;
+
+    private static int LineOf(SyntaxNode node) => node.GetLocation().GetLineSpan().StartLinePosition.Line + 1;
+
+    private sealed record MemberRule(string Kind, string Type, Func<string, bool> Matches);
+
+    /// <summary>Using aliases (alias to the target's rightmost name) and static-import types in effect in one scope.</summary>
+    private sealed record ImportScope(IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> Aliases, IReadOnlySet<string> StaticTypes)
+    {
+        internal static readonly ImportScope Empty =
+            new(new Dictionary<string, string>(StringComparer.Ordinal), new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.Ordinal));
+    }
+}
+
+/// <summary>TR-MCP-USECASE-018 (AC-006): one platform-dependent line in a unit test class.</summary>
+/// <param name="Path">Repository-relative source path.</param>
+/// <param name="Line">1-based line number.</param>
+/// <param name="Kind">os-check, os-attribute, skip, or process-host.</param>
+/// <param name="TestClass">Unit test class that holds the line, or <c>&lt;assembly&gt;</c> for an assembly- or module-level attribute.</param>
+internal sealed record UnitSuitePlatformFinding(string Path, int Line, string Kind, string TestClass)
+{
+    /// <inheritdoc />
+    public override string ToString() => $"{Path}:{Line} {Kind} in {TestClass}";
+}

+using System.Globalization;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests.Configuration;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-USECASE-018 (AC-006), TR-MCP-USECASE-019 (AC-008): the unit-suite platform scanner resolves a using alias
+/// or a <c>using static</c> import in the lexical scope of the code that uses it (the enclosing namespace, then the
+/// file), in the compiler's scope order, so an alias or import declared in one namespace neither hides nor invents a
+/// platform check in another namespace of the same file. An alias whose target is another alias and a
+/// <c>using static</c> that names an alias are not resolved, a declared limit (hostile validation round 4 finding
+/// R4-F1; operator 2026-10-21 "Declare the limit, no fix"; TODO IMPL-SCANNERALIAS-001), and no case here uses either form.
+/// </summary>
+/// <remarks>
+/// Found by hostile validation round 3 (finding R3-F2: the scanner collected every alias of a file into one dictionary,
+/// so a sibling namespace's <c>using OS = System.String</c> hid a real <c>OS.IsWindows()</c>); fix chosen by the
+/// operator on 2026-10-20 ("Structural fix, round 4 (Recommended)"). Test data: scripted C# sources with the findings
+/// the rule requires (the reviewer's counterexample, the same namespaces in reverse order, a nested namespace that
+/// overrides a file-level alias, a namespace-scoped static import, a file-scoped namespace alias, and an alias whose
+/// name is OperatingSystem but whose target is String). Negative controls are the outputs the file-wide resolution
+/// gave, each rejected by the finding assertion inside a passing harness.
+/// </remarks>
+public sealed class UnitSuitePlatformScannerAliasScopeTests
+{
+    private static readonly Dictionary<string, (string Source, string[] Expected)> Cases = new(StringComparer.Ordinal)
+    {
+        ["sibling-namespace-alias"] = ("""
+            namespace A
+            {
+                using OS = System.OperatingSystem;
+                public class AliasTests
+                {
+                    [Fact]
+                    public void A() { _ = OS.IsWindows(); }
+                }
+            }
+            namespace B
+            {
+                using OS = System.String;
+                public class Helper
+                {
+                    public OS Name { get; set; }
+                }
+            }
+            """, ["7 os-check AliasTests"]),
+        ["reverse-order-alias"] = ("""
+            namespace B
+            {
+                using OS = System.String;
+                public class StringTests
+                {
+                    [Fact]
+                    public void B() { _ = OS.IsNullOrEmpty("x"); }
+                }
+            }
+            namespace A
+            {
+                using OS = System.OperatingSystem;
+                public class AliasTests
+                {
+                    [Fact]
+                    public void A() { _ = OS.IsWindows(); }
+                }
+            }
+            """, ["16 os-check AliasTests"]),
+        ["nested-namespace-override"] = ("""
+            using OS = System.OperatingSystem;
+            namespace Outer
+            {
+                namespace Inner
+                {
+                    using OS = System.String;
+                    public class InnerTests
+                    {
+                        [Fact]
+                        public void A() { _ = OS.IsNullOrEmpty("x"); }
+                    }
+                }
+                public class OuterTests
+                {
+                    [Fact]
+                    public void B() { _ = OS.IsWindows(); }
+                }
+            }
+            """, ["16 os-check OuterTests"]),
+        ["namespace-static-import"] = ("""
+            namespace A
+            {
+                using static System.OperatingSystem;
+                public class StaticTests
+                {
+                    [Fact]
+                    public void A() { _ = IsWindows(); }
+                }
+            }
+            namespace B
+            {
+                public class OtherTests
+                {
+                    [Fact]
+                    public void B() { _ = IsWindows(); }
+                    private static bool IsWindows() => true;
+                }
+            }
+            """, ["7 os-check StaticTests"]),
+        ["file-scoped-namespace-alias"] = ("""
+            namespace N;
+            using OS = System.OperatingSystem;
+            public sealed class FileScopedTests
+            {
+                [Fact]
+                public void A() { _ = OS.IsLinux(); }
+            }
+            """, ["6 os-check FileScopedTests"]),
+        ["non-platform-alias-named-like-os"] = ("""
+            namespace N
+            {
+                using OperatingSystem = System.String;
+                public class StringTests
+                {
+                    [Fact]
+                    public void A() { _ = OperatingSystem.IsNullOrEmpty("x"); }
+                }
+            }
+            """, []),
+    };
+
+    /// <summary>The scripted case names, for the theories.</summary>
+    public static TheoryData<string> CaseNames =>
+        new()
+        {
+            "sibling-namespace-alias",
+            "reverse-order-alias",
+            "nested-namespace-override",
+            "namespace-static-import",
+            "file-scoped-namespace-alias",
+            "non-platform-alias-named-like-os",
+        };
+
+    /// <summary>Builds scanner findings from "line kind class" strings, as the scanner would report them.</summary>
+    private static UnitSuitePlatformFinding[] Findings(string path, IEnumerable<string> lines) =>
+        lines.Select(line => line.Split(' '))
+            .Select(parts => new UnitSuitePlatformFinding(path, int.Parse(parts[0], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture), parts[1], parts[2]))
+            .ToArray();
+
+    /// <summary>Mock gate positive control: each case's required findings pass the finding assertion.</summary>
+    /// <param name="name">Case name.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [MemberData(nameof(CaseNames))]
+    public void ScriptedCase_RequiredFindings_PassTheFindingAssertion(string name)
+    {
+        var expected = Cases[name].Expected;
+        UnitSuitePlatformScannerMockGateTests.AssertFindings(expected, Findings(name + ".cs", expected));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Production binding: the scanner gives each case exactly its required findings, so an alias or static import
+    /// declared in one namespace neither hides nor invents a finding in another.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="name">Case name.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [MemberData(nameof(CaseNames))]
+    public void ScriptedCase_Scanner_ReportsTheRequiredFindings(string name)
+    {
+        var (source, expected) = Cases[name];
+        UnitSuitePlatformScannerMockGateTests.AssertFindings(expected, UnitSuitePlatformScanner.Scan(name + ".cs", source));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>The theories cover every scripted case.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ScriptedTheory_CoversEveryCase() => Assert.Equal(6, Cases.Count);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Mock gate negative controls: the outputs the file-wide alias and import resolution gave are each rejected by
+    /// the finding assertion: no finding for the reviewer's counterexample (the sibling alias won), a false finding
+    /// on String.IsNullOrEmpty in reverse order (the later OperatingSystem alias won), no finding for the outer test
+    /// when an inner namespace overrides the alias, and a false finding on another namespace's own IsWindows method
+    /// when a static import was applied file-wide.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void FileWideResolutionOutputs_AreRejectedInsidePassingHarness()
+    {
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => UnitSuitePlatformScannerMockGateTests.AssertFindings(
+            Cases["sibling-namespace-alias"].Expected, Findings("sibling.cs", []))));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => UnitSuitePlatformScannerMockGateTests.AssertFindings(
+            Cases["reverse-order-alias"].Expected, Findings("reverse.cs", ["7 os-check StringTests", "16 os-check AliasTests"]))));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => UnitSuitePlatformScannerMockGateTests.AssertFindings(
+            Cases["nested-namespace-override"].Expected, Findings("nested.cs", []))));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => UnitSuitePlatformScannerMockGateTests.AssertFindings(
+            Cases["namespace-static-import"].Expected, Findings("static.cs", ["7 os-check StaticTests", "15 os-check OtherTests"]))));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => UnitSuitePlatformScannerMockGateTests.AssertFindings(
+            Cases["non-platform-alias-named-like-os"].Expected, Findings("named.cs", ["7 os-check StringTests"]))));
+    }
+}

+    /// <summary>Every scripted source yields exactly the findings the rule requires.</summary>
+    /// <param name="name">Case name.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("unit-os-check")]
+    [InlineData("integration-class")]
+    [InlineData("string-and-comment")]
+    [InlineData("os-attribute-before-class")]
+    [InlineData("skip-forms")]

+    [InlineData("abstract-contract-with-skip")]
+    [InlineData("integration-with-other-attributes")]
+    [InlineData("multiline-skip-attribute")]
+    [InlineData("qualified-skip-attribute")]
+    [InlineData("interpolation-holes")]
+    [InlineData("split-and-qualified-os-calls")]
+    [InlineData("qualified-and-listed-os-attributes")]
+    [InlineData("aliases-static-imports-and-method-groups")]
+    [InlineData("assembly-attribute")]
+    [InlineData("method-level-integration")]
+    [InlineData("skip-variants")]
+    [InlineData("conditional-compilation-branches")]
+    public void ScriptedSource_YieldsRuleFindings(string name)
+    {
+        var (source, expected) = Cases[name];
+        AssertFindings(expected, UnitSuitePlatformScanner.Scan(name + ".cs", source));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>The theory above covers every scripted case.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ScriptedTheory_CoversEveryCase() => Assert.Equal(24, Cases.Count);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Negative controls for the forms the syntax-tree scanner reads (structural fix after hostile validation
+    /// round 2): each new case's findings are not empty; counting a name inside <c>nameof</c> is rejected;
+    /// honoring the Integration trait only on classes (which also counts the method-level Integration test) is
+    /// rejected; and the output of one parse with no conditional symbols defined (which sees only the
+    /// <c>#else</c> branch) is rejected. A plain parse with no symbols has no <c>IsWindows</c> access at all,
+    /// which is why every branch combination is parsed.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void RoundThreeFormNegativeControls_AreRejectedInsidePassingHarness()
+    {
+        foreach (var name in new[] { "aliases-static-imports-and-method-groups", "assembly-attribute", "method-level-integration", "skip-variants", "conditional-compilation-branches" })
+        {
+            var expected = Cases[name].Expected;
+            Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertFindings(expected, [])));
+        }
+
+        var aliases = Cases["aliases-static-imports-and-method-groups"];
+        var aliasFindings = UnitSuitePlatformScanner.Scan("aliases.cs", aliases.Source);
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertFindings([.. aliases.Expected, "14 os-check FooTests"], aliasFindings)));
+        Assert.Null(Record.Exception(() => AssertFindings(aliases.Expected, aliasFindings)));
+
+        var methodLevel = Cases["method-level-integration"];
+        var methodFindings = UnitSuitePlatformScanner.Scan("method-level.cs", methodLevel.Source);
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertFindings(
+            ["6 os-attribute FooTests", "7 os-check FooTests", "9 os-check FooTests"], methodFindings)));
+        Assert.Null(Record.Exception(() => AssertFindings(methodLevel.Expected, methodFindings)));
+
+        var branches = Cases["conditional-compilation-branches"];
+        var branchFindings = UnitSuitePlatformScanner.Scan("branches.cs", branches.Source);
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertFindings(["10 os-check FooTests"], branchFindings)));
+        Assert.Null(Record.Exception(() => AssertFindings(branches.Expected, branchFindings)));
+        var plainParse = CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(branches.Source, cancellationToken: TestContext.Current.CancellationToken)
+            .GetRoot(TestContext.Current.CancellationToken);
+        Assert.DoesNotContain(
+            plainParse.DescendantNodes().OfType<MemberAccessExpressionSyntax>(),
+            access => access.Name.Identifier.ValueText == "IsWindows");
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// A file that names more conditional-compilation symbols than the scanner parses combinations for fails the
+    /// scan and the skip definition instead of leaving a branch unread; a file at the limit is read.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ConditionalSymbolsBeyondTheLimit_FailClosed()
+    {
+        static string SourceWith(int symbolCount) =>
+            "#if " + string.Join(" || ", Enumerable.Range(1, symbolCount).Select(index => "S" + index.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)))
+            + "\npublic sealed class FooTests\n{\n    [Fact]\n    public void A() => Assert.Skip(\"s\");\n}\n#endif\n";
+
+        var over = SourceWith(UnitSuitePlatformScanner.MaxConditionalSymbols + 1);
+        Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => UnitSuitePlatformScanner.Scan("over.cs", over));
+        Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => UnitSuitePlatformScanner.FindSkipLines(over));
+
+        var atLimit = SourceWith(UnitSuitePlatformScanner.MaxConditionalSymbols);
+        AssertFindings(["5 skip FooTests"], UnitSuitePlatformScanner.Scan("limit.cs", atLimit));
+        Assert.Equal(new[] { 5 }, UnitSuitePlatformScanner.FindSkipLines(atLimit));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Negative controls for the cases added after hostile validation round 2 (finding R2-F2): the outputs
+    /// the line-based scanner gave for a member call split after the dot and for a qualified or listed OS
+    /// attribute (no findings), an output that drops the global-qualified call, and an output that also
+    /// counts an OS attribute name inside a string argument are each rejected by the finding assertion.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void RoundTwoCounterexampleNegativeControls_AreRejectedInsidePassingHarness()
+    {
+        foreach (var name in new[] { "split-and-qualified-os-calls", "qualified-and-listed-os-attributes" })
+        {
+            var expected = Cases[name].Expected;
+            Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertFindings(expected, [])));
+        }
+
+        var calls = UnitSuitePlatformScanner.Scan("split-and-qualified-os-calls.cs", Cases["split-and-qualified-os-calls"].Source);
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertFindings(
+            ["7 os-check FooTests", "9 os-check FooTests", "12 os-check FooTests", "14 os-check FooTests"], calls)));
+        Assert.Null(Record.Exception(() => AssertFindings(Cases["split-and-qualified-os-calls"].Expected, calls)));
+
+        var attributes = UnitSuitePlatformScanner.Scan("qualified-and-listed-os-attributes.cs", Cases["qualified-and-listed-os-attributes"].Source);
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertFindings(
+            ["4 os-attribute FooTests", "7 os-attribute FooTests", "10 os-attribute FooTests", "12 os-attribute FooTests"], attributes)));
+        Assert.Null(Record.Exception(() => AssertFindings(Cases["qualified-and-listed-os-attributes"].Expected, attributes)));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Negative controls for the cases added after hostile validation round 1 (finding F3): the outputs a
+    /// line-based skip match, an unqualified attribute match, and a literal-blanking interpolation strip gave
+    /// (no findings), and an output that also counts the literal text outside a hole or an escaped-brace
+    /// block, are each rejected by the finding assertion.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void RoundOneCounterexampleNegativeControls_AreRejectedInsidePassingHarness()
+    {
+        foreach (var name in new[] { "multiline-skip-attribute", "qualified-skip-attribute", "interpolation-holes" })
+        {
+            var expected = Cases[name].Expected;
+            Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertFindings(expected, [])));
+        }
+
+        var interpolation = UnitSuitePlatformScanner.Scan("interpolation-holes.cs", Cases["interpolation-holes"].Source);
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertFindings(
+            ["7 os-check FooTests", "8 os-check FooTests", "9 os-check FooTests", "10 os-check FooTests"], interpolation)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertFindings(
+            ["7 os-check FooTests", "9 os-check FooTests", "10 os-check FooTests", "11 os-check FooTests"], interpolation)));
+        Assert.Null(Record.Exception(() => AssertFindings(Cases["interpolation-holes"].Expected, interpolation)));
+
+        var qualified = UnitSuitePlatformScanner.Scan("qualified-skip-attribute.cs", Cases["qualified-skip-attribute"].Source);
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertFindings(
+            ["4 skip FooTests", "6 skip FooTests", "8 skip FooTests", "10 skip FooTests"], qualified)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertFindings(
+            ["4 skip FooTests", "6 skip FooTests", "8 skip FooTests", "12 skip FooTests"], qualified)));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Negative controls: the finding assertion rejects a missing finding, an extra finding, a finding on
+    /// the wrong line, and a finding attributed to the wrong class.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void FindingAssertionNegativeControls_AreRejectedInsidePassingHarness()
+    {
+        var actual = UnitSuitePlatformScanner.Scan("skip-forms.cs", Cases["skip-forms"].Source);
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertFindings(["4 skip FooTests", "9 skip FooTests", "10 skip FooTests"], actual)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertFindings(["4 skip FooTests", "9 skip FooTests", "10 skip FooTests", "11 skip FooTests", "12 skip FooTests"], actual)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertFindings(["4 skip FooTests", "9 skip FooTests", "10 skip FooTests", "12 skip FooTests"], actual)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertFindings(["4 skip FooTests", "9 skip FooTests", "10 skip FooTests", "11 skip BarTests"], actual)));
+        Assert.Null(Record.Exception(() => AssertFindings(["4 skip FooTests", "9 skip FooTests", "10 skip FooTests", "11 skip FooTests"], actual)));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Literals and comments never yield a finding or a skip line: every rule's text placed only inside
+    /// regular, verbatim, raw and interpolated string text, character literals, line comments, block comments
+    /// and XML documentation is ignored, while the same text as code in the last line is found.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void LiteralsAndComments_NeverYieldFindings()
+    {
+        const string source = """"
+            namespace N;
+            public sealed class FooTests
+            {
+                /// <summary>OperatingSystem.IsWindows() [SupportedOSPlatform("x")] Assert.Skip("y")</summary>
+                [Fact]
+                public void A()
+                {
+                    var a = "OperatingSystem.IsWindows() RuntimeInformation.IsOSPlatform(p)";
+                    var b = @"[Fact(Skip = ""x"")] SkipException SystemWorkspaceHostEnvironment.Instance";
+                    var c = """[SupportedOSPlatform("linux")] WorkspacePathNormalizer.Process""";
+                    var d = $"text OperatingSystem.IsLinux() {{Assert.SkipWhen(true, w)}}";
+                    var e = '[';
+                    // OperatingSystem.IsMacOS(); Assert.SkipUnless(false, "u");
+                    /* [SkippableFact] [UnsupportedOSPlatform("ios")] */
+                    _ = OperatingSystem.IsLinux();
+                }
+            }
+            """";
+        AssertFindings(["15 os-check FooTests"], UnitSuitePlatformScanner.Scan("literals.cs", source));
+        Assert.Empty(UnitSuitePlatformScanner.FindSkipLines(source));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Scripted sources for the DRY guard <see cref="UnitSuitePlatformScanner.FindRepositoryLocators"/>: a locator is a
+    /// walk up (<c>.Parent</c>, <c>?.Parent</c>, <c>Directory.GetParent</c>) in a member that starts from the test output
+    /// or the caller's source path, a relative walk up from the test output, or a read of the <c>McpRepositoryRoot</c>
+    /// build metadata, in any type. A fixture read from the test output, a walk up from another directory, a call to
+    /// the shared locator, and the same text in comments or strings are not locators.
+    /// </summary>
+    private static readonly Dictionary<string, (string Source, int[] Expected)> LocatorCases = new(StringComparer.Ordinal)
+    {
+        ["walk-up-from-output"] = ("""
+            namespace N;
+            internal static class Paths
+            {
+                internal static string Root()
+                {
+                    var directory = new DirectoryInfo(AppContext.BaseDirectory);
+                    while (directory is not null && !File.Exists(Path.Combine(directory.FullName, "X.sln")))
+                        directory = directory.Parent;
+                    return directory!.FullName;
+                }
+            }
+            """, [8]),
+        ["conditional-walk-up-in-test-class"] = ("""
+            namespace N;
+            public sealed class FooTests
+            {
+                private static string Root()
+                {
+                    for (var d = new DirectoryInfo(AppContext.BaseDirectory); d is not null; d = d?.Parent)
+                    {
+                        if (File.Exists(Path.Combine(d.FullName, "X.sln"))) return d.FullName;
+                    }
+                    throw new InvalidOperationException();
+                }
+            }
+            """, [6]),
+        ["relative-walk-up"] = ("""
+            namespace N;
+            public sealed class FooTests
+            {
+                [Fact]
+                public void A()
+                {
+                    var src = Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "..", "..", "src"));
+                    var up = Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "../../docs");
+                }
+            }
+            """, [7, 8]),
+        ["caller-file-path"] = ("""
+            using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;
+            namespace N;
+            internal static class Paths
+            {
+                internal static string Root([CallerFilePath] string path = "") =>
+                    new FileInfo(path).Directory!.Parent!.FullName;
+            }
+            """, [6]),
+        ["build-metadata"] = ("""
+            namespace N;
+            internal static class Paths
+            {
+                internal static string? Root() => typeof(Paths).Assembly
+                    .GetCustomAttributes<System.Reflection.AssemblyMetadataAttribute>()
+                    .FirstOrDefault(a => a.Key == "McpRepositoryRoot")?.Value;
+            }
+            """, [6]),
+        ["not-locators"] = ("""
+            namespace N;
+            public sealed class FooTests
+            {
+                private static string Fixture(string name) => Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "Fixtures", name);
+                private static string Up(string temp) => new DirectoryInfo(temp).Parent!.FullName;
+                private static string Shared() => TestRepositoryRoot.Resolve();
+                // var d = new DirectoryInfo(AppContext.BaseDirectory); d = d.Parent;
+                private const string Text = "McpRepositoryRoot AppContext.BaseDirectory .Parent";
+                private static bool IsAnchorKey(string key) => key == "McpRepositoryRoot";
+            }
+            """, []),
+    };
+
+    /// <summary>Every scripted source yields exactly the locator lines the DRY rule requires.</summary>
+    /// <param name="name">Case name.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("walk-up-from-output")]
+    [InlineData("conditional-walk-up-in-test-class")]
+    [InlineData("relative-walk-up")]
+    [InlineData("caller-file-path")]
+    [InlineData("build-metadata")]
+    [InlineData("not-locators")]
+    public void ScriptedSource_YieldsRepositoryLocatorLines(string name)
+    {
+        var (source, expected) = LocatorCases[name];
+        Assert.Equal(expected, UnitSuitePlatformScanner.FindRepositoryLocators(source));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>The locator theory above covers every scripted case.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void LocatorTheory_CoversEveryCase() => Assert.Equal(6, LocatorCases.Count);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Negative controls for the DRY guard: an output with no locator lines, one that drops the conditional
+    /// <c>?.Parent</c> step, and one that also counts the fixture read from the test output are each rejected.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void LocatorNegativeControls_AreRejectedInsidePassingHarness()
+    {
+        var relative = UnitSuitePlatformScanner.FindRepositoryLocators(LocatorCases["relative-walk-up"].Source);
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => Assert.Equal(new[] { 7, 8 }, Array.Empty<int>())));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => Assert.Equal(new[] { 7 }, relative)));
+        Assert.Null(Record.Exception(() => Assert.Equal(new[] { 7, 8 }, relative)));
+
+        var conditional = UnitSuitePlatformScanner.FindRepositoryLocators(LocatorCases["conditional-walk-up-in-test-class"].Source);
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => Assert.Equal(Array.Empty<int>(), conditional)));
+
+        var notLocators = UnitSuitePlatformScanner.FindRepositoryLocators(LocatorCases["not-locators"].Source);
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => Assert.Equal(new[] { 4 }, notLocators)));
+        Assert.Null(Record.Exception(() => Assert.Equal(Array.Empty<int>(), notLocators)));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Assertion shared by the scripted cases and the negative controls.</summary>
+    /// <param name="expected">Findings as "line kind class".</param>
+    /// <param name="actual">Scanner output.</param>
+    internal static void AssertFindings(IReadOnlyList<string> expected, IReadOnlyList<UnitSuitePlatformFinding> actual) =>
+        Assert.Equal(expected, actual.Select(finding => $"{finding.Line} {finding.Kind} {finding.TestClass}").ToArray());
+}
