+using QBrainAi.Tests.Shared;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests.Configuration;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-USECASE-018 (AC-006), TR-MCP-USECASE-019 (AC-008): binds <see cref="UnitSuitePlatformScanner"/>,
+/// unchanged from its mock gate, to the C# sources of every project the Nuke <c>Test</c> target runs
+/// (<c>build/Build.Test.cs</c>: test projects except integration, plugin-integration, review and Build.Tests
+/// projects). A test that depends on the platform is an integration test, so the unit tests hold no
+/// operating-system check, OS-platform attribute, xUnit skip, or real process-host binding. These tests
+/// read the repository checkout, so they are integration tests on both hosts and run in each host's
+/// MigrationIntegrationTests (hostile validation round 2, finding R2-F1); the scripted cases in
+/// <see cref="UnitSuitePlatformScannerMockGateTests"/> stay unit tests.
+/// </summary>
+[Trait("Category", "Integration")]
+public sealed class UnitSuitePlatformScannerTests
+{
+    private static readonly string[] ExpectedUnitProjects =
+    [
+        "QBrainAi.Client.Tests",
+        "QBrainAi.Cqrs.Tests",
+        "QBrainAi.Launcher.Tests",
+        "QBrainAi.McpAgent.Tests",
+        "QBrainAi.QBAgent.Tests",
+        "QBrainAi.Repl.Core.Tests",
+        "QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests",
+    ];
+
+    /// <summary>No unit test class in the unit-suite projects depends on the platform.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void UnitTestClasses_HaveNoPlatformDependence()
+    {
+        var testsRoot = FindTestsRoot();
+        var findings = UnitProjects(testsRoot)
+            .SelectMany(project => Directory.EnumerateFiles(project, "*.cs", SearchOption.AllDirectories))
+            .Where(path => !IsBuildOutput(path))
+            .OrderBy(path => path, StringComparer.Ordinal)
+            .SelectMany(path => UnitSuitePlatformScanner.Scan(
+                Path.GetRelativePath(testsRoot, path).Replace('\\', '/'),
+                File.ReadAllText(path)))
+            .Select(finding => finding.ToString())
+            .ToArray();
+
+        Assert.True(
+            findings.Length == 0,
+            "A test that depends on the platform is an integration test: tag the test method or its class [Trait(\"Category\", \"Integration\")] "
+            + "(with [Trait(\"Platform\", ...)] when it applies to one host) or remove the dependence. Findings:"
+            + Environment.NewLine + string.Join(Environment.NewLine, findings));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Pins the projects the guard scans to the seven the Nuke <c>Test</c> target ran when this was written
+    /// (<c>build/Build.Test.cs:16-24</c>): a test project added to or removed from <c>tests/</c> changes the
+    /// guard's selection and fails here. Limit: this test does not read <c>build/Build.Test.cs</c>, so a
+    /// change confined to that target's selection leaves it green and must be mirrored here by hand.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void GuardProjectSelection_MatchesTheUnitTestTarget()
+    {
+        var names = UnitProjects(FindTestsRoot()).Select(directory => new DirectoryInfo(directory).Name).Order(StringComparer.Ordinal).ToArray();
+        Assert.Equal(ExpectedUnitProjects, names);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// DRY: no source in the unit-suite projects holds a repository-root locator beside the shared
+    /// <c>tests/Shared/TestRepositoryRoot.cs</c>, and the guard does find the shared locator (positive control on the
+    /// real file).
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void UnitProjects_HoldNoRepositoryLocatorBesideTheSharedOne()
+    {
+        var testsRoot = FindTestsRoot();
+        var findings = UnitProjects(testsRoot)
+            .SelectMany(project => Directory.EnumerateFiles(project, "*.cs", SearchOption.AllDirectories))
+            .Where(path => !IsBuildOutput(path))
+            .OrderBy(path => path, StringComparer.Ordinal)
+            .SelectMany(path => UnitSuitePlatformScanner.FindRepositoryLocators(File.ReadAllText(path))
+                .Select(line => $"{Path.GetRelativePath(testsRoot, path).Replace('\\', '/')}:{line}"))
+            .ToArray();
+
+        Assert.True(
+            findings.Length == 0,
+            "Locate the repository with the shared TestRepositoryRoot (tests/Shared/TestRepositoryRoot.cs) instead of a "
+            + "local walk up. Findings:" + Environment.NewLine + string.Join(Environment.NewLine, findings));
+        Assert.NotEmpty(UnitSuitePlatformScanner.FindRepositoryLocators(
+            File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(testsRoot, "Shared", "TestRepositoryRoot.cs"))));
+    }
+
+    private static IEnumerable<string> UnitProjects(string testsRoot) =>
+        Directory.EnumerateDirectories(testsRoot)
+            .Where(directory =>
+            {
+                var name = new DirectoryInfo(directory).Name;
+                return name.EndsWith(".Tests", StringComparison.Ordinal)
+                    && !name.Contains("IntegrationTests", StringComparison.Ordinal)
+                    && !name.Contains("PluginIntegration", StringComparison.Ordinal)
+                    && !name.Contains("Review.Tests", StringComparison.Ordinal)
+                    && !name.EndsWith("Build.Tests", StringComparison.Ordinal)
+                    && Directory.EnumerateFiles(directory, "*.csproj").Any();
+            });
+
+    private static bool IsBuildOutput(string path)
+    {
+        var separator = Path.DirectorySeparatorChar;
+        return path.Contains($"{separator}bin{separator}", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
+            || path.Contains($"{separator}obj{separator}", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
+    }
+
+    private static string FindTestsRoot() => TestRepositoryRoot.Combine("tests");
+}

+using QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Services;
+using QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests;
+using Xunit;
+
+[assembly: AssemblyFixture(typeof(HermeticUserProfileFixture))]
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-WS-005 / AC-TR-MCP-WS-005-001: gives marker agent-plugin version resolution test-owned sources for the whole
+/// test assembly: no plugin-root environment variable, no folder beside the workspace, and an empty temporary profile,
+/// so no test in this assembly reads the environment, the machine or the plugin caches of the user who runs it
+/// (operator 2026-10-20: "Fix scans in PR 87 (Recommended)" and "Structural fix, round 4 (Recommended)"; unit tests
+/// may not depend on the environment).
+/// </summary>
+/// <remarks>
+/// Test data: one empty directory under the system temp folder, created when the assembly starts and deleted when
+/// it ends. The sources are set once here and never changed at run time; tests that exercise resolution with other
+/// data pass their own <see cref="AgentPluginVersionSources"/> to the resolution they call.
+/// </remarks>
+public sealed class HermeticUserProfileFixture : IDisposable
+{
+    /// <summary>Creates the empty profile and installs the hermetic sources.</summary>
+    public HermeticUserProfileFixture()
+    {
+        Profile = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"mcp-hermetic-profile-{Guid.NewGuid():N}");
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(Profile);
+        Sources = new AgentPluginVersionSources(_ => null, (_, _) => null, Profile);
+        MarkerFileService.AgentPluginVersionSourcesOverride = Sources;
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>The empty temporary profile used for the whole assembly.</summary>
+    public string Profile { get; }
+
+    /// <summary>The hermetic sources installed for the whole assembly.</summary>
+    internal AgentPluginVersionSources Sources { get; }
+
+    /// <summary>Clears the override when it still holds these sources, and deletes the profile.</summary>
+    public void Dispose()
+    {
+        if (ReferenceEquals(MarkerFileService.AgentPluginVersionSourcesOverride, Sources))
+            MarkerFileService.AgentPluginVersionSourcesOverride = null;
+        if (Directory.Exists(Profile))
+            Directory.Delete(Profile, recursive: true);
+    }
+}
diff --git a/tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/HermeticUserProfileFixtureTests.cs b/tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/HermeticUserProfileFixtureTests.cs
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..161f481c
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/HermeticUserProfileFixtureTests.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,82 @@
+using QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Services;
+using Xunit;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Tests that <see cref="HermeticUserProfileFixture"/> is in effect for this assembly: marker agent-plugin version
+/// resolution reads the fixture's sources, which resolve no plugin-root variable, offer no folder beside the workspace,
+/// and point at an existing, empty directory under the system temp folder, never the real user profile.
+/// </summary>
+/// <remarks>
+/// Requirement coverage: TR-MCP-WS-005 / AC-TR-MCP-WS-005-001 (operator 2026-10-20: "Fix scans in PR 87
+/// (Recommended)" and "Structural fix, round 4 (Recommended)"). Test data: the assembly fixture's profile and sources,
+/// injected; negative controls feed the profile assertion an unset override, a directory outside the temp folder, and
+/// a temp directory that holds a plugin cache, and prove the assertion rejects each (the source-level controls are in
+/// <c>AgentPluginVersionSourcesTests</c>). No test changes the live override at run time, so it is checked to be the
+/// fixture's own sources.
+/// </remarks>
+public sealed class HermeticUserProfileFixtureTests
+{
+    private readonly HermeticUserProfileFixture _fixture;
+
+    /// <summary>Receives the assembly fixture.</summary>
+    /// <param name="fixture">The assembly-wide hermetic profile.</param>
+    public HermeticUserProfileFixtureTests(HermeticUserProfileFixture fixture) => _fixture = fixture;
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Asserts that <paramref name="profile"/> is a hermetic plugin-version profile: set, under
+    /// <paramref name="tempRoot"/>, existing, and empty.
+    /// </summary>
+    private static void AssertHermetic(string? profile, string tempRoot)
+    {
+        Assert.False(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(profile));
+        Assert.StartsWith(Path.GetFullPath(tempRoot), Path.GetFullPath(profile), StringComparison.Ordinal);
+        Assert.True(Directory.Exists(profile));
+        Assert.Empty(Directory.EnumerateFileSystemEntries(profile));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// The fixture's profile is an existing, empty directory under the temp folder, and the live override is the
+    /// fixture's sources, which pass the source hermeticity assertion, so plugin-version resolution never reads the
+    /// environment, the machine or the real user profile.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void AgentPluginVersionSourcesOverride_IsTheHermeticAssemblySources()
+    {
+        AssertHermetic(_fixture.Profile, Path.GetTempPath());
+
+        var live = MarkerFileService.AgentPluginVersionSourcesOverride;
+        Assert.Same(_fixture.Sources, live);
+        QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests.Services.AgentPluginVersionSourcesTests.AssertHermetic(live!);
+        Assert.Equal(_fixture.Profile, live!.UserProfile);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Negative controls: an unset override, a profile outside the temp folder, and a temp profile that holds a
+    /// plugin cache are each rejected by the hermeticity assertion, inside a passing harness.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void HermeticityNegativeControls_AreRejectedInsidePassingHarness()
+    {
+        var tempRoot = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"mcp-hermetic-controls-{Guid.NewGuid():N}");
+        var outside = Path.Combine(tempRoot, "outside");
+        var temp = Path.Combine(tempRoot, "temp");
+        var empty = Path.Combine(temp, "empty");
+        var populated = Path.Combine(temp, "populated");
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(outside);
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(empty);
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.Combine(populated, ".claude", "plugins", "cache"));
+        try
+        {
+            Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertHermetic(null, temp)));
+            Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertHermetic(outside, temp)));
+            Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertHermetic(populated, temp)));
+            Assert.Null(Record.Exception(() => AssertHermetic(empty, temp)));
+        }
+        finally
+        {
+            Directory.Delete(tempRoot, recursive: true);
+        }
+    }
+}

diff --git a/tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/Services/AgentPluginVersionSourcesTests.cs b/tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/Services/AgentPluginVersionSourcesTests.cs
new file mode 100644

+++ b/tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/Services/AgentPluginVersionSourcesTests.cs

+using QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Services;
+using Xunit;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests.Services;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Tests that agent-plugin version resolution reads only the sources it is given (<see cref="AgentPluginVersionSources"/>):
+/// the plugin root an environment variable names, then the plugin folder beside the workspace, then the plugin caches
+/// under the user profile, then the synced version; and that the sources the unit-test assembly installs are hermetic
+/// (no plugin-root variable, no sibling folder, an empty temporary profile).
+/// </summary>
+/// <remarks>
+/// Requirement coverage: TR-MCP-WS-005 / AC-TR-MCP-WS-005-001 (unit tests take every plugin-version input from
+/// test-owned data). Found by hostile validation round 3 (finding R3-F1: the assembly fixture replaced only the
+/// profile, so a plugin-root variable reached 19 unit tests); fix chosen by the operator on 2026-10-20 ("Structural
+/// fix, round 4 (Recommended)"). Test data: a temporary root per test holding test-owned plugin folders with fixed
+/// versions (environment root 4.0.0, sibling root 3.0.0, profile cache 2.0.0) and a stand-in for the machine (a
+/// plugin-root variable at 9.0.0, the folder beside the workspace at 8.0.0, a profile cache at 7.0.0); no real
+/// environment variable, machine folder or profile is read. Expected versions follow the criterion's reading order.
+/// Negative controls run deliberately wrong resolutions that read one machine input instead of the given source, and
+/// hermeticity controls reject sources that name a plugin root, compute a sibling, or point at a populated or
+/// non-temporary profile.
+/// </remarks>
+public sealed class AgentPluginVersionSourcesTests : IDisposable
+{
+    private const string Variable = "CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT";
+
+    private static readonly string[] PluginRootVariables =
+        ["CODEX_PLUGIN_ROOT", "CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT", "COPILOT_PLUGIN_ROOT", "CLINE_PLUGIN_ROOT", "GROK_PLUGIN_ROOT"];
+
+    private readonly string _root = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"mcp-plugin-sources-{Guid.NewGuid():N}");
+
+    private readonly string _plugin = $"mcpserver-sources-test-{Guid.NewGuid():N}";
+
+    /// <summary>A resolution under test: resolves a plugin version for a workspace from the given sources.</summary>
+    private delegate string ResolveFunction(string workspacePath, string pluginName, string variable, AgentPluginVersionSources sources);
+
+    /// <summary>Creates the test-owned plugin folders and the machine stand-in under a temporary root.</summary>
+    public AgentPluginVersionSourcesTests()
+    {
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(Workspace);
+        WriteVersion(EnvironmentRoot, "4.0.0");
+        WriteVersion(GivenSibling, "3.0.0");
+        WriteVersion(Path.Combine(GivenProfile, ".claude", "plugins", "cache", _plugin, "2.0.0"), "2.0.0");
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(EmptyProfile);
+        WriteVersion(MachineEnvironmentRoot, "9.0.0");
+        WriteVersion(Path.Combine(_root, _plugin), "8.0.0");
+        WriteVersion(Path.Combine(MachineProfile, ".claude", "plugins", "cache", _plugin, "7.0.0"), "7.0.0");
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Deletes the temporary root.</summary>
+    public void Dispose()
+    {
+        if (Directory.Exists(_root))
+            Directory.Delete(_root, recursive: true);
+    }
+
+    private string Workspace => Path.Combine(_root, "workspace");
+
+    private string EnvironmentRoot => Path.Combine(_root, "given-environment-root");
+
+    private string GivenSibling => Path.Combine(_root, "elsewhere", _plugin);
+
+    private string GivenProfile => Path.Combine(_root, "given-profile");
+
+    private string EmptyProfile => Path.Combine(_root, "empty-profile");
+
+    private string MachineEnvironmentRoot => Path.Combine(_root, "machine-environment-root");
+
+    private string MachineProfile => Path.Combine(_root, "machine-profile");
+
+    /// <summary>The stand-in for the machine: what a resolution that ignores its sources would read instead.</summary>
+    private AgentPluginVersionSources Machine => new(
+        name => name == Variable ? MachineEnvironmentRoot : null,
+        (workspace, plugin) => Path.Combine(Path.GetDirectoryName(Path.GetFullPath(workspace))!, plugin),
+        MachineProfile);
+
+    private static void WriteVersion(string directory, string version)
+    {
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
+        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(directory, ".version"), version);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>The scripted cases: the given sources and the version the criterion requires.</summary>
+    private (AgentPluginVersionSources Sources, string Version) Case(string name) => name switch
+    {
+        "environment-root-first" => (new(v => v == Variable ? EnvironmentRoot : null, (_, _) => GivenSibling, GivenProfile), "4.0.0"),
+        "sibling-root-next" => (new(_ => null, (_, _) => GivenSibling, GivenProfile), "3.0.0"),
+        "profile-cache-next" => (new(_ => null, (_, _) => null, GivenProfile), "2.0.0"),
+        "nothing-falls-back" => (new(_ => null, (_, _) => null, EmptyProfile), MarkerFileService.SyncedAgentPluginVersion),
+        "no-profile" => (new(_ => null, (_, _) => null, null), MarkerFileService.SyncedAgentPluginVersion),
+        _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(name), name, "Unknown case."),
+    };
+
+    /// <summary>The scripted case names, for the theories.</summary>
+    public static TheoryData<string> CaseNames =>
+        new() { "environment-root-first", "sibling-root-next", "profile-cache-next", "nothing-falls-back", "no-profile" };
+
+    /// <summary>Asserts one resolution result against the version a case requires.</summary>
+    private static void AssertVersion(string expected, string actual) => Assert.Equal(expected, actual);
+
+    private void RunAndAssert(string name, ResolveFunction resolve)
+    {
+        var (sources, version) = Case(name);
+        AssertVersion(version, resolve(Workspace, _plugin, Variable, sources));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// A model of resolution that reads the plugin roots in the criterion's order from the sources it is handed:
+    /// the environment root, the sibling root, then the Claude plugin cache versions under the profile, newest first.
+    /// </summary>
+    private static string ModelResolve(string workspacePath, string pluginName, string variable, AgentPluginVersionSources sources)
+    {
+        foreach (var root in Roots())
+        {
+            var version = MarkerFileService.TryReadAgentPluginVersion(root, MarkerFileService.ReadExistingFileText);
+            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(version))
+                return version;
+        }
+
+        return MarkerFileService.SyncedAgentPluginVersion;
+
+        IEnumerable<string> Roots()
+        {
+            if (sources.EnvironmentVariable(variable) is { Length: > 0 } environmentRoot)
+                yield return environmentRoot;
+            if (sources.SiblingRoot(workspacePath, pluginName) is { Length: > 0 } siblingRoot)
+                yield return siblingRoot;
+            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sources.UserProfile))
+            {
+                var cache = Path.Combine(sources.UserProfile, ".claude", "plugins", "cache", pluginName);
+                if (Directory.Exists(cache))
+                {
+                    foreach (var candidate in Directory.EnumerateDirectories(cache).Order(StringComparer.Ordinal).Reverse())
+                        yield return candidate;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Mock gate positive control: each case's required version passes the version assertion.</summary>
+    /// <param name="name">Scripted case name.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [MemberData(nameof(CaseNames))]
+    public void ScriptedCase_RequiredVersion_PassesTheVersionAssertion(string name) =>
+        AssertVersion(Case(name).Version, Case(name).Version);
+
+    /// <summary>Mock gate positive control: the model that reads only its given sources yields every required version.</summary>
+    /// <param name="name">Scripted case name.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [MemberData(nameof(CaseNames))]
+    public void ScriptedCase_SourceOnlyModel_PassesTheVersionAssertion(string name) =>
+        RunAndAssert(name, ModelResolve);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Production binding: <c>MarkerFileService.ResolveAgentPluginVersion</c> with the file-system walk reads only the
+    /// sources it is given and yields every required version, while the machine stand-in holds a
+    /// different version at each input.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="name">Scripted case name.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [MemberData(nameof(CaseNames))]
+    public void ScriptedCase_ProductionResolution_YieldsTheRequiredVersion(string name) =>
+        RunAndAssert(name, (w, p, v, s) => MarkerFileService.ResolveAgentPluginVersion(w, p, v, s, DirectoryTreeWalker.FileSystem));
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Production binding: the marker's agent-plugin contract takes every version from the sources it is given: the
+    /// agent whose variable resolves gets that root's version, and every other agent gets the given sibling's version.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void BuildDefaultAgentPlugins_GivenSources_ResolvesEveryAgentFromThem()
+    {
+        var (sources, _) = Case("environment-root-first");
+
+        var plugins = MarkerFileService.BuildDefaultAgentPlugins(Workspace, sources);
+
+        Assert.Equal(5, plugins.Agents.Count);
+        Assert.Equal("4.0.0", plugins.Agents["Claude"].PluginVersion);
+        Assert.All(plugins.Agents.Where(pair => pair.Key != "Claude"), pair => Assert.Equal("3.0.0", pair.Value.PluginVersion));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Production binding: the sources the unit-test assembly installs, which every default marker build in this
+    /// assembly reads, pass the hermeticity assertion.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void AssemblySources_AreHermetic()
+    {
+        var live = MarkerFileService.AgentPluginVersionSourcesOverride;
+
+        Assert.NotNull(live);
+        AssertHermetic(live);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Mock gate negative controls: a resolution that reads the machine's plugin-root variable, one that computes the
+    /// folder beside the workspace, one that scans the machine's profile, and one that ignores every given source are
+    /// each rejected by the version assertion on the case that exposes it, inside a passing harness.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void MachineReadingNegativeControls_AreRejectedInsidePassingHarness()
+    {
+        var machine = Machine;
+        ResolveFunction readsMachineEnvironment = (w, p, v, s) => ModelResolve(w, p, v, s with { EnvironmentVariable = machine.EnvironmentVariable });
+        ResolveFunction computesSibling = (w, p, v, s) => ModelResolve(w, p, v, s with { SiblingRoot = machine.SiblingRoot });
+        ResolveFunction readsMachineProfile = (w, p, v, s) => ModelResolve(w, p, v, s with { UserProfile = machine.UserProfile });
+        ResolveFunction ignoresSources = (w, p, v, _) => ModelResolve(w, p, v, machine);
+
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => RunAndAssert("environment-root-first", readsMachineEnvironment)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => RunAndAssert("profile-cache-next", computesSibling)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => RunAndAssert("nothing-falls-back", readsMachineProfile)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => RunAndAssert("no-profile", readsMachineProfile)));
+        foreach (var name in new[] { "environment-root-first", "sibling-root-next", "profile-cache-next", "nothing-falls-back", "no-profile" })
+            Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => RunAndAssert(name, ignoresSources)));
+
+        // Each wrong resolution is wrong only where its machine input is reached: when an earlier given source already
+        // yields a version, it returns the required one, so the rejections above come from the input it reads.
+        Assert.Null(Record.Exception(() => RunAndAssert("environment-root-first", computesSibling)));
+        Assert.Null(Record.Exception(() => RunAndAssert("environment-root-first", readsMachineProfile)));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Asserts that sources are hermetic for unit tests: no plugin-root variable (nor any other name) resolves, no
+    /// sibling folder is offered for any workspace, and the profile is an existing, empty directory under the
+    /// system temporary folder.
+    /// </summary>
+    internal static void AssertHermetic(AgentPluginVersionSources sources)
+    {
+        foreach (var name in PluginRootVariables.Append("ANY_OTHER_VARIABLE"))
+            Assert.Null(sources.EnvironmentVariable(name));
+        Assert.Null(sources.SiblingRoot(Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "workspace"), "mcpserver-claude-code-plugin"));
+        Assert.Null(sources.SiblingRoot(@"C:\test", "mcpserver-codex-plugin"));
+        Assert.False(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sources.UserProfile));
+        Assert.True(Directory.Exists(sources.UserProfile));
+        Assert.Empty(Directory.EnumerateFileSystemEntries(sources.UserProfile!));
+        Assert.StartsWith(Path.GetFullPath(Path.GetTempPath()), Path.GetFullPath(sources.UserProfile!), StringComparison.Ordinal);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Mock gate positive control: scripted hermetic sources pass the hermeticity assertion.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ScriptedHermeticSources_PassTheHermeticityAssertion() =>
+        AssertHermetic(new AgentPluginVersionSources(_ => null, (_, _) => null, EmptyProfile));
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Mock gate negative controls: sources that resolve a plugin-root variable, compute the folder beside the
+    /// workspace, point at a populated profile, or point at no profile are each rejected by the hermeticity
+    /// assertion, inside a passing harness.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void LeakySourcesNegativeControls_AreRejectedInsidePassingHarness()
+    {
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertHermetic(new AgentPluginVersionSources(Machine.EnvironmentVariable, (_, _) => null, EmptyProfile))));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertHermetic(new AgentPluginVersionSources(_ => null, Machine.SiblingRoot, EmptyProfile))));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertHermetic(new AgentPluginVersionSources(_ => null, (_, _) => null, GivenProfile))));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertHermetic(new AgentPluginVersionSources(_ => null, (_, _) => null, null))));
+    }
+}

+/// <summary>
+/// TEST-MCP-TRIAGE-003: the Codex CLI strategy on a Windows host launches through <c>cmd.exe /c</c>.
+/// The strategy chooses its launcher by the running OS, so these are integration tests on Windows hosts
+/// (operator 2026-10-19: "Integration + Platform (Recommended)"; TR-MCP-USECASE-018 AC-006, TR-MCP-NUKE-019). Each
+/// launch sets and restores every CLI temp variable through <see cref="CliTempVariables"/>, so the class runs in the
+/// serialized <see cref="ProcessEnvironmentCollection"/> (TR-MCP-USECASE-019 AC-008; hostile validation finding R6-F1).
+/// </summary>
+[Trait("Category", "Integration")]
+[Trait("Platform", "Windows")]
+[Collection(ProcessEnvironmentCollection.Name)]
+public sealed class CodexCliAgentExecutionStrategyWindowsTests
+{
+    /// <summary>
+    /// TEST-MCP-TRIAGE-003: the one-shot exec launch goes through cmd.exe, which needs the TOML quotes escaped.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public async Task ReadInitialResponseAsync_OnWindows_WrapsCodexExecThroughCmd()
+    {
+        var spawner = await CodexCliAgentExecutionStrategyTests.LaunchDefaultAsync().ConfigureAwait(true);
+
+        CodexCliAgentExecutionStrategyTests.AssertCodexExecWithoutCopilotInteractiveFlags(spawner);
+        Assert.EndsWith("cmd.exe", spawner.StartInfo!.FileName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
+        Assert.Contains("/c", spawner.StartInfo.ArgumentList);
+        Assert.Contains(spawner.StartInfo.ArgumentList, argument => argument.Contains("codex.cmd", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
+        Assert.Contains(spawner.StartInfo.ArgumentList, argument => argument.Contains("exec", StringComparison.Ordinal));
+        Assert.Contains("model_reasoning_effort=\\\"xhigh\\\"", string.Join(" ", spawner.StartInfo.ArgumentList), StringComparison.Ordinal);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// TEST-MCP-TRIAGE-003: Windows npm command shims are wrapped through
+    /// <c>cmd.exe</c> so desktop spawning does not execute <c>.cmd</c> directly.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public async Task ReadInitialResponseAsync_WithWindowsNpmShim_UsesCmdWrapperAndStdinPrompt()
+    {
+        var shimDirectory = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"codex-shim-{Guid.NewGuid():N}");
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(shimDirectory);
+        var cmdPath = Path.Combine(shimDirectory, "codex.cmd");
+        File.WriteAllText(cmdPath, "@echo off", Encoding.UTF8);
+        try
+        {
+            var spawner = new CodexCliAgentExecutionStrategyTests.CapturingProcessSpawner(
+                exitCode: 0,
+                outputBody: """{"title":"triage result"}""",
+                stdout: "codex stdout",
+                stderr: "codex stderr");
+            AgentCliResult? launched = null;
+            await CliTempVariables.RunWithTestFolderAsync(CodexCliAgentExecutionStrategyTests.PrimaryTempName, async _ =>
+            {
+                var strategy = new CodexCliAgentExecutionStrategy(
+                    new CodexCliAgentExecutionStrategyTests.CapturingProcessEnvironmentService(),
+                    spawner,
+                    NullLogger<CodexCliAgentExecutionStrategy>.Instance);
+
+                await using var session = await strategy.CreateSessionAsync(CodexCliAgentExecutionStrategyTests.CreateRequest(cmdPath), cancellationToken: TestContext.Current.CancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(true);
+
+                launched = await session.ReadInitialResponseAsync(cancellationToken: TestContext.Current.CancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(true);
+            }).ConfigureAwait(true);
+
+            var result = Assert.IsType<AgentCliResult>(launched);
+            Assert.Equal(AgentCliResultState.Success, result.State);
+            Assert.Equal("codex stdout", result.Stdout);
+            Assert.Equal("codex stderr", result.Stderr);
+            Assert.Equal(0, result.ExitCode);
+            Assert.NotNull(spawner.StartInfo);
+            Assert.EndsWith("cmd.exe", spawner.StartInfo.FileName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
+            Assert.Contains("/c", spawner.StartInfo.ArgumentList);
+            Assert.Contains(spawner.StartInfo.ArgumentList, argument => argument.Contains(cmdPath, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
+            Assert.Contains(spawner.StartInfo.ArgumentList, argument => argument.Contains("exec", StringComparison.Ordinal));
+            Assert.Contains(spawner.StartInfo.ArgumentList, argument => argument.Contains("-o", StringComparison.Ordinal));
+            Assert.Contains(spawner.StartInfo.ArgumentList, argument => argument.EndsWith(" -", StringComparison.Ordinal));
+            Assert.True(spawner.StartInfo.RedirectStandardInput);
+            Assert.DoesNotContain(spawner.StartInfo.ArgumentList, argument => argument.Contains("rendered prompt", StringComparison.Ordinal));
+            Assert.Equal("rendered prompt", spawner.CapturedStandardInput);
+        }
+        finally
+        {
+            Directory.Delete(shimDirectory, recursive: true);
+        }
+    }
+}

+
+/// <summary>
+/// TEST-MCP-TRIAGE-003: the default spawner on an interactive Windows host. Windows-only and dependent on an
+/// interactive session, so an integration test on Windows hosts that fails, instead of returning silently, when
+/// the session is not interactive (operator 2026-10-19: "Platform=Windows, fail loudly (Recommended)";
+/// TR-MCP-USECASE-018 AC-006, TR-MCP-NUKE-019).
+/// </summary>
+[Trait("Category", "Integration")]
+[Trait("Platform", "Windows")]
+public sealed class DesktopProcessSpawnerWindowsInteractiveTests
+{
+    /// <summary>
+    /// TEST-MCP-TRIAGE-003: Interactive local hosts use the default process spawner
+    /// instead of the Windows service desktop-token launcher.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public async Task Spawn_WhenHostIsInteractive_CanRunCmdThroughDefaultSpawner()
+    {
+        Assert.True(
+            Environment.UserInteractive,
+            "This case needs an interactive Windows session; a non-interactive runner (for example a WSMan session) cannot prove the interactive default-spawner path.");
+
+        var spawner = new DesktopProcessSpawner(NullLoggerFactory.Instance);
+        var startInfo = new ProcessStartInfo
+        {
+            FileName = Path.Combine(Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.System), "cmd.exe"),
+            UseShellExecute = false,
+            RedirectStandardOutput = true,
+            RedirectStandardError = true,
+            CreateNoWindow = true,
+        };
+        startInfo.ArgumentList.Add("/d");
+        startInfo.ArgumentList.Add("/s");
+        startInfo.ArgumentList.Add("/c");
+        startInfo.ArgumentList.Add("echo desktop-spawner-default");
+
+        using var process = spawner.Spawn(startInfo);
+        var stdoutTask = process.StandardOutput.ReadToEndAsync(cancellationToken: TestContext.Current.CancellationToken);
+        var stderrTask = process.StandardError.ReadToEndAsync(cancellationToken: TestContext.Current.CancellationToken);
+        await process.WaitForExitAsync(cancellationToken: TestContext.Current.CancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(true);
+
+        var stdout = await stdoutTask.ConfigureAwait(true);
+        var stderr = await stderrTask.ConfigureAwait(true);
+        Assert.Equal(0, process.ExitCode);
+        Assert.Contains("desktop-spawner-default", stdout, StringComparison.Ordinal);
+        Assert.True(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(stderr), stderr);
+    }
+}

+using QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Services;
+using Xunit;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests.Services;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Tests the directory walk that both user-profile scans use (marker agent-plugin version resolution and the
+/// session-log agent-history scan): a walk never follows a directory link or other reparse point, skips the
+/// contents of any directory whose listing fails with an IO or access error, and keeps walking the rest.
+/// </summary>
+/// <remarks>
+/// Requirement coverage: TR-MCP-WS-005 / AC-TR-MCP-WS-005-001 and TR-MCP-SESSIONLOG-006 /
+/// AC-TR-MCP-SESSIONLOG-006-009 (operator 2026-10-20: "Fix scans in PR 87 (Recommended)"; triage
+/// report b0e41c36). Test data: scripted in-memory directory trees (<see cref="ScriptedDirectoryTree"/>) whose
+/// listings return fixed entries or throw the exceptions observed on PAYTON-LEGION2 as kingd (an untrusted mount
+/// point), an access-denied folder, and a vanished folder; no file system is read. Expected outputs are derived
+/// from the acceptance criteria: a directory is yielded when its parent lists it unless it is a link, its contents
+/// are walked only when its own listing succeeds, and file links are yielded as files. Negative controls run
+/// deliberately wrong walks (one that follows links, one that propagates listing errors as the legacy recursive
+/// enumeration did, one that stops at the first error, one that drops an unlistable directory) over the same trees
+/// and prove the walk assertion rejects each. The reachability cases (hostile validation round 5 finding R5-F1;
+/// operator 2026-10-21: "Fix both, strengthen ACs" and "Approve, strengthen AC-006/008 (Recommended)") script the
+/// folders between a boundary (the user profile or home) and a scan root: a scan root is walked only when it and
+/// every folder between the boundary and it are listed by their parents as directories that are not links, and the
+/// boundary itself is trusted. Their negative controls run wrong checks (the unchecked root, a check that follows
+/// links, a check of the root alone, a check that also rejects a linked boundary, a prefix match without a
+/// separator, a check that propagates listing errors, a check that also lists the root) and prove the reachability
+/// assertion rejects each.
+/// </remarks>
+public sealed class DirectoryTreeWalkerTests
+{
+    private const string UntrustedMountPointMessage =
+        "The path cannot be traversed because it contains an untrusted mount point.";
+
+    /// <summary>A walk under test: lists through <c>list</c> from <c>root</c> and returns what it yielded.</summary>
+    private delegate (List<string> Directories, List<string> Files) WalkFunction(
+        Func<string, IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry>> list,
+        string root);
+
+    private static DirectoryListingEntry DirEntry(string path) => new(path, IsDirectory: true, IsLink: false);
+
+    private static DirectoryListingEntry DirLinkEntry(string path) => new(path, IsDirectory: true, IsLink: true);
+
+    private static DirectoryListingEntry FileEntry(string path) => new(path, IsDirectory: false, IsLink: false);
+
+    private static DirectoryListingEntry FileLinkEntry(string path) => new(path, IsDirectory: false, IsLink: true);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Scripted cases: the tree, the walk root, and the directories, files and listed directories the acceptance
+    /// criteria require. Each case builds a fresh tree so the listing record starts empty.
+    /// </summary>
+    private static readonly Dictionary<string, (Func<ScriptedDirectoryTree> Tree, string Root, string[] Directories, string[] Files, string[] Listed)> Cases =
+        new(StringComparer.Ordinal)
+        {
+            ["nested-tree"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+                    .Lists("/p", DirEntry("/p/a"), FileEntry("/p/f1"))
+                    .Lists("/p/a", DirEntry("/p/a/b"), FileEntry("/p/a/f2"))
+                    .Lists("/p/a/b", FileEntry("/p/a/b/f3")),
+                "/p",
+                ["/p/a", "/p/a/b"],
+                ["/p/f1", "/p/a/f2", "/p/a/b/f3"],
+                ["/p", "/p/a", "/p/a/b"]),
+            ["untrusted-mount-point"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+                    .Lists("/p", DirEntry("/p/bad"), DirEntry("/p/good"))
+                    .Throws("/p/bad", () => new IOException(UntrustedMountPointMessage))
+                    .Lists("/p/good", DirEntry("/p/good/inner"), FileEntry("/p/good/g.md"))
+                    .Lists("/p/good/inner", FileEntry("/p/good/inner/i.md")),
+                "/p",
+                ["/p/bad", "/p/good", "/p/good/inner"],
+                ["/p/good/g.md", "/p/good/inner/i.md"],
+                ["/p", "/p/bad", "/p/good", "/p/good/inner"]),
+            ["access-denied"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+                    .Lists("/p", DirEntry("/p/locked"), DirEntry("/p/open"))
+                    .Throws("/p/locked", () => new UnauthorizedAccessException("Access to the path is denied."))
+                    .Lists("/p/open", FileEntry("/p/open/o.md")),
+                "/p",
+                ["/p/locked", "/p/open"],
+                ["/p/open/o.md"],
+                ["/p", "/p/locked", "/p/open"]),
+            ["vanished-directory"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+                    .Lists("/p", DirEntry("/p/gone"), DirEntry("/p/here"))
+                    .Throws("/p/gone", () => new DirectoryNotFoundException("Could not find a part of the path."))
+                    .Lists("/p/here", FileEntry("/p/here/h.md")),
+                "/p",
+                ["/p/gone", "/p/here"],
+                ["/p/here/h.md"],
+                ["/p", "/p/gone", "/p/here"]),
+            ["directory-link"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+                    .Lists("/p", DirEntry("/p/real"), DirLinkEntry("/p/link"), FileEntry("/p/f"))
+                    .Lists("/p/real", FileEntry("/p/real/x.md"))
+                    .Lists("/p/link", FileEntry("/p/link/y.md")),
+                "/p",
+                ["/p/real"],
+                ["/p/f", "/p/real/x.md"],
+                ["/p", "/p/real"]),
+            ["file-link"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+                    .Lists("/p", FileLinkEntry("/p/linked.md"), FileEntry("/p/plain.md")),
+                "/p",
+                [],
+                ["/p/linked.md", "/p/plain.md"],
+                ["/p"]),
+            ["root-unlistable"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+                    .Throws("/p", () => new IOException(UntrustedMountPointMessage)),
+                "/p",
+                [],
+                [],
+                ["/p"]),
+        };
+
+    /// <summary>The scripted case names, for the theories.</summary>
+    public static TheoryData<string> CaseNames => new(Cases.Keys.Order(StringComparer.Ordinal));
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Asserts one walk result against a case: the yielded directories, the yielded files, and the directories
+    /// the walk listed, each compared as an ordinal set (the acceptance criteria fix membership, not order).
+    /// </summary>
+    private static void AssertWalk(
+        (string[] Directories, string[] Files, string[] Listed) expected,
+        IEnumerable<string> directories,
+        IEnumerable<string> files,
+        IEnumerable<string> listed)
+    {
+        Assert.Equal(expected.Directories.Order(StringComparer.Ordinal), directories.Order(StringComparer.Ordinal));
+        Assert.Equal(expected.Files.Order(StringComparer.Ordinal), files.Order(StringComparer.Ordinal));
+        Assert.Equal(
+            expected.Listed.Distinct(StringComparer.Ordinal).Order(StringComparer.Ordinal),
+            listed.Distinct(StringComparer.Ordinal).Order(StringComparer.Ordinal));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Runs one walk over a fresh copy of a case's tree and asserts its result.</summary>
+    private static void RunAndAssert(string name, WalkFunction walk)
+    {
+        var (treeFactory, root, directories, files, listed) = Cases[name];
+        var tree = treeFactory();
+        var result = walk(tree.List, root);
+        AssertWalk((directories, files, listed), result.Directories, result.Files, tree.Listed);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Mock gate positive control: each case's required output passes the walk assertion.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="name">Scripted case name.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [MemberData(nameof(CaseNames))]
+    public void ScriptedCase_RequiredOutput_PassesTheWalkAssertion(string name)
+    {
+        var (_, _, directories, files, listed) = Cases[name];
+        AssertWalk((directories, files, listed), directories, files, listed);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>The theories cover every scripted case.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ScriptedTheory_CoversEveryCase() => Assert.Equal(7, Cases.Count);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// The scripted tree is itself correct: it records every listing in order, returns the scripted entries,
+    /// throws the scripted exception, and refuses a directory it was not given, so a walk that lists a link
+    /// it should have skipped fails loudly.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ScriptedTree_RecordsListingsAndRefusesUnscriptedDirectories()
+    {
+        var tree = new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+            .Lists("/p", DirEntry("/p/a"))
+            .Throws("/p/a", () => new IOException(UntrustedMountPointMessage));
+
+        Assert.Equal(new[] { DirEntry("/p/a") }, tree.List("/p"));
+        Assert.Equal(UntrustedMountPointMessage, Assert.Throws<IOException>(() => tree.List("/p/a")).Message);
+        Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => tree.List("/p/unscripted"));
+        Assert.Equal(new[] { "/p", "/p/a", "/p/unscripted" }, tree.Listed);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Mock gate negative controls: each wrong walk is rejected by the walk assertion on the case that exposes it,
+    /// inside a passing harness. The legacy recursive enumeration (follows links, propagates listing errors) fails
+    /// on the untrusted mount point, the access-denied folder, the vanished folder, the unlistable root and the
+    /// directory link; a walk that follows links fails on the directory link; a walk that stops at the first
+    /// listing error loses the healthy sibling; a walk that drops an unlistable directory loses its entry.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void WrongWalkNegativeControls_AreRejectedInsidePassingHarness()
+    {
+        foreach (var name in new[] { "untrusted-mount-point", "access-denied", "vanished-directory", "root-unlistable", "directory-link" })
+            Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => RunAndAssert(name, LegacyRecursiveWalk)));
+
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => RunAndAssert("directory-link", FollowsLinksWalk)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => RunAndAssert("untrusted-mount-point", StopsAtFirstErrorWalk)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => RunAndAssert("untrusted-mount-point", DropsUnlistableDirectoryWalk)));
+
+        // Each wrong walk is wrong only where a case exposes it: on the nested tree, which has no link and no
+        // listing error, every one of them yields the required output, so the rejections above come from the
+        // behavior under test and not from an assertion that rejects everything.
+        foreach (var walk in new WalkFunction[] { LegacyRecursiveWalk, FollowsLinksWalk, StopsAtFirstErrorWalk, DropsUnlistableDirectoryWalk })
+            Assert.Null(Record.Exception(() => RunAndAssert("nested-tree", walk)));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Mock gate negative control for the error filter: a scripted listing error that is neither an IO nor an
+    /// access error escapes the scripted tree, and a walk with a catch-all filter hides it; the production
+    /// binding requires the walk to let it propagate.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void UnexpectedListingError_EscapesTheScriptedTree()
+    {
+        var tree = new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+            .Lists("/p", DirEntry("/p/a"))
+            .Throws("/p/a", () => new InvalidOperationException("not an IO error"));
+
+        Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => LegacyRecursiveWalk(tree.List, "/p"));
+        Assert.Null(Record.Exception(() => SwallowsEverythingWalk(tree.List, "/p")));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Production binding of the scripted cases: <see cref="DirectoryTreeWalker"/> over each scripted tree yields
+    /// the required directories and files, and both its directory walk and its file walk list exactly the required
+    /// directories (never a link, never the contents of an unlistable directory).
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="name">Scripted case name.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [MemberData(nameof(CaseNames))]
+    public void ScriptedCase_ProductionWalk_YieldsTheRequiredOutput(string name)
+    {
+        var (treeFactory, root, directories, files, listed) = Cases[name];
+        var directoryTree = treeFactory();
+        var fileTree = treeFactory();
+
+        var yieldedDirectories = new DirectoryTreeWalker(directoryTree.List).EnumerateDirectories(root).ToList();
+        var yieldedFiles = new DirectoryTreeWalker(fileTree.List).EnumerateFiles(root).ToList();
+
+        AssertWalk((directories, files, listed), yieldedDirectories, yieldedFiles, directoryTree.Listed);
+        AssertWalk((directories, files, listed), yieldedDirectories, yieldedFiles, fileTree.Listed);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Production binding of the error filter: a listing error that is neither an IO nor an access error
+    /// propagates out of <see cref="DirectoryTreeWalker"/> instead of being skipped.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ProductionWalk_UnexpectedListingError_Propagates()
+    {
+        var tree = new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+            .Lists("/p", DirEntry("/p/a"))
+            .Throws("/p/a", () => new InvalidOperationException("not an IO error"));
+
+        Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => new DirectoryTreeWalker(tree.List).EnumerateDirectories("/p").ToList());
+        Assert.Throws<ArgumentNullException>(() => new DirectoryTreeWalker(null!));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Production binding of the scripted reachability cases: <see cref="DirectoryTreeWalker.IsReachable"/> over
+    /// each scripted tree gives the required answer and never lists a directory link below the boundary.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="name">Scripted reachability case name.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [MemberData(nameof(ReachCaseNames))]
+    public void ScriptedReachCase_ProductionCheck_GivesTheRequiredAnswer(string name) =>
+        RunReachAndAssert(name, (list, boundary, root) => new DirectoryTreeWalker(list).IsReachable(boundary, root));
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Production binding of the reachability error filter: a listing error that is neither an IO nor an access
+    /// error propagates out of <see cref="DirectoryTreeWalker.IsReachable"/> instead of being taken as unreachable.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ProductionReach_UnexpectedListingError_Propagates()
+    {
+        var tree = new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+            .Lists("/p", DirEntry("/p/.codex"))
+            .Throws("/p/.codex", () => new InvalidOperationException("not an IO error"));
+
+        Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => new DirectoryTreeWalker(tree.List).IsReachable("/p", "/p/.codex/plugins"));
+        Assert.Throws<ArgumentNullException>(() => new DirectoryTreeWalker(tree.List).IsReachable(null!, "/p"));
+        Assert.Throws<ArgumentNullException>(() => new DirectoryTreeWalker(tree.List).IsReachable("/p", null!));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Integration: on the real file system, <see cref="DirectoryTreeWalker.FileSystem"/> reaches a real folder below
+    /// a boundary, does not reach a scan root that is a directory symbolic link or one reached through a linked
+    /// ancestor (each link's target lies outside the boundary), and still reaches a root below a boundary that is
+    /// itself a directory symbolic link (AC-TR-MCP-WS-005-001, AC-TR-MCP-SESSIONLOG-006-009; hostile validation
+    /// round 5 finding R5-F1). Creating symbolic links depends on the host (on Windows, Developer Mode or the
+    /// create-symbolic-link privilege), so this is an integration test.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    [Trait("Category", "Integration")]
+    public void FileSystemReach_RealRootAndAncestorLinks_AreNotReachable()
+    {
+        var boundary = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"mcp-reach-links-{Guid.NewGuid():N}");
+        var outside = boundary + "-outside";
+        var linkedBoundary = boundary + "-boundary-link";
+        var realRoot = Path.Combine(boundary, "real", "plugins", "cache");
+        var rootLink = Path.Combine(boundary, "real", "plugins", "root-link");
+        var ancestorLink = Path.Combine(boundary, "ancestor-link");
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(realRoot);
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.Combine(outside, "cache"));
+        try
+        {
+            Directory.CreateSymbolicLink(rootLink, Path.Combine(outside, "cache"));
+            Directory.CreateSymbolicLink(ancestorLink, outside);
+            Directory.CreateSymbolicLink(linkedBoundary, boundary);
+            Assert.True(Directory.Exists(rootLink));
+            Assert.True(Directory.Exists(Path.Combine(ancestorLink, "cache")));
+
+            Assert.True(DirectoryTreeWalker.FileSystem.IsReachable(boundary, realRoot));
+            Assert.False(DirectoryTreeWalker.FileSystem.IsReachable(boundary, rootLink));
+            Assert.False(DirectoryTreeWalker.FileSystem.IsReachable(boundary, Path.Combine(ancestorLink, "cache")));
+            Assert.True(DirectoryTreeWalker.FileSystem.IsReachable(linkedBoundary, Path.Combine(linkedBoundary, "real", "plugins", "cache")));
+        }
+        finally
+        {
+            foreach (var link in new[] { linkedBoundary, ancestorLink, rootLink })
+            {
+                if (Directory.Exists(link))
+                    Directory.Delete(link);
+            }
+
+            Directory.Delete(boundary, recursive: true);
+            Directory.Delete(outside, recursive: true);
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Integration: on the real file system, <see cref="DirectoryTreeWalker.ListFileSystem"/> flags a directory
+    /// symbolic link and a file symbolic link as links, and <see cref="DirectoryTreeWalker.FileSystem"/> neither
+    /// yields nor descends the directory link (whose target lies outside the walked tree) while it yields the file
+    /// link as a file. Creating symbolic links depends on the host (on Windows, Developer Mode or the
+    /// create-symbolic-link privilege), so this is an integration test.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    [Trait("Category", "Integration")]
+    public void FileSystemWalk_RealLinks_AreFlaggedAndNotDescended()
+    {
+        var root = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"mcp-walk-links-{Guid.NewGuid():N}");
+        var outside = root + "-outside";
+        var real = Path.Combine(root, "real");
+        var realFile = Path.Combine(real, "x.md");
+        var directoryLink = Path.Combine(root, "dir-link");
+        var fileLink = Path.Combine(root, "file-link.md");
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(real);
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(outside);
+        System.IO.File.WriteAllText(realFile, "x");
+        System.IO.File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(outside, "y.md"), "y");
+        try
+        {
+            Directory.CreateSymbolicLink(directoryLink, outside);
+            System.IO.File.CreateSymbolicLink(fileLink, realFile);
+
+            var entries = DirectoryTreeWalker.ListFileSystem(root);
+            Assert.Contains(new DirectoryListingEntry(directoryLink, IsDirectory: true, IsLink: true), entries);
+            Assert.Contains(new DirectoryListingEntry(fileLink, IsDirectory: false, IsLink: true), entries);
+            Assert.Contains(new DirectoryListingEntry(real, IsDirectory: true, IsLink: false), entries);
+
+            Assert.Equal(new[] { real }, DirectoryTreeWalker.FileSystem.EnumerateDirectories(root));
+            Assert.Equal(
+                new[] { fileLink, realFile }.Order(StringComparer.Ordinal),
+                DirectoryTreeWalker.FileSystem.EnumerateFiles(root).Order(StringComparer.Ordinal));
+        }
+        finally
+        {
+            if (System.IO.File.Exists(fileLink))
+                System.IO.File.Delete(fileLink);
+            if (Directory.Exists(directoryLink))
+                Directory.Delete(directoryLink);
+            Directory.Delete(root, recursive: true);
+            Directory.Delete(outside, recursive: true);
+        }
+    }
+
+    // Wrong walks used only as negative controls.
+
+    private static (List<string> Directories, List<string> Files) LegacyRecursiveWalk(
+        Func<string, IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry>> list, string root)
+    {
+        var directories = new List<string>();
+        var files = new List<string>();
+        var pending = new Queue<string>([root]);
+        while (pending.Count > 0)
+        {
+            foreach (var entry in list(pending.Dequeue()))
+            {
+                if (entry.IsDirectory)
+                {
+                    directories.Add(entry.Path);
+                    pending.Enqueue(entry.Path);
+                }
+                else
+                {
+                    files.Add(entry.Path);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        return (directories, files);
+    }
+
+    private static (List<string> Directories, List<string> Files) FollowsLinksWalk(
+        Func<string, IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry>> list, string root) =>
+        ConfigurableWalk(list, root, skipLinks: false, stopAtFirstError: false, yieldUnlistable: true, catchAll: false);
+
+    private static (List<string> Directories, List<string> Files) StopsAtFirstErrorWalk(
+        Func<string, IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry>> list, string root) =>
+        ConfigurableWalk(list, root, skipLinks: true, stopAtFirstError: true, yieldUnlistable: true, catchAll: false);
+
+    private static (List<string> Directories, List<string> Files) DropsUnlistableDirectoryWalk(
+        Func<string, IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry>> list, string root) =>
+        ConfigurableWalk(list, root, skipLinks: true, stopAtFirstError: false, yieldUnlistable: false, catchAll: false);
+
+    private static (List<string> Directories, List<string> Files) SwallowsEverythingWalk(
+        Func<string, IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry>> list, string root) =>
+        ConfigurableWalk(list, root, skipLinks: true, stopAtFirstError: false, yieldUnlistable: true, catchAll: true);
+
+    private static (List<string> Directories, List<string> Files) ConfigurableWalk(
+        Func<string, IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry>> list,
+        string root,
+        bool skipLinks,
+        bool stopAtFirstError,
+        bool yieldUnlistable,
+        bool catchAll)
+    {
+        var directories = new List<string>();
+        var files = new List<string>();
+        var pending = new Queue<string>([root]);
+        while (pending.Count > 0)
+        {
+            var directory = pending.Dequeue();
+            IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry> entries;
+            try
+            {
+                entries = list(directory);
+            }
+            catch (Exception ex) when (catchAll || ex is IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException)
+            {
+                if (!yieldUnlistable)
+                    directories.Remove(directory);
+                if (stopAtFirstError)
+                    break;
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            foreach (var entry in entries)
+            {
+                if (entry.IsDirectory && entry.IsLink && skipLinks)
+                    continue;
+                if (entry.IsDirectory)
+                {
+                    directories.Add(entry.Path);
+                    pending.Enqueue(entry.Path);
+                }
+                else
+                {
+                    files.Add(entry.Path);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        return (directories, files);
+    }
+
+    // Reachability of a scan root below a boundary (hostile validation round 5 finding R5-F1).
+
+    /// <summary>A reachability check under test: lists through <c>list</c> from <c>boundary</c> down to <c>root</c>.</summary>
+    private delegate bool ReachFunction(
+        Func<string, IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry>> list,
+        string boundary,
+        string root);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Scripted reachability cases: the tree, the boundary (the user profile or home), the scan root, and whether
+    /// the root may be walked. Expected values come from the strengthened criteria AC-TR-MCP-WS-005-001 and
+    /// AC-TR-MCP-SESSIONLOG-006-009 (operator 2026-10-21: "Fix both, strengthen ACs"): the root and every folder
+    /// between the boundary and it must each be listed by its parent as a directory that is not a link; a folder
+    /// whose listing fails with an IO or access error cannot be traversed; the boundary itself is trusted, so a
+    /// linked profile or home is still scanned; a root that is the boundary is reachable without any listing.
+    /// </summary>
+    private static readonly Dictionary<string, (Func<ScriptedDirectoryTree> Tree, string Boundary, string Root, bool Reachable)> ReachCases =
+        new(StringComparer.Ordinal)
+        {
+            ["reach-normal"] = (() => CodexTree(rootIsLink: false), "/p", "/p/.codex/plugins/cache", true),
+            ["reach-root-link"] = (() => CodexTree(rootIsLink: true), "/p", "/p/.codex/plugins/cache", false),
+            ["reach-top-ancestor-link"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+                    .Lists("/", DirEntry("/p"))
+                    .Lists("/p", DirLinkEntry("/p/.codex"), FileEntry("/p/.bashrc")),
+                "/p",
+                "/p/.codex/plugins/cache",
+                false),
+            ["reach-middle-ancestor-link"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+                    .Lists("/", DirEntry("/p"))
+                    .Lists("/p", DirEntry("/p/.codex"))
+                    .Lists("/p/.codex", DirLinkEntry("/p/.codex/plugins")),
+                "/p",
+                "/p/.codex/plugins/cache",
+                false),
+            ["reach-boundary-link"] = (
+                () => CodexTree(rootIsLink: false).Lists("/", DirLinkEntry("/p")),
+                "/p",
+                "/p/.codex/plugins/cache",
+                true),
+            ["reach-parent-unlistable"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+                    .Lists("/", DirEntry("/p"))
+                    .Lists("/p", DirEntry("/p/.codex"))
+                    .Throws("/p/.codex", () => new IOException(UntrustedMountPointMessage)),
+                "/p",
+                "/p/.codex/plugins/cache",
+                false),
+            ["reach-parent-access-denied"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+                    .Lists("/", DirEntry("/p"))
+                    .Lists("/p", DirEntry("/p/.codex"))
+                    .Throws("/p/.codex", () => new UnauthorizedAccessException("Access to the path is denied.")),
+                "/p",
+                "/p/.codex/plugins/cache",
+                false),
+            ["reach-root-absent"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+                    .Lists("/", DirEntry("/p"))
+                    .Lists("/p", DirEntry("/p/.codex"))
+                    .Lists("/p/.codex", DirEntry("/p/.codex/plugins"))
+                    .Lists("/p/.codex/plugins", DirEntry("/p/.codex/plugins/other")),
+                "/p",
+                "/p/.codex/plugins/cache",
+                false),
+            ["reach-root-is-file"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+                    .Lists("/", DirEntry("/p"))
+                    .Lists("/p", DirEntry("/p/.codex"))
+                    .Lists("/p/.codex", DirEntry("/p/.codex/plugins"))
+                    .Lists("/p/.codex/plugins", FileEntry("/p/.codex/plugins/cache")),
+                "/p",
+                "/p/.codex/plugins/cache",
+                false),
+            ["reach-root-unlistable"] = (
+                () => CodexTree(rootIsLink: false).Throws("/p/.codex/plugins/cache", () => new IOException(UntrustedMountPointMessage)),
+                "/p",
+                "/p/.codex/plugins/cache",
+                true),
+            ["reach-root-is-boundary"] = (() => new ScriptedDirectoryTree().Lists("/", DirEntry("/p")), "/p", "/p", true),
+            ["reach-outside-boundary"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+                    .Lists("/", DirEntry("/p"), DirEntry("/q"))
+                    .Lists("/p", DirEntry("/p/.codex")),
+                "/p",
+                "/q/.codex",
+                false),
+            ["reach-prefix-sibling-link"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+                    .Lists("/", DirEntry("/p"))
+                    .Lists("/p", DirLinkEntry("/p/.cod"), DirEntry("/p/.codex"))
+                    .Lists("/p/.codex", DirEntry("/p/.codex/plugins")),
+                "/p",
+                "/p/.codex/plugins",
+                true),
+            ["reach-trailing-separator"] = (() => CodexTree(rootIsLink: false), "/p", "/p/.codex/plugins/cache/", true),
+            ["reach-history-root"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+                    .Lists("/", DirEntry("/h"))
+                    .Lists("/h", DirEntry("/h/.claude"), DirEntry("/h/.codex")),
+                "/h",
+                "/h/.claude",
+                true),
+            ["reach-history-root-link"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+                    .Lists("/", DirEntry("/h"))
+                    .Lists("/h", DirLinkEntry("/h/.claude"), DirEntry("/h/.codex")),
+                "/h",
+                "/h/.claude",
+                false),
+        };
+
+    /// <summary>The scripted reachability case names, for the theories.</summary>
+    public static TheoryData<string> ReachCaseNames => new(ReachCases.Keys.Order(StringComparer.Ordinal));
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// The Codex cache layout <c>/p/.codex/plugins/cache</c> under the boundary <c>/p</c>, with the cache root
+    /// listed as a link when <paramref name="rootIsLink"/>.
+    /// </summary>
+    private static ScriptedDirectoryTree CodexTree(bool rootIsLink) =>
+        new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+            .Lists("/", DirEntry("/p"))
+            .Lists("/p", DirEntry("/p/.codex"), FileEntry("/p/.bashrc"))
+            .Lists("/p/.codex", DirEntry("/p/.codex/plugins"))
+            .Lists("/p/.codex/plugins", rootIsLink ? DirLinkEntry("/p/.codex/plugins/cache") : DirEntry("/p/.codex/plugins/cache"))
+            .Lists("/p/.codex/plugins/cache", DirEntry("/p/.codex/plugins/cache/mcpserver"));
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Asserts one reachability result against a case: the answer equals the required one, and the check never
+    /// listed a directory link below the boundary (it never descends into a link to find the root).
+    /// </summary>
+    private static void AssertReach(string boundary, bool expected, bool actual, ScriptedDirectoryTree tree)
+    {
+        Assert.Equal(expected, actual);
+        Assert.DoesNotContain(tree.Listed, directory => !string.Equals(directory, boundary, StringComparison.Ordinal) && tree.IsLink(directory));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Runs one reachability check over a fresh copy of a case's tree and asserts its result.</summary>
+    private static void RunReachAndAssert(string name, ReachFunction reach)
+    {
+        var (treeFactory, boundary, root, reachable) = ReachCases[name];
+        var tree = treeFactory();
+        AssertReach(boundary, reachable, reach(tree.List, boundary, root), tree);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Mock gate positive control: each reachability case's required answer passes the reachability assertion on a
+    /// tree that has listed nothing.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="name">Scripted reachability case name.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [MemberData(nameof(ReachCaseNames))]
+    public void ScriptedReachCase_RequiredAnswer_PassesTheReachAssertion(string name)
+    {
+        var (treeFactory, boundary, _, reachable) = ReachCases[name];
+        AssertReach(boundary, reachable, reachable, treeFactory());
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>The reachability theories cover every scripted case.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ScriptedReachTheory_CoversEveryCase() => Assert.Equal(16, ReachCases.Count);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Mock gate negative controls: each wrong reachability check is rejected by the reachability assertion on the
+    /// case that exposes it, inside a passing harness. The unchecked root (the behavior before the fix) fails on a
+    /// linked root, a linked ancestor and a linked history root; a check that follows links fails on a linked root
+    /// and a linked ancestor; a check of the root alone fails on a linked ancestor; a check that also rejects a
+    /// linked boundary fails on a linked profile; a prefix match without a separator takes the linked sibling
+    /// <c>.cod</c> for <c>.codex</c>; a check that propagates listing errors fails on an unlistable parent; a check
+    /// that also lists the root fails when only the root cannot be listed.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void WrongReachNegativeControls_AreRejectedInsidePassingHarness()
+    {
+        foreach (var name in new[] { "reach-root-link", "reach-top-ancestor-link", "reach-history-root-link" })
+            Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => RunReachAndAssert(name, UncheckedReach)));
+
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => RunReachAndAssert("reach-root-link", FollowsLinksReach)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => RunReachAndAssert("reach-middle-ancestor-link", FollowsLinksReach)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => RunReachAndAssert("reach-top-ancestor-link", RootOnlyReach)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => RunReachAndAssert("reach-middle-ancestor-link", RootOnlyReach)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => RunReachAndAssert("reach-boundary-link", ChecksBoundaryReach)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => RunReachAndAssert("reach-prefix-sibling-link", PrefixMatchReach)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => RunReachAndAssert("reach-parent-unlistable", PropagatesListingErrorsReach)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => RunReachAndAssert("reach-root-unlistable", ListsRootReach)));
+
+        // Each wrong check is wrong only where a case exposes it: on the normal layout, which has no link and no
+        // listing error, every one of them gives the required answer, so the rejections above come from the
+        // behavior under test and not from an assertion that rejects everything.
+        foreach (var reach in new ReachFunction[] { UncheckedReach, FollowsLinksReach, RootOnlyReach, ChecksBoundaryReach, PrefixMatchReach, PropagatesListingErrorsReach, ListsRootReach })
+            Assert.Null(Record.Exception(() => RunReachAndAssert("reach-normal", reach)));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Mock gate negative control for the reachability error filter: a scripted listing error that is neither an IO
+    /// nor an access error escapes the scripted tree, and a check with a catch-all filter hides it; the production
+    /// binding requires the check to let it propagate.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void UnexpectedReachListingError_EscapesTheScriptedTree()
+    {
+        var tree = new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+            .Lists("/p", DirEntry("/p/.codex"))
+            .Throws("/p/.codex", () => new InvalidOperationException("not an IO error"));
+
+        Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => PropagatesListingErrorsReach(tree.List, "/p", "/p/.codex/plugins"));
+        Assert.Null(Record.Exception(() => SwallowsEverythingReach(tree.List, "/p", "/p/.codex/plugins")));
+    }
+
+    // Wrong reachability checks used only as negative controls.
+
+    private static readonly ReachFunction UncheckedReach = (_, _, _) => true;
+
+    private static bool FollowsLinksReach(Func<string, IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry>> list, string boundary, string root) =>
+        ConfigurableReach(list, boundary, root, checkLinks: false, checkAncestors: true, checkBoundary: false, requireSeparator: true, catchListingErrors: true, catchAll: false, listRoot: false);
+
+    private static bool RootOnlyReach(Func<string, IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry>> list, string boundary, string root) =>
+        ConfigurableReach(list, boundary, root, checkLinks: true, checkAncestors: false, checkBoundary: false, requireSeparator: true, catchListingErrors: true, catchAll: false, listRoot: false);
+
+    private static bool ChecksBoundaryReach(Func<string, IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry>> list, string boundary, string root) =>
+        ConfigurableReach(list, boundary, root, checkLinks: true, checkAncestors: true, checkBoundary: true, requireSeparator: true, catchListingErrors: true, catchAll: false, listRoot: false);
+
+    private static bool PrefixMatchReach(Func<string, IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry>> list, string boundary, string root) =>
+        ConfigurableReach(list, boundary, root, checkLinks: true, checkAncestors: true, checkBoundary: false, requireSeparator: false, catchListingErrors: true, catchAll: false, listRoot: false);
+
+    private static bool PropagatesListingErrorsReach(Func<string, IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry>> list, string boundary, string root) =>
+        ConfigurableReach(list, boundary, root, checkLinks: true, checkAncestors: true, checkBoundary: false, requireSeparator: true, catchListingErrors: false, catchAll: false, listRoot: false);
+
+    private static bool ListsRootReach(Func<string, IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry>> list, string boundary, string root) =>
+        ConfigurableReach(list, boundary, root, checkLinks: true, checkAncestors: true, checkBoundary: false, requireSeparator: true, catchListingErrors: true, catchAll: false, listRoot: true);
+
+    private static bool SwallowsEverythingReach(Func<string, IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry>> list, string boundary, string root) =>
+        ConfigurableReach(list, boundary, root, checkLinks: true, checkAncestors: true, checkBoundary: false, requireSeparator: true, catchListingErrors: true, catchAll: true, listRoot: false);
+
+    private static bool ConfigurableReach(
+        Func<string, IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry>> list,
+        string boundary,
+        string root,
+        bool checkLinks,
+        bool checkAncestors,
+        bool checkBoundary,
+        bool requireSeparator,
+        bool catchListingErrors,
+        bool catchAll,
+        bool listRoot)
+    {
+        var target = root.Length > 1 ? root.TrimEnd('/') : root;
+        try
+        {
+            if (checkBoundary && boundary.Length > 1)
+            {
+                var parent = boundary[..Math.Max(1, boundary.LastIndexOf('/'))];
+                var self = list(parent)
+                    .Where(entry => entry.Path == boundary)
+                    .Select(entry => (DirectoryListingEntry?)entry)
+                    .FirstOrDefault();
+                if (self is null || self.Value.IsLink)
+                    return false;
+            }
+
+            var current = boundary;
+            while (current != target)
+            {
+                var found = list(current)
+                    .Where(entry => target.StartsWith(entry.Path, StringComparison.Ordinal)
+                        && (!requireSeparator || target.Length == entry.Path.Length || target[entry.Path.Length] == '/'))
+                    .Select(entry => (DirectoryListingEntry?)entry)
+                    .FirstOrDefault();
+                if (found is not { } next || !next.IsDirectory || next.Path.Length <= current.Length)
+                    return false;
+                if (next.IsLink && checkLinks && (checkAncestors || next.Path == target))
+                    return false;
+                current = next.Path;
+            }
+
+            if (listRoot)
+                _ = list(target);
+            return true;
+        }
+        catch (Exception ex) when (catchAll || (catchListingErrors && ex is IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException))
+        {
+            return false;
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Scripted in-memory directory tree: each directory lists fixed entries or throws a fixed exception, every
+    /// listing is recorded in order, and an unscripted directory throws <see cref="InvalidOperationException"/>.
+    /// </summary>
+    private sealed class ScriptedDirectoryTree
+    {
+        private readonly Dictionary<string, Func<IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry>>> _listings = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);
+
+        private readonly HashSet<string> _directoryLinks = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);
+
+        /// <summary>Directories listed so far, in order.</summary>
+        public List<string> Listed { get; } = [];
+
+        /// <summary>True when some scripted listing reports <paramref name="directory"/> as a directory link.</summary>
+        public bool IsLink(string directory) => _directoryLinks.Contains(directory);
+
+        /// <summary>Scripts <paramref name="directory"/> to list <paramref name="entries"/>.</summary>
+        public ScriptedDirectoryTree Lists(string directory, params DirectoryListingEntry[] entries)
+        {
+            _listings[directory] = () => entries;
+            foreach (var entry in entries.Where(entry => entry.IsDirectory && entry.IsLink))
+                _directoryLinks.Add(entry.Path);
+            return this;
+        }
+
+        /// <summary>Scripts <paramref name="directory"/> to throw the exception <paramref name="error"/> creates.</summary>
+        public ScriptedDirectoryTree Throws(string directory, Func<Exception> error)
+        {
+            _listings[directory] = () => throw error();
+            return this;
+        }
+
+        /// <summary>Lists one directory as scripted.</summary>
+        public IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry> List(string directory)
+        {
+            Listed.Add(directory);
+            if (!_listings.TryGetValue(directory, out var listing))
+                throw new InvalidOperationException($"Unscripted directory listed: {directory}");
+            return listing();
+        }
+    }
+}
