+using Xunit;
+using static QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests.TestSupport.CliTempVariables;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests.TestSupport;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Tests the shared CLI temporary-folder variable support of <see cref="CliTempVariables"/>: its name list matches the
+/// four strategies' fallback chains, and its snapshot assertions accept a run that pointed the primary name at the test
+/// folder, every other name at a separate fallback folder, and restored every name, and reject one that missed or
+/// mis-set a name.
+/// </summary>
+/// <remarks>
+/// Requirement coverage: TR-MCP-USECASE-018 AC-006 and TR-MCP-USECASE-019 AC-008 as amended 2026-10-26 ("set and
+/// restore every variable their assertions or the code they call depend on, every name of a fallback chain the code
+/// reads"; operator: "Strengthen AC, move 14 (Recommended)" and "Dependence + proof AC (Recommended)"; hostile
+/// validation finding R6-F1). Test data: scripted snapshots (dictionaries of name to value); these mock gate controls
+/// read and write no process environment, so they are unit tests. The class runs in
+/// <see cref="ProcessEnvironmentCollection"/> because its binding tests write the process environment; those are
+/// integration tests.
+/// </remarks>
+[Collection(ProcessEnvironmentCollection.Name)]
+public sealed class CliTempVariablesTests
+{
+    private const string Folder = "/scripted/cli-temp-folder";
+
+    private const string Fallback = "/scripted/cli-temp-fallback";
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// The name list holds the five chain names once each: CliBrainSlotChatClient (QUADBRAIN_CLI, ONESHOT), Codex
+    /// (CODEX_ONESHOT, CODEX_TRIAGE), one-shot (ONESHOT, CODEX_ONESHOT, CODEX_TRIAGE) and Grok (GROK_ONESHOT, ONESHOT).
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void Names_HoldEveryChainNameOnce()
+    {
+        string[] expected =
+        [
+            "MCPSERVER_CODEX_ONESHOT_TEMP",
+            "MCPSERVER_CODEX_TRIAGE_TEMP",
+            "MCPSERVER_GROK_ONESHOT_TEMP",
+            "MCPSERVER_ONESHOT_TEMP",
+            "MCPSERVER_QUADBRAIN_CLI_TEMP",
+        ];
+
+        Assert.Equal(expected, Names.Order(StringComparer.Ordinal));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Mock gate positive control: a scripted snapshot with the primary name at the folder and every other name at the
+    /// fallback folder passes, for each name as the primary.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="primaryName">The name read first.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("MCPSERVER_QUADBRAIN_CLI_TEMP")]
+    [InlineData("MCPSERVER_ONESHOT_TEMP")]
+    [InlineData("MCPSERVER_CODEX_ONESHOT_TEMP")]
+    public void ScriptedChain_PrimaryAtFolderOthersAtFallback_Passes(string primaryName) =>
+        AssertChainAt(Scripted(name => name == primaryName ? Folder : Fallback), primaryName, Folder, Fallback);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Mock gate negative controls: the primary name at the fallback folder, another name at the primary folder, a
+    /// missing name, an unset name, a fallback folder equal to the primary folder, and a primary name outside the
+    /// chain are each rejected, inside a passing harness.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ScriptedChain_WrongSnapshot_IsRejectedInsidePassingHarness()
+    {
+        const string primary = "MCPSERVER_QUADBRAIN_CLI_TEMP";
+        var good = Scripted(name => name == primary ? Folder : Fallback);
+        var primaryAtFallback = Scripted(_ => Fallback);
+        var otherAtFolder = Scripted(name => name == primary || name == "MCPSERVER_ONESHOT_TEMP" ? Folder : Fallback);
+        var missing = Scripted(name => name == primary ? Folder : Fallback);
+        missing.Remove("MCPSERVER_GROK_ONESHOT_TEMP");
+        var unset = Scripted(name => name == primary ? Folder : name == "MCPSERVER_CODEX_TRIAGE_TEMP" ? null : Fallback);
+        var sameFolders = Scripted(_ => Folder);
+
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertChainAt(primaryAtFallback, primary, Folder, Fallback)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertChainAt(otherAtFolder, primary, Folder, Fallback)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertChainAt(missing, primary, Folder, Fallback)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertChainAt(unset, primary, Folder, Fallback)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertChainAt(sameFolders, primary, Folder, Folder)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertChainAt(good, "MCPSERVER_NOT_A_CHAIN_NAME", Folder, Fallback)));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Mock gate positive control: equal before and after snapshots, an unset name included, pass.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ScriptedRestore_EqualSnapshots_Pass() =>
+        AssertRestored(Scripted(Prior), Scripted(Prior));
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Mock gate negative controls: an after snapshot that left one name at the folder, one that set a previously
+    /// unset name, and one missing a name are each rejected, inside a passing harness.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ScriptedRestore_UnrestoredName_IsRejectedInsidePassingHarness()
+    {
+        var before = Scripted(Prior);
+        var leftSet = Scripted(name => name == "MCPSERVER_QUADBRAIN_CLI_TEMP" ? Folder : Prior(name));
+        var newlySet = Scripted(name => name == "MCPSERVER_GROK_ONESHOT_TEMP" ? Folder : Prior(name));
+        var missing = Scripted(Prior);
+        missing.Remove("MCPSERVER_ONESHOT_TEMP");
+
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertRestored(before, leftSet)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertRestored(before, newlySet)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertRestored(before, missing)));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Binding: for each name as the primary, the helper points that name at a new test folder and every other name at
+    /// a separate new fallback folder, both under the official temporary folder, while the body runs, then restores
+    /// every scripted earlier value (an unset name included) and deletes both folders. The test writes the process
+    /// environment and restores the ambient values, so it is an integration test.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="primaryName">The name read first.</param>
+    /// <returns>A task that completes when the assertions have run.</returns>
+    [Theory]
+    [Trait("Category", "Integration")]
+    [InlineData("MCPSERVER_QUADBRAIN_CLI_TEMP")]
+    [InlineData("MCPSERVER_ONESHOT_TEMP")]
+    [InlineData("MCPSERVER_CODEX_ONESHOT_TEMP")]
+    [InlineData("MCPSERVER_CODEX_TRIAGE_TEMP")]
+    [InlineData("MCPSERVER_GROK_ONESHOT_TEMP")]
+    public async Task RunWithTestFoldersAsync_PointsPrimaryAndFallbackThenRestoresThem(string primaryName)
+    {
+        var ambient = Snapshot();
+        try
+        {
+            SetAll(Prior);
+            var before = Snapshot();
+            string? seenFolder = null;
+            string? seenFallback = null;
+            Dictionary<string, string?>? inside = null;
+            var existed = false;
+
+            await RunWithTestFoldersAsync(primaryName, (folder, fallbackFolder) =>
+            {
+                seenFolder = folder;
+                seenFallback = fallbackFolder;
+                inside = Snapshot();
+                existed = Directory.Exists(folder) && Directory.Exists(fallbackFolder);
+                return Task.CompletedTask;
+            }).ConfigureAwait(true);
+
+            Assert.NotNull(seenFolder);
+            Assert.NotNull(seenFallback);
+            Assert.NotNull(inside);
+            AssertChainAt(inside, primaryName, seenFolder, seenFallback);
+            Assert.True(existed);
+            var temp = Path.GetFullPath(Path.GetTempPath());
+            Assert.StartsWith(temp, Path.GetFullPath(seenFolder), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
+            Assert.StartsWith(temp, Path.GetFullPath(seenFallback), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
+            Assert.False(Directory.Exists(seenFolder));
+            Assert.False(Directory.Exists(seenFallback));
+            AssertRestored(before, Snapshot());
+        }
+        finally
+        {
+            SetAll(name => ambient[name]);
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Binding: when the body throws, the exception reaches the caller and the helper still restores every scripted
+    /// earlier value and deletes both folders. The test writes the process environment and restores the ambient
+    /// values, so it is an integration test.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <returns>A task that completes when the assertions have run.</returns>
+    [Fact]
+    [Trait("Category", "Integration")]
+    public async Task RunWithTestFolderAsync_BodyThrows_StillRestoresAndDeletes()
+    {
+        var ambient = Snapshot();
+        try
+        {
+            SetAll(Prior);
+            var before = Snapshot();
+            string? seenFolder = null;
+            string? seenFallback = null;
+
+            await Assert.ThrowsAsync<InvalidOperationException>(() => RunWithTestFoldersAsync("MCPSERVER_CODEX_ONESHOT_TEMP", (folder, fallbackFolder) =>
+            {
+                seenFolder = folder;
+                seenFallback = fallbackFolder;
+                throw new InvalidOperationException("scripted body failure");
+            })).ConfigureAwait(true);
+
+            Assert.NotNull(seenFolder);
+            Assert.NotNull(seenFallback);
+            Assert.False(Directory.Exists(seenFolder));
+            Assert.False(Directory.Exists(seenFallback));
+            AssertRestored(before, Snapshot());
+        }
+        finally
+        {
+            SetAll(name => ambient[name]);
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Binding: a primary name outside the chain is refused before any variable is written or any folder is made.
+    /// The test reads the process environment to prove nothing changed, so it is an integration test.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <returns>A task that completes when the assertions have run.</returns>
+    [Fact]
+    [Trait("Category", "Integration")]
+    public async Task RunWithTestFolderAsync_UnknownPrimary_IsRefusedWithoutChanges()
+    {
+        var before = Snapshot();
+        var ran = false;
+
+        await Assert.ThrowsAsync<ArgumentException>(() => RunWithTestFolderAsync("MCPSERVER_NOT_A_CHAIN_NAME", _ =>
+        {
+            ran = true;
+            return Task.CompletedTask;
+        })).ConfigureAwait(true);
+
+        Assert.False(ran);
+        AssertRestored(before, Snapshot());
+    }
+
+    private static void SetAll(Func<string, string?> value)
+    {
+        foreach (var name in Names)
+            Environment.SetEnvironmentVariable(name, value(name));
+    }
+
+    private static string? Prior(string name) =>
+        name == "MCPSERVER_GROK_ONESHOT_TEMP" ? null : "/scripted/prior/" + name;
+
+    private static Dictionary<string, string?> Scripted(Func<string, string?> value) =>
+        Names.ToDictionary(name => name, value, StringComparer.Ordinal);
+}

+using System.Reflection;
+using Xunit;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests.TestSupport;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Assertions on xUnit collection membership and collection definitions, read through <see cref="CustomAttributeData"/>
+/// so no test is run. Shared by the collection tests of TR-MCP-NUKE-019 AC-2 (<c>SqlServerLocalDbCollectionTests</c>)
+/// and of TR-MCP-USECASE-018 AC-006 and TR-MCP-USECASE-019 AC-008 (<c>ProcessEnvironmentCollectionTests</c>).
+/// </summary>
+internal static class CollectionAssertions
+{
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Asserts that <paramref name="type"/> carries exactly one xUnit collection attribute and that it names
+    /// <paramref name="collection"/>.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="type">The test class.</param>
+    /// <param name="collection">The required collection name.</param>
+    internal static void AssertInCollection(Type type, string collection)
+    {
+        var names = type.GetCustomAttributesData()
+            .Where(attribute => attribute.AttributeType == typeof(CollectionAttribute))
+            .Select(attribute => attribute.ConstructorArguments.Count == 1 ? attribute.ConstructorArguments[0].Value as string : null)
+            .ToList();
+        var name = Assert.Single(names);
+        Assert.Equal(collection, name);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Asserts that <paramref name="definition"/> carries exactly one xUnit collection definition, that it names
+    /// <paramref name="collection"/>, and that it sets <c>DisableParallelization</c> to true, so the collection never
+    /// runs beside another collection.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="definition">The collection definition class.</param>
+    /// <param name="collection">The required collection name.</param>
+    internal static void AssertSerializedDefinition(Type definition, string collection)
+    {
+        var attribute = Assert.Single(
+            definition.GetCustomAttributesData(),
+            data => data.AttributeType == typeof(CollectionDefinitionAttribute));
+        var name = Assert.Single(attribute.ConstructorArguments);
+        Assert.Equal(collection, name.Value as string);
+        var flag = Assert.Single(
+            attribute.NamedArguments,
+            argument => argument.MemberName == nameof(CollectionDefinitionAttribute.DisableParallelization));
+        Assert.True(flag.TypedValue.Value is true);
+    }
+}

+/// TR-MCP-USECASE-018 AC-006 and TR-MCP-USECASE-019 AC-008: the test classes that write the process environment run

+[CollectionDefinition(Name, DisableParallelization = true)]

+/// <summary>
+/// Tests that the test classes which write the process environment run one at a time: each belongs to an xUnit
+/// collection with parallelization disabled, so no two of them, and no other test, run while one of them has a
+/// variable changed.
+/// </summary>
+/// <remarks>
+/// Requirement coverage: TR-MCP-USECASE-018 AC-006 and TR-MCP-USECASE-019 AC-008 ("the tests that write the process
+/// environment run one at a time"; operator 2026-10-21: "Fix both, strengthen ACs" and "Approve, strengthen AC-006/008
+/// (Recommended)"; hostile validation round 5 finding R5-F2). Test data: scripted nested types carrying a collection
+/// attribute with the required name, another name, or none; the attributes are read through the shared
+/// <see cref="CollectionAssertions"/>, so no test is run. Negative controls prove the membership assertion rejects a
+/// type outside any collection and a type in another collection; the definition assertion's own controls are in
+/// <c>SqlServerLocalDbCollectionTests</c>. The binding checks the real definition and the classes that write the
+/// process environment, found by the one-time environment sweep of plan section 19.3 and its extension through
+/// production code in plan section 20 (the one-shot strategy tests, the two Codex platform classes and the
+/// <see cref="CliTempVariables"/> tests, which write the CLI temp variables; hostile validation finding R6-F1). Stated

diff --git a/tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/TestSupport/WindowsJunction.cs b/tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/TestSupport/WindowsJunction.cs

+using System.Buffers.Binary;
+using System.ComponentModel;
+using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
+using System.Runtime.Versioning;
+using System.Text;
+using Microsoft.Win32.SafeHandles;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests.TestSupport;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Creates a Windows directory junction with operating-system calls made in the test process: the alias folder is
+/// created, opened as a reparse point (CreateFileW) and given a mount-point reparse buffer
+/// (DeviceIoControl FSCTL_SET_REPARSE_POINT), so a fixture never depends on how long a helper process takes to start
+/// (TR-MCP-USECASE-019 AC-008 as replaced 2026-10-26T14:18:23; operator: "Fix flake, strengthen AC (Recommended)";
+/// triage-report-8c2c30ef63d442bdb29fd785ec91e7d7).
+/// </summary>
+internal static class WindowsJunction
+{
+    /// <summary>IO_REPARSE_TAG_MOUNT_POINT, the reparse tag of a directory junction.</summary>
+    internal const uint MountPointTag = 0xA0000003;
+
+    private const uint SetReparsePointControl = 0x000900A4;
+    private const uint GenericWrite = 0x40000000;
+    private const uint OpenExisting = 3;
+    private const uint FlagOpenReparsePoint = 0x00200000;
+    private const uint FlagBackupSemantics = 0x02000000;
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Builds the mount-point REPARSE_DATA_BUFFER for <paramref name="targetFullPath"/>: the tag, the data length,
+    /// a reserved field, the substitute and print name offsets and lengths, then the UTF-16 substitute name "\??\"
+    /// plus the target, a null, the print name (the target) and a null.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="targetFullPath">The absolute target folder.</param>
+    /// <returns>The buffer bytes.</returns>
+    internal static byte[] BuildMountPointBuffer(string targetFullPath)
+    {
+        ArgumentException.ThrowIfNullOrEmpty(targetFullPath);
+        var substitute = Encoding.Unicode.GetBytes(@"\??\" + targetFullPath);
+        var print = Encoding.Unicode.GetBytes(targetFullPath);
+        var pathLength = substitute.Length + 2 + print.Length + 2;
+        var buffer = new byte[16 + pathLength];
+        BinaryPrimitives.WriteUInt32LittleEndian(buffer.AsSpan(0, 4), MountPointTag);
+        BinaryPrimitives.WriteUInt16LittleEndian(buffer.AsSpan(4, 2), checked((ushort)(8 + pathLength)));
+        BinaryPrimitives.WriteUInt16LittleEndian(buffer.AsSpan(10, 2), checked((ushort)substitute.Length));
+        BinaryPrimitives.WriteUInt16LittleEndian(buffer.AsSpan(12, 2), checked((ushort)(substitute.Length + 2)));
+        BinaryPrimitives.WriteUInt16LittleEndian(buffer.AsSpan(14, 2), checked((ushort)print.Length));
+        substitute.CopyTo(buffer, 16);
+        print.CopyTo(buffer, 16 + substitute.Length + 2);
+        return buffer;
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Creates the junction <paramref name="aliasPath"/> that points at <paramref name="targetPath"/>; the alias folder
+    /// must not exist. On failure the alias folder is removed and a <see cref="Win32Exception"/> is thrown.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="aliasPath">The junction to create.</param>
+    /// <param name="targetPath">The existing folder it points at.</param>
+    [SupportedOSPlatform("windows")]
+    internal static void Create(string aliasPath, string targetPath)
+    {
+        ArgumentException.ThrowIfNullOrEmpty(aliasPath);
+        ArgumentException.ThrowIfNullOrEmpty(targetPath);
+        var buffer = BuildMountPointBuffer(Path.GetFullPath(targetPath));
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(aliasPath);
+        try
+        {
+            using var handle = CreateFile(aliasPath, GenericWrite, 0, IntPtr.Zero, OpenExisting, FlagOpenReparsePoint | FlagBackupSemantics, IntPtr.Zero);
+            if (handle.IsInvalid)
+                throw new Win32Exception(Marshal.GetLastWin32Error(), $"Opening {aliasPath} as a reparse point failed.");
+            if (!DeviceIoControl(handle, SetReparsePointControl, buffer, (uint)buffer.Length, IntPtr.Zero, 0, out _, IntPtr.Zero))
+                throw new Win32Exception(Marshal.GetLastWin32Error(), $"Setting the junction reparse point on {aliasPath} failed.");
+        }
+        catch
+        {
+            Directory.Delete(aliasPath);
+            throw;
+        }
+    }
+
+    [DllImport(
+        "kernel32.dll",
+        EntryPoint = "CreateFileW",
+        CharSet = CharSet.Unicode,
+        SetLastError = true)]
+    private static extern SafeFileHandle CreateFile(
+        string fileName,
+        uint desiredAccess,
+        uint shareMode,
+        IntPtr securityAttributes,
+        uint creationDisposition,
+        uint flagsAndAttributes,
+        IntPtr templateFile);
+
+    [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
+    [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
+    private static extern bool DeviceIoControl(
+        SafeFileHandle device,
+        uint controlCode,
+        byte[] inBuffer,
+        uint inBufferSize,
+        IntPtr outBuffer,
+        uint outBufferSize,
+        out uint bytesReturned,
+        IntPtr overlapped);
+}

+using System.Buffers.Binary;
+using System.Text;
+using Xunit;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests.TestSupport;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Tests the mount-point reparse buffer a test fixture writes to make a Windows directory junction with an
+/// operating-system call in the test process, instead of starting a helper process on a time budget.
+/// </summary>
+/// <remarks>
+/// Requirement coverage: TR-MCP-USECASE-019 AC-008 as replaced 2026-10-26T14:18:23 ("A test fixture that needs a
+/// directory junction or link creates it with an operating-system call made in the test process"; operator: "Fix
+/// flake, strengthen AC (Recommended)"; triage-report-8c2c30ef63d442bdb29fd785ec91e7d7). Test data: the documented
+/// REPARSE_DATA_BUFFER layout for IO_REPARSE_TAG_MOUNT_POINT (tag 0xA0000003; ReparseDataLength; Reserved;
+/// SubstituteNameOffset, SubstituteNameLength, PrintNameOffset, PrintNameLength; then the UTF-16 substitute name
+/// "\??\" plus the target, a null, the print name, a null), pinned as literal bytes for one target and decoded field
+/// by field for another, run against a builder passed in, so the contract is proved on a scripted reference builder
+/// with negative controls before the fixture's builder exists. The checks are pure byte comparisons on every host and
+/// touch no file system or environment, so they are unit tests.
+/// </remarks>
+public sealed class WindowsJunctionTests
+{
+    private const uint MountPointTag = 0xA0000003;
+
+    /// <summary>The documented buffer for the target C:\a, byte by byte.</summary>
+    private static readonly byte[] PinnedBufferForCDriveA =
+    [
+        0x03, 0x00, 0x00, 0xA0, // ReparseTag 0xA0000003
+        0x24, 0x00, // ReparseDataLength 36
+        0x00, 0x00, // Reserved
+        0x00, 0x00, // SubstituteNameOffset 0
+        0x10, 0x00, // SubstituteNameLength 16
+        0x12, 0x00, // PrintNameOffset 18
+        0x08, 0x00, // PrintNameLength 8
+        0x5C, 0x00, 0x3F, 0x00, 0x3F, 0x00, 0x5C, 0x00, 0x43, 0x00, 0x3A, 0x00, 0x5C, 0x00, 0x61, 0x00, // \??\C:\a
+        0x00, 0x00, // null
+        0x43, 0x00, 0x3A, 0x00, 0x5C, 0x00, 0x61, 0x00, // C:\a
+        0x00, 0x00, // null
+    ];
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Asserts that <paramref name="build"/> gives the pinned bytes for C:\a and, for a longer target with a space,
+    /// a buffer whose fields decode to the documented layout.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="build">The buffer builder under test.</param>
+    internal static void AssertBufferContract(Func<string, byte[]> build)
+    {
+        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(build);
+        Assert.Equal(PinnedBufferForCDriveA, build(@"C:\a"));
+
+        const string target = @"D:\Temp\mcp twelfth external";
+        var buffer = build(target);
+        var substitute = @"\??\" + target;
+        Assert.Equal(MountPointTag, BinaryPrimitives.ReadUInt32LittleEndian(buffer.AsSpan(0, 4)));
+        Assert.Equal(buffer.Length - 8, BinaryPrimitives.ReadUInt16LittleEndian(buffer.AsSpan(4, 2)));
+        Assert.Equal(0, BinaryPrimitives.ReadUInt16LittleEndian(buffer.AsSpan(6, 2)));
+        Assert.Equal(0, BinaryPrimitives.ReadUInt16LittleEndian(buffer.AsSpan(8, 2)));
+        Assert.Equal(substitute.Length * 2, BinaryPrimitives.ReadUInt16LittleEndian(buffer.AsSpan(10, 2)));
+        Assert.Equal((substitute.Length * 2) + 2, BinaryPrimitives.ReadUInt16LittleEndian(buffer.AsSpan(12, 2)));
+        Assert.Equal(target.Length * 2, BinaryPrimitives.ReadUInt16LittleEndian(buffer.AsSpan(14, 2)));
+        Assert.Equal(substitute, Encoding.Unicode.GetString(buffer, 16, substitute.Length * 2));
+        Assert.Equal(0, BinaryPrimitives.ReadUInt16LittleEndian(buffer.AsSpan(16 + (substitute.Length * 2), 2)));
+        Assert.Equal(target, Encoding.Unicode.GetString(buffer, 16 + (substitute.Length * 2) + 2, target.Length * 2));
+        Assert.Equal(0, BinaryPrimitives.ReadUInt16LittleEndian(buffer.AsSpan(buffer.Length - 2, 2)));
+        Assert.Equal(16 + (substitute.Length * 2) + 2 + (target.Length * 2) + 2, buffer.Length);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Binding: the fixture's builder, <see cref="WindowsJunction.BuildMountPointBuffer"/>, satisfies the same
+    /// contract the scripted reference passed and the wrong builders failed, and its tag is the documented one.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void FixtureBuilder_SatisfiesTheContract()
+    {
+        Assert.Equal(MountPointTag, WindowsJunction.MountPointTag);
+        AssertBufferContract(WindowsJunction.BuildMountPointBuffer);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Mock gate positive control: the scripted reference builder satisfies the contract.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ScriptedReferenceBuilder_SatisfiesTheContract() =>
+        AssertBufferContract(target => Scripted(target, MountPointTag, @"\??\", nullGap: true, headerInLength: false));
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Mock gate negative controls, each rejected inside a passing harness: the symbolic-link tag instead of the
+    /// mount-point tag, a substitute name without the \??\ prefix, a print name that starts without the null after the
+    /// substitute name, and a data length that also counts the 8-byte header.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void WrongBuilders_AreRejectedInsidePassingHarness()
+    {
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertBufferContract(target => Scripted(target, 0xA000000C, @"\??\", nullGap: true, headerInLength: false))));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertBufferContract(target => Scripted(target, MountPointTag, string.Empty, nullGap: true, headerInLength: false))));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertBufferContract(target => Scripted(target, MountPointTag, @"\??\", nullGap: false, headerInLength: false))));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertBufferContract(target => Scripted(target, MountPointTag, @"\??\", nullGap: true, headerInLength: true))));
+    }
+
+    private static byte[] Scripted(string target, uint tag, string prefix, bool nullGap, bool headerInLength)
+    {
+        var substitute = Encoding.Unicode.GetBytes(prefix + target);
+        var print = Encoding.Unicode.GetBytes(target);
+        var gap = nullGap ? 2 : 0;
+        var pathLength = substitute.Length + gap + print.Length + 2;
+        var buffer = new byte[16 + pathLength];
+        BinaryPrimitives.WriteUInt32LittleEndian(buffer.AsSpan(0, 4), tag);
+        BinaryPrimitives.WriteUInt16LittleEndian(buffer.AsSpan(4, 2), (ushort)(8 + pathLength + (headerInLength ? 8 : 0)));
+        BinaryPrimitives.WriteUInt16LittleEndian(buffer.AsSpan(10, 2), (ushort)substitute.Length);
+        BinaryPrimitives.WriteUInt16LittleEndian(buffer.AsSpan(12, 2), (ushort)(substitute.Length + gap));
+        BinaryPrimitives.WriteUInt16LittleEndian(buffer.AsSpan(14, 2), (ushort)print.Length);
+        substitute.CopyTo(buffer, 16);
+        print.CopyTo(buffer, 16 + substitute.Length + gap);
+        return buffer;
+    }
+}
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Binding on a Windows host: <see cref="WindowsJunction.Create"/> makes a real directory junction in the test process
+/// that the file system resolves to its target. It creates folders and a file under the official temporary folder and
+/// needs the Windows file system, so it is an integration test on Windows hosts (TR-MCP-USECASE-019 AC-008 as replaced
+/// 2026-10-26T14:18:23; TR-MCP-NUKE-019).
+/// </summary>
+[Trait("Category", "Integration")]
+[Trait("Platform", "Windows")]
+public sealed class WindowsJunctionWindowsTests
+{
+    /// <summary>
+    /// A junction created in the test process is a mount-point reparse point whose link target is the target folder,
+    /// and a file read through it is the target's file.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void Create_MakesAJunctionThatResolvesToItsTarget()
+    {
+        if (!OperatingSystem.IsWindows())
+            throw new PlatformNotSupportedException("This integration test runs only in the Windows lane.");
+        var root = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "mcp-junction-" + Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));
+        var target = Path.Combine(root, "target");
+        var alias = Path.Combine(root, "alias");
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(target);
+        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(target, "document.md"), "through the junction");
+        try
+        {
+            WindowsJunction.Create(alias, target);
+
+            var info = new DirectoryInfo(alias);
+            Assert.True(info.Attributes.HasFlag(FileAttributes.ReparsePoint));
+            Assert.Equal(Path.GetFullPath(target), Path.GetFullPath(Assert.IsType<string>(info.LinkTarget)));
+            Assert.Equal("through the junction", File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(alias, "document.md")));
+        }
+        finally
+        {
+            if (Directory.Exists(alias))
+                Directory.Delete(alias);
+            Directory.Delete(root, recursive: true);
+        }
+    }
+}

+using System.Reflection;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Tests.Shared;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-USECASE-018 (AC-006), TR-MCP-USECASE-019 (AC-008): the one repository-root locator for the unit-test
+/// projects, linked into each project that reads the checkout, so every test that reads repository files reaches
+/// it. Reading the checkout is an environment dependence (operator 2026-10-19: "If it depends on the environment,
+/// it's an integration test."), so the locator resolves only inside a test case, or a test-class fixture, tagged
+/// <c>[Trait("Category", "Integration")]</c>; a unit test that reaches it fails with the reason, which makes the
+/// full unit run the proof that no unit test reads the repository. The root comes from <c>MCP_REPOSITORY_ROOT</c>,
+/// then the <c>McpRepositoryRoot</c> build metadata, then the first directory above the test assembly, the test
+/// output or the current directory that holds the repository markers (<c>.git</c>, <c>QBrainAi.sln</c>,
+/// <c>Directory.Build.props</c>).
+/// </summary>
+internal static class TestRepositoryRoot
+{
+    private const string RuleMessage =
+        "A unit test may not read the repository checkout: it depends on the environment, so it is an integration "
+        + "test. Tag the test method or its class [Trait(\"Category\", \"Integration\")], or read a fixture copied "
+        + "to the test output.";
+
+    private static readonly Lazy<string> Located = new(Locate, LazyThreadSafetyMode.ExecutionAndPublication);
+
+    /// <summary>The repository root; resolves only for an integration test or its class fixture.</summary>
+    internal static string Resolve()
+    {
+        EnsureIntegration(TestContext.Current.TestCase?.Traits ?? TestContext.Current.TestClass?.Traits);
+        return Located.Value;
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// The <c>McpRepositoryRoot</c> build metadata of the test assembly (TR-MCP-USECASE-018: the deterministic
+    /// repository-root anchor), or null when the project does not embed it; available only for an integration test
+    /// or its class fixture, since the value names the checkout.
+    /// </summary>
+    internal static string? BuildMetadataRoot
+    {
+        get
+        {
+            EnsureIntegration(TestContext.Current.TestCase?.Traits ?? TestContext.Current.TestClass?.Traits);
+            return ReadBuildMetadata();
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>A path under the repository root; resolves only for an integration test or its class fixture.</summary>
+    /// <param name="segments">Path segments below the root.</param>
+    internal static string Combine(params string[] segments)
+    {
+        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(segments);
+        return Path.Combine(new[] { Resolve() }.Concat(segments).ToArray());
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// The trait policy shared by <see cref="Resolve"/> and its mock tests: passes only when the traits hold
+    /// <c>Category=Integration</c>; otherwise, including when there is no test context, throws naming the rule.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="traits">The running test case's or test class's traits, or null outside a test.</param>
+    internal static void EnsureIntegration(IReadOnlyDictionary<string, IReadOnlyCollection<string>>? traits)
+    {
+        if (traits is not null
+            && traits.TryGetValue("Category", out var categories)
+            && categories.Contains("Integration", StringComparer.Ordinal))
+        {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        throw new InvalidOperationException(RuleMessage);
+    }
+
+    private static string Locate()
+    {
+        var configured = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("MCP_REPOSITORY_ROOT");
+        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(configured))
+            return Validate(configured, "Configured");
+
+        var buildRoot = ReadBuildMetadata();
+        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(buildRoot))
+            return Validate(buildRoot, "Build-metadata");
+
+        var startingPoints = new[]
+        {
+            Path.GetDirectoryName(typeof(TestRepositoryRoot).Assembly.Location),
+            AppContext.BaseDirectory,
+            Directory.GetCurrentDirectory(),
+        };
+        foreach (var startingPoint in startingPoints.Where(candidate => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(candidate)))
+        {
+            for (var current = new DirectoryInfo(Path.GetFullPath(startingPoint!)); current is not null; current = current.Parent)
+            {
+                if (IsRepositoryRoot(current.FullName))
+                    return current.FullName;
+            }
+        }
+
+        throw new InvalidOperationException(
+            "Repository root was not found from MCP_REPOSITORY_ROOT, build metadata, the test assembly, the test output, "
+            + "or the current directory.");
+    }
+
+    private static string? ReadBuildMetadata() =>
+        typeof(TestRepositoryRoot).Assembly
+            .GetCustomAttributes<AssemblyMetadataAttribute>()
+            .FirstOrDefault(attribute => string.Equals(attribute.Key, "McpRepositoryRoot", StringComparison.Ordinal))
+            ?.Value;
+
+    /// <summary>Validates an explicit root without falling back to another checkout.</summary>
+    private static string Validate(string candidate, string source)
+    {
+        var root = Path.GetFullPath(candidate);
+        if (!IsRepositoryRoot(root))
+            throw new InvalidOperationException($"{source} repository root is invalid: {root}");
+
+        return root;
+    }
+
+    private static bool IsRepositoryRoot(string root)
+    {
+        var gitMarker = Path.Combine(root, ".git");
+        return (File.Exists(gitMarker) || Directory.Exists(gitMarker))
+            && File.Exists(Path.Combine(root, "QBrainAi.sln"))
+            && File.Exists(Path.Combine(root, "Directory.Build.props"));
+    }
+}

new file mode 100644
index 00000000..f5e2fcec

+++ b/tests/TestSupport.Shared/ScratchQBrainAi.cs

+using System.Diagnostics;
+using System.Globalization;
+using System.Net;
+using System.Net.Sockets;
+using System.Text;
+using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
+using QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.IntegrationTests;
+using QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Services;
+using QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Storage;
+using QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Storage.Entities;
+using QBrainAi.Tests.Shared;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.TestSupport.TemporaryInstance;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019 AC-6: one temporary QBrain.AI instance an integration case owns: a QBrainAi.Support.Mcp process on
+/// a free high port, started from the built assembly in a test-owned root under the temporary folder, with its own
+/// workspace, SQLite database, appsettings and generated marker, never the live server, its marker or its API key.
+/// <see cref="StartAsync"/> returns once the instance wrote its marker and answers health; disposing it stops the
+/// process tree and removes the root. Shared by the integration projects that start REPL children against it
+/// (formerly private to RequirementScopeLayerReplIntegrationTests, behavior unchanged).
+/// </summary>
+internal sealed class ScratchQBrainAi : IAsyncDisposable
+{
+    private static readonly TimeSpan MarkerTimeout = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(1);
+    private static readonly TimeSpan ServerHealthTimeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(30);
+
+    private readonly List<string> _stdout = new();
+    private readonly List<string> _stderr = new();
+    private readonly object _lock = new();
+    private Process? _process;
+
+    private ScratchQBrainAi(string rootPath, int port, string supportAssemblyPath, string replHostAssemblyPath)
+    {
+        RootPath = rootPath;
+        Port = port;
+        WorkspacePath = Path.Combine(rootPath, "workspace");
+        DataPath = Path.Combine(rootPath, "data");
+        DatabasePath = Path.Combine(DataPath, "mcp.db");
+        MarkerPath = Path.Combine(WorkspacePath, MarkerFileService.MarkerFileName);
+        SupportAssemblyPath = supportAssemblyPath;
+        ReplHostAssemblyPath = replHostAssemblyPath;
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Gets the test-owned root folder that holds everything the instance writes.</summary>
+    public string RootPath { get; }
+
+    /// <summary>Gets the loopback port the instance listens on.</summary>
+    public int Port { get; }
+
+    /// <summary>Gets the instance's workspace folder.</summary>
+    public string WorkspacePath { get; }
+
+    /// <summary>Gets the instance's data folder.</summary>
+    public string DataPath { get; }
+
+    /// <summary>Gets the instance's SQLite database path.</summary>
+    public string DatabasePath { get; }
+
+    /// <summary>Gets the marker the instance generates in its workspace.</summary>
+    public string MarkerPath { get; }
+
+    /// <summary>Gets the built QBrainAi.Support.Mcp assembly the instance runs.</summary>
+    public string SupportAssemblyPath { get; }
+
+    /// <summary>Gets the built QBrainAi.Repl.Host assembly REPL children run against the instance.</summary>
+    public string ReplHostAssemblyPath { get; }
+
+    /// <summary>Gets the base URL the instance's marker names, set once <see cref="StartAsync"/> returns.</summary>
+    public Uri BaseUrl { get; private set; } = new("http://127.0.0.1/", UriKind.Absolute);
+
+    /// <summary>Gets whether the instance process has exited (TR-MCP-NUKE-019: a lost port ends the process).</summary>
+    public bool ProcessExited => _process is { HasExited: true };
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Starts a new temporary instance and returns once it wrote its marker for its own port and answers health. On
+    /// any failure the partial instance is disposed. TR-MCP-NUKE-019: when the instance exited because its port
+    /// was taken after the probe (address already in use), a new instance starts on a newly picked port, at most
+    /// <see cref="TemporaryInstanceChecks.MaxStarts"/> starts in all; any other failure, or the last lost port, is
+    /// rethrown with the process output.
+    /// </summary>
+    public static async Task<ScratchQBrainAi> StartAsync()
+    {
+        var repoRoot = TestRepositoryRoot.Resolve();
+        var outputDirectory = new DirectoryInfo(AppContext.BaseDirectory.TrimEnd(
+            Path.DirectorySeparatorChar,
+            Path.AltDirectorySeparatorChar));
+        var targetFramework = outputDirectory.Name;
+        var configuration = outputDirectory.Parent?.Name ?? "Debug";
+        var supportAssembly = ResolveAssemblyPath(repoRoot, configuration, targetFramework, "QBrainAi.Support.Mcp");
+        var replAssembly = ResolveAssemblyPath(repoRoot, configuration, targetFramework, "QBrainAi.Repl.Host");
+
+        for (var starts = 1; ; starts++)
+        {
+            var root = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"mcp-scratch-instance-{Guid.NewGuid():N}");
+            var server = new ScratchQBrainAi(root, AllocateHighPort(), supportAssembly, replAssembly);
+
+            try
+            {
+                await server.InitializeAsync(repoRoot).ConfigureAwait(false);
+                server.StartProcess();
+                var marker = await server.WaitForMarkerAsync().ConfigureAwait(false);
+                Assert.Equal(server.Port, marker.Port);
+                Assert.False(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(marker.ApiKey), $"Generated marker did not contain an API key.{Environment.NewLine}{server.Diagnostics}");
+                await server.WaitForHealthAsync(marker.BaseUrl).ConfigureAwait(false);
+                server.BaseUrl = new Uri(marker.BaseUrl + "/", UriKind.Absolute);
+                return server;
+            }
+            catch (Exception) when (TemporaryInstanceChecks.ShouldRestartOnNewPort(starts, server.ProcessExited, server.Diagnostics))
+            {
+                await server.DisposeAsync().ConfigureAwait(false);
+            }
+            catch
+            {
+                await server.DisposeAsync().ConfigureAwait(false);
+                throw;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// <inheritdoc />
+    public async ValueTask DisposeAsync()
+    {
+        if (_process is not null)
+        {
+            try
+            {
+                if (!_process.HasExited)
+                {
+                    _process.Kill(entireProcessTree: true);
+                }
+
+                await _process.WaitForExitAsync().ConfigureAwait(false);
+                _process.Dispose();
+            }
+            catch
+            {
+                // Best-effort cleanup for failed integration-test starts.
+            }
+        }
+
+        try
+        {
+            if (Directory.Exists(RootPath))
+            {
+                Directory.Delete(RootPath, recursive: true);
+            }
+        }
+        catch
+        {
+            // Temp cleanup is best effort; diagnostics above preserve the failure cause.
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Gets the instance process output captured so far, for assertion messages.</summary>
+    public string Diagnostics
+    {
+        get
+        {
+            lock (_lock)
+            {
+                var stdout = _stdout.Count == 0 ? "<none>" : string.Join(Environment.NewLine, _stdout);
+                var stderr = _stderr.Count == 0 ? "<none>" : string.Join(Environment.NewLine, _stderr);
+                return $"STDOUT:{Environment.NewLine}{stdout}{Environment.NewLine}STDERR:{Environment.NewLine}{stderr}";
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    private async Task InitializeAsync(string repoRoot)
+    {
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(WorkspacePath);
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(DataPath);
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.Combine(WorkspacePath, "docs", "Project"));
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.Combine(WorkspacePath, "docs", "sessions"));
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.Combine(WorkspacePath, "docs", "external"));
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.Combine(WorkspacePath, "templates"));
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.Combine(RootPath, "logs"));
+
+        await File.WriteAllTextAsync(
+            Path.Combine(WorkspacePath, "docs", "Project", "TODO.yaml"),
+            "sections: []\n",
+            Encoding.UTF8).ConfigureAwait(false);
+        await File.WriteAllTextAsync(
+            Path.Combine(WorkspacePath, "docs", "Project", "Functional-Requirements.md"),
+            "# Functional Requirements\n\n",
+            Encoding.UTF8).ConfigureAwait(false);
+        await File.WriteAllTextAsync(
+            Path.Combine(WorkspacePath, "docs", "Project", "Technical-Requirements.md"),
+            "# Technical Requirements\n\n",
+            Encoding.UTF8).ConfigureAwait(false);
+        await File.WriteAllTextAsync(
+            Path.Combine(WorkspacePath, "docs", "Project", "Testing-Requirements.md"),
+            "# Testing Requirements\n\n",
+            Encoding.UTF8).ConfigureAwait(false);
+        await File.WriteAllTextAsync(
+            Path.Combine(WorkspacePath, "docs", "Project", "TR-per-FR-Mapping.md"),
+            "# TR per FR Mapping\n\n",
+            Encoding.UTF8).ConfigureAwait(false);
+        await File.WriteAllTextAsync(
+            Path.Combine(WorkspacePath, "docs", "Project", "Requirements-Matrix.md"),
+            "# Requirements Matrix\n\n",
+            Encoding.UTF8).ConfigureAwait(false);
+
+        File.Copy(
+            Path.Combine(repoRoot, "templates", "prompt-templates.yaml"),
+            Path.Combine(WorkspacePath, "templates", "prompt-templates.yaml"));
+
+        await File.WriteAllTextAsync(Path.Combine(WorkspacePath, "docs", "unified-model-schema.json"), "{}\n", Encoding.UTF8)
+            .ConfigureAwait(false);
+        await File.WriteAllTextAsync(Path.Combine(RootPath, "appsettings.yaml"), BuildAppSettingsYaml(), Encoding.UTF8)
+            .ConfigureAwait(false);
+
+        await ScratchSqliteSchema.ApplyAndVerifyAsync(DatabasePath).ConfigureAwait(false);
+        await StageWorkspaceDatabaseAsync().ConfigureAwait(false);
+    }
+
+    private async Task StageWorkspaceDatabaseAsync()
+    {
+        var options = new DbContextOptionsBuilder<McpDbContext>()
+            .UseSqlite(
+                $"Data Source={DatabasePath}",
+                sqlite => sqlite.MigrationsAssembly("QBrainAi.Storage.SqliteMigrations"))
+            .Options;
+
+        await using var db = new McpDbContext(options);
+        var now = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;
+        db.Workspaces.Add(new WorkspaceEntity
+        {
+            WorkspaceId = NormalizePath(WorkspacePath),
+            WorkspacePath = NormalizePath(WorkspacePath),
+            Name = "Temporary integration instance",
+            TodoPath = Path.Combine("docs", "Project", "TODO.yaml"),
+            DataDirectory = DataPath,
+            IsPrimary = true,
+            IsEnabled = true,
+            CurrentRequirementLayerKey = "layer-1",
+            DateTimeCreated = now,
+            DateTimeModified = now,
+        });
+        await db.SaveChangesAsync().ConfigureAwait(false);
+    }
+
+    private void StartProcess()
+    {
+        var startInfo = new ProcessStartInfo
+        {
+            FileName = "dotnet",
+            WorkingDirectory = RootPath,
+            UseShellExecute = false,
+            RedirectStandardOutput = true,
+            RedirectStandardError = true,
+            CreateNoWindow = true,
+            StandardOutputEncoding = Encoding.UTF8,
+            StandardErrorEncoding = Encoding.UTF8,
+        };
+        startInfo.ArgumentList.Add(SupportAssemblyPath);
+        startInfo.Environment["ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT"] = "Production";
+        startInfo.Environment["DOTNET_ENVIRONMENT"] = "Production";
+        startInfo.Environment["DOTNET_CLI_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT"] = "1";
+        startInfo.Environment["DOTNET_NOLOGO"] = "1";
+        startInfo.Environment["PORT"] = Port.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
+        startInfo.Environment["MCP_SQLITE_DATA_SOURCE"] = DatabasePath;
+        startInfo.Environment["MCP_WORKSPACE_PATH"] = WorkspacePath;
+        startInfo.Environment["MCPSERVER_WORKSPACE_PATH"] = WorkspacePath;
+
+        _process = new Process { StartInfo = startInfo };
+        _process.OutputDataReceived += (_, e) =>
+        {
+            if (e.Data is not null)
+            {
+                lock (_lock)
+                {
+                    _stdout.Add(e.Data);
+                }
+            }
+        };
+        _process.ErrorDataReceived += (_, e) =>
+        {
+            if (e.Data is not null)
+            {
+                lock (_lock)
+                {
+                    _stderr.Add(e.Data);
+                }
+            }
+        };
+
+        if (!_process.Start())
+        {
+            throw new InvalidOperationException("Failed to start QBrain.AI process.");
+        }
+
+        _process.BeginOutputReadLine();
+        _process.BeginErrorReadLine();
+    }
+
+    private async Task<MarkerSettings> WaitForMarkerAsync()
+    {
+        using var timeout = new CancellationTokenSource(MarkerTimeout);
+        using var watcher = new FileSystemWatcher(WorkspacePath, MarkerFileService.MarkerFileName)
+        {
+            NotifyFilter = NotifyFilters.CreationTime
+                | NotifyFilters.FileName
+                | NotifyFilters.LastWrite
+                | NotifyFilters.Size,
+        };
+        var signal = new TaskCompletionSource(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
+        FileSystemEventHandler onChanged = (_, _) => signal.TrySetResult();
+        RenamedEventHandler onRenamed = (_, _) => signal.TrySetResult();
+        watcher.Created += onChanged;
+        watcher.Changed += onChanged;
+        watcher.Renamed += onRenamed;
+        watcher.EnableRaisingEvents = true;
+
+        while (!timeout.IsCancellationRequested)
+        {
+            if (TryReadMarker(out var marker))
+            {
+                return marker!;
+            }
+
+            if (_process is { HasExited: true })
+            {
+                // Drain the redirected output so the message and the restart check see all of it.
+                await _process.WaitForExitAsync().ConfigureAwait(false);
+                throw new InvalidOperationException(
+                    $"QBrain.AI exited before generating '{MarkerPath}' with code {_process.ExitCode}.{Environment.NewLine}{Diagnostics}");
+            }
+
+            var delay = Task.Delay(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(250), timeout.Token);
+            var completed = await Task.WhenAny(signal.Task, delay).ConfigureAwait(false);
+            if (completed == signal.Task)
+            {
+                signal = new TaskCompletionSource(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
+            }
+        }
+
+        throw new TimeoutException(
+            $"Timed out after {MarkerTimeout} waiting for generated marker '{MarkerPath}'.{Environment.NewLine}{Diagnostics}");
+    }
+
+    private bool TryReadMarker(out MarkerSettings? marker)
+    {
+        marker = null;
+        if (!File.Exists(MarkerPath))
+        {
+            return false;
+        }
+
+        try
+        {
+            var values = new Dictionary<string, string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
+            foreach (var rawLine in File.ReadLines(MarkerPath))
+            {
+                var line = rawLine.Trim();
+                if (line.Length == 0 || line.StartsWith("#", StringComparison.Ordinal))
+                {
+                    continue;
+                }
+
+                var separator = line.IndexOf(':', StringComparison.Ordinal);
+                if (separator <= 0)
+                {
+                    continue;
+                }
+
+                var key = line[..separator].Trim();
+                if (key is not ("port" or "baseUrl" or "apiKey" or "workspacePath"))
+                {
+                    continue;
+                }
+
+                values[key] = Unquote(line[(separator + 1)..].Trim());
+            }
+
+            if (!values.TryGetValue("port", out var rawPort)
+                || !int.TryParse(rawPort, NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var port)
+                || !values.TryGetValue("baseUrl", out var baseUrl)
+                || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(baseUrl)
+                || !values.TryGetValue("apiKey", out var apiKey)
+                || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(apiKey))
+            {
+                return false;
+            }
+
+            marker = new MarkerSettings(port, baseUrl.TrimEnd('/'), apiKey);
+            return true;
+        }
+        catch (IOException)
+        {
+            return false;
+        }
+        catch (UnauthorizedAccessException)
+        {
+            return false;
+        }
+    }
+
+    private async Task WaitForHealthAsync(string baseUrl)
+    {
+        using var http = new HttpClient { BaseAddress = new Uri(baseUrl, UriKind.Absolute) };
+        using var timeout = new CancellationTokenSource(ServerHealthTimeout);
+        while (!timeout.IsCancellationRequested)
+        {
+            if (_process is { HasExited: true })
+            {
+                // Drain the redirected output so the message and the restart check see all of it.
+                await _process.WaitForExitAsync().ConfigureAwait(false);
+                throw new InvalidOperationException(
+                    $"QBrain.AI exited before health was available with code {_process.ExitCode}.{Environment.NewLine}{Diagnostics}");
+            }
+
+            try
+            {
+                using var response = await http.GetAsync("/health", timeout.Token).ConfigureAwait(false);
+                if (response.IsSuccessStatusCode)
+                {
+                    return;
+                }
+            }
+            catch (HttpRequestException)
+            {
+            }
+            catch (TaskCanceledException) when (!timeout.IsCancellationRequested)
+            {
+            }
+
+            await Task.Delay(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(250), timeout.Token).ConfigureAwait(false);
+        }
+
+        throw new TimeoutException(
+            $"Timed out after {ServerHealthTimeout} waiting for QBrain.AI health at '{baseUrl}'.{Environment.NewLine}{Diagnostics}");
+    }
+
+    private string BuildAppSettingsYaml()
+    {
+        return $"""
+            AllowedHosts: '*'
+            DataFolder: {YamlQuote(DataPath)}
+            Serilog:
+              MinimumLevel:
+                Default: Warning
+            Mcp:
+              Port: {Port.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)}
+              RepoRoot: {YamlQuote(WorkspacePath)}
+              DataDirectory: {YamlQuote(DataPath)}
+              DataSource: {YamlQuote(DatabasePath)}
+              DatabaseProvider: sqlite
+              DatabaseMigrationsAssembly: QBrainAi.Storage.SqliteMigrations
+              Database:
+                Provider: sqlite
+                MigrationsAssembly: QBrainAi.Storage.SqliteMigrations
+                Sqlite:
+                  DataSource: {YamlQuote(DatabasePath)}
+              TodoFilePath: {YamlQuote(Path.Combine(WorkspacePath, "docs", "Project", "TODO.yaml"))}
+              SessionsPath: {YamlQuote(Path.Combine(WorkspacePath, "docs", "sessions"))}
+              ExternalDocsPath: {YamlQuote(Path.Combine(WorkspacePath, "docs", "external"))}
+              UnifiedModelSchemaPath: {YamlQuote(Path.Combine(WorkspacePath, "docs", "unified-model-schema.json"))}
+              Requirements:
+                FunctionalRequirementsPath: {YamlQuote(Path.Combine(WorkspacePath, "docs", "Project", "Functional-Requirements.md"))}
+                TechnicalRequirementsPath: {YamlQuote(Path.Combine(WorkspacePath, "docs", "Project", "Technical-Requirements.md"))}
+                TestingRequirementsPath: {YamlQuote(Path.Combine(WorkspacePath, "docs", "Project", "Testing-Requirements.md"))}
+                MappingPath: {YamlQuote(Path.Combine(WorkspacePath, "docs", "Project", "TR-per-FR-Mapping.md"))}
+                MatrixPath: {YamlQuote(Path.Combine(WorkspacePath, "docs", "Project", "Requirements-Matrix.md"))}
+              TemplateStorage:
+                Provider: yaml
+                FilePath: {YamlQuote(Path.Combine(WorkspacePath, "templates", "prompt-templates.yaml"))}
+              TodoStorage:
+                Provider: database
+                MigrateFromLegacySqlite: false
+              GraphRag:
+                Enabled: false
+                RootPath: {YamlQuote(Path.Combine(DataPath, "graphrag"))}
+              Parseable:
+                Enabled: false
+              IdentityServer:
+                Enabled: false
+              Tunnel:
+                Port: {Port.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)}
+              Federation:
+                Role: Standalone
+            AgentPool:
+              Agents: []
+            VoiceConversation:
+              Enabled: false
+            Triage:
+              Enabled: false
+
+            """;
+    }
+
+    private static string YamlQuote(string value) => $"'{value.Replace("'", "''", StringComparison.Ordinal)}'";
+
+    private static string Unquote(string value)
+    {
+        if (value.Length >= 2
+            && ((value[0] == '"' && value[^1] == '"')
+                || (value[0] == '\'' && value[^1] == '\'')))
+        {
+            return value[1..^1];
+        }
+
+        return value;
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// TR-MCP-NUKE-019: picks a port that is free on every address the instance binds. The instance listens on
+    /// http://[::]:port (IPv6 any in dual mode, so IPv4 too), so the probe binds the same way; a probe on 127.0.0.1
+    /// alone missed ports held on [::] or 0.0.0.0.
+    /// </summary>
+    private static int AllocateHighPort()
+    {
+        for (var attempt = 0; attempt < 50; attempt++)
+        {
+            var port = Random.Shared.Next(20_000, 60_000);
+            try
+            {
+                using var listener = CreatePortProbe(port);
+                listener.Start();
+                return port;
+            }
+            catch (SocketException)
+            {
+            }
+        }
+
+        using var fallback = CreatePortProbe(0);
+        fallback.Start();
+        return ((IPEndPoint)fallback.LocalEndpoint).Port;
+    }
+
+    private static TcpListener CreatePortProbe(int port)
+    {
+        if (!Socket.OSSupportsIPv6)
+        {
+            return new TcpListener(IPAddress.Any, port);
+        }
+
+        var listener = new TcpListener(IPAddress.IPv6Any, port);
+        try
+        {
+            listener.Server.DualMode = true;
+            return listener;
+        }
+        catch
+        {
+            listener.Dispose();
+            throw;
+        }
+    }
+
+    private static string ResolveAssemblyPath(string repoRoot, string configuration, string targetFramework, string projectName)
+    {
+        var candidate = Path.Combine(
+            repoRoot,
+            "src",
+            projectName,
+            "bin",
+            configuration,
+            targetFramework,
+            $"{projectName}.dll");
+        if (File.Exists(candidate))
+        {
+            return candidate;
+        }
+
+        var copiedCandidate = Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, $"{projectName}.dll");
+        if (File.Exists(copiedCandidate))
+        {
+            return copiedCandidate;
+        }
+
+        throw new FileNotFoundException(
+            $"Could not find built {projectName} assembly. Build the test project before running REPL integration tests. Checked '{copiedCandidate}' and '{candidate}'.",
+            candidate);
+    }
+
+    private static string NormalizePath(string path)
+        => Path.TrimEndingDirectorySeparator(Path.GetFullPath(path));
+
+    private sealed record MarkerSettings(int Port, string BaseUrl, string ApiKey);
+}

+namespace QBrainAi.TestSupport.TemporaryInstance;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019 AC-6: the checks an integration case uses to prove it runs on a temporary instance it owns, never
+/// the live server, its marker or its API key. Pure functions over strings and numbers (no file system, network or
+/// environment), linked into the unit project that proves their contract (QBrainAi.Repl.Core.Tests) and the
+/// integration projects whose cases call them. Each check returns null when the input passes and a message naming the
+/// violation otherwise, so a caller asserts <c>Assert.Null(check)</c> and the failure message says what was wrong.
+/// </summary>
+internal static class TemporaryInstanceChecks
+{
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Checks the REPL host's start arguments: a <c>--workspace-path</c> and a <c>--marker-file</c> are present and
+    /// both lie strictly inside <paramref name="ownedRoot"/>, compared segment by segment ('/' and '\' both separate
+    /// segments), and neither path holds a '.' or '..' segment.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="arguments">The argument list the REPL host was started with.</param>
+    /// <param name="ownedRoot">The temporary instance's root folder.</param>
+    /// <returns>Null when the arguments pass; otherwise the violation.</returns>
+    internal static string? CheckReplStartArguments(IReadOnlyList<string> arguments, string ownedRoot)
+    {
+        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(arguments);
+        ArgumentException.ThrowIfNullOrWhiteSpace(ownedRoot);
+
+        var workspace = ValueAfter(arguments, "--workspace-path");
+        if (workspace is null)
+            return "The REPL host was started without --workspace-path.";
+
+        var marker = ValueAfter(arguments, "--marker-file");
+        if (marker is null)
+            return "The REPL host was started without --marker-file.";
+
+        if (!IsStrictlyInside(workspace, ownedRoot))
+            return $"The REPL host's workspace '{workspace}' is not inside the temporary instance root '{ownedRoot}'.";
+
+        return IsStrictlyInside(marker, ownedRoot)
+            ? null
+            : $"The REPL host's marker '{marker}' is not inside the temporary instance root '{ownedRoot}'.";
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Checks a health answer from the temporary instance: the status is 200 and the body holds the request's nonce
+    /// (ordinal), the byte-for-byte echo the server's liveness contract gives.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="statusCode">The HTTP status code of the answer.</param>
+    /// <param name="body">The answer body, or null.</param>
+    /// <param name="nonce">The nonce the request sent.</param>
+    /// <returns>Null when the answer passes; otherwise the violation.</returns>
+    internal static string? CheckHealthAnswer(int statusCode, string? body, string nonce)
+    {
+        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(nonce))
+            return "The health request carried no nonce to echo.";
+
+        if (statusCode != 200)
+            return $"The temporary instance answered health with status {statusCode}, not 200.";
+
+        return body is not null && body.Contains(nonce, StringComparison.Ordinal)
+            ? null
+            : $"The temporary instance's health answer did not echo the nonce '{nonce}'.";
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>TR-MCP-NUKE-019: the most starts a case makes of a temporary instance that keeps losing its port.</summary>
+    internal const int MaxStarts = 3;
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// TR-MCP-NUKE-019: decides whether a temporary instance that failed to start is started again on a newly
+    /// picked port. Only an instance whose process exited and whose output says its address was already in use
+    /// (ignoring case) is restarted, and only while fewer than <see cref="MaxStarts"/> starts were made; any other start
+    /// failure, an instance still running, or a third lost port is not, so the case fails with the instance's output.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="starts">The starts made so far, the failed one included.</param>
+    /// <param name="exited">Whether the instance process has exited.</param>
+    /// <param name="output">The instance process output.</param>
+    /// <returns>True when the case starts a new instance on a new port.</returns>
+    internal static bool ShouldRestartOnNewPort(int starts, bool exited, string output) =>
+        starts < MaxStarts
+        && exited
+        && (output ?? string.Empty).Contains("address already in use", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
+
+    private static string? ValueAfter(IReadOnlyList<string> arguments, string name)
+    {
+        for (var index = 0; index < arguments.Count - 1; index++)
+        {
+            if (string.Equals(arguments[index], name, StringComparison.Ordinal))
+                return arguments[index + 1];
+        }
+
+        return null;
+    }
+
+    private static bool IsStrictlyInside(string path, string root)
+    {
+        var pathSegments = Segments(path);
+        if (pathSegments.Any(segment => segment is "." or ".."))
+            return false;
+
+        var rootSegments = Segments(root);
+        if (pathSegments.Length <= rootSegments.Length)
+            return false;
+
+        for (var index = 0; index < rootSegments.Length; index++)
+        {
+            if (!string.Equals(pathSegments[index], rootSegments[index], StringComparison.Ordinal))
+                return false;
+        }
+
+        return true;
+    }
+
+    private static string[] Segments(string path) =>
+        path.Split(['/', '\\'], StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
+}
